# Web App and Demo Tooling Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build `apps/web`, the Next.js app where a Producer registers fields with an email-login wallet, a SCAS stub attests them, an Issuer runs a Pool from a demo console, and KYC-approved investors subscribe and redeem in USDC on devnet. Also build `scripts/demo-e2e.ts`, which runs the whole main flow and doubles as the demo-video script.

**Architecture:** One Next.js App Router service on Railway. Server code owns every privileged key (Issuer, compliance, SCAS stub, deployer as test-USDC faucet) and every Anchor call: pages read Program accounts on the server through a cached `ChainReader`; user transactions (register project, subscribe, refund, redeem) are built and simulated on the server, returned as unsigned v0 transactions, signed in the browser by Phantom (wallet adapter) or a Privy embedded wallet, then confirmed by the server. SCAS is an external provider integrated later, so `lib/scas-stub.ts` writes a canonical evidence JSON to the Railway bucket and signs `record_attestation` with the SCAS key. There is no database: state lives in Program accounts; polygons and evidence live in the bucket under their SHA-256.

**Tech Stack:** Node 22, pnpm 12.3.4, Nx 23.2.1, TypeScript 6.0.3, Next.js 16.3.8 (App Router, `output: "standalone"`), React 19.3.0, Tailwind CSS 4.3.3 with shadcn 4.21.1, `@anchor-lang/core` 1.2.0 (server and scripts only), `@solana/web3.js` 1.99.0, `@solana/spl-token` 0.4.15, `@solana/wallet-adapter-react` 0.15.40 / `-react-ui` 0.9.40 / `-base` 0.9.28, `@privy-io/react-auth` 3.47.0 with `@solana/kit` 8.4.0, maplibre-gl 6.11.2, `@aws-sdk/client-s3` 3.1146.0, `@turf/area` and `@turf/kinks` 7.4.0, zod 4.6.5, bs58 6.0.0, bn.js 5.2.5, `@sentry/nextjs` 11.4.0 (GlitchTip), posthog-js 1.435.8, vitest 5.0.3 with vite 8.3.2, `@vitejs/plugin-react` 6.1.1, jsdom 30.1.1, `@testing-library/react` 16.3.3, `@testing-library/user-event` 14.6.7, `@testing-library/jest-dom` 7.0.1, `@playwright/test` 1.63.0 (stretch).

**Spec:** [docs/specs/2026-10-03-mvp-design.md](../specs/2026-10-03-mvp-design.md), sections 4.5 (web app), 5 (main flow), 6 (errors), 7 (testing), 8 (schedule). Section 4.4 is replaced in Task 19 by the SCAS-stub decision (ADR 0012). Program interface: [docs/systems/program.md](../systems/program.md), [ADR 0011](../architecture/decisions/0011-platform-level-kyc.md) and `packages/sdk/src/idl/f2f.json`. Working references for the new KYC model on `main`: `scripts/devnet-onboard-investor.ts`, `scripts/buy/server.ts`.

**Prerequisites:**
- `main` contains `packages/sdk` (commits `16eac36`, `f0cba35`), the platform-level KYC program and its synced IDL (`bb040c1`), and the style-lock (`835c6f9`). The SCAS worker plan is stopped (`cc6f343`); nothing here depends on `apps/scas-worker`.
- Work on branch `feat/web` created from the latest `main`: `git switch -c feat/web main`.
- The Program is deployed on devnet and Config is initialized. `SCAS_SECRET_KEY` in Infisical is the `Config.scas` key.
- Infisical project `field-to-finance`, env `dev`, holds `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIVY_APP_ID` (Privy is configured). `PRIVY_APP_SECRET` is there too but this plan does not use it.

## Global Constraints

- Deadline 2026-10-12 23:59 PT. Web work Oct 4–8, demo script and deploy Oct 8–9, review Oct 9. Commit small, push daily, never squash or rewrite pushed history. Add a devlog entry for each working day.
- All UI copy in English, no em dashes in UI copy (style-lock). Amounts in USD only (`$1,250.00`), from integer USDC base units (6 decimals); never floats for money (ADR 0004). Yield reads "Fixed yield 8.0%" with its term.
- `.tastemaker/style-lock.md` is law for colours, type, shape, spacing and motion: paper `#f5f2ea`, surface `#fbfaf5`, inlay `#e1e4d3`, forest `#142d23`, gold `#c9a227` only on forest or ink fills, gold text `#79632e`, Instrument Sans for everything, JetBrains Mono for addresses, signatures and hashes.
- Never commit secrets. Secrets live in Infisical project `field-to-finance`, env `dev` (root path). Never print a secret value in logs, errors, analytics, GlitchTip events or evidence. Errors about keys name the variable, never the value.
- Keys for Issuer, compliance, SCAS stub and deployer are read only in server code (`src/lib/server/*`, route handlers, server actions). `src/lib/server/*` starts with `import "server-only"`. No `NEXT_PUBLIC_` variable holds a key.
- KYC is platform-level (ADR 0011): `approve_investor` takes only `{ compliance, config, investor, investorRecord, systemProgram }`; `subscribe` creates and thaws the investor's Token-2022 Instrument account itself; `revoke_investor` takes the Instrument account as optional.
- `@anchor-lang/core` 1.2.0 is CommonJS: import `AnchorProvider`, `Program`, `Wallet` by name and `BN` from `bn.js`. Anchor runs only on the server and in scripts; the browser bundle uses `@solana/web3.js` only.
- Build every instruction with `.accountsStrict({...})` so no account is resolved over the network and builders are testable offline.
- Program limits: `evidence_uri` and pool metadata `uri` at most 128 bytes; crop at most 16 bytes; pool name at most 32, symbol at most 10; premium 500–600 bps; yield at most 5 000 bps; at most 8 Fields per project; `ndvi_mean_x10k` in −10 000..10 000.
- The SCAS stub always records verdict `Pass`, `ndvi_mean_x10k = 0`, `scene_date = now`. Every place that shows an Attestation shows the badge "Verified by SCAS stub: external provider pending".
- `register_field` stores `polygonHash` from `@f2f/sdk` (normalize → canonical JSON → sha256). The web never hashes a polygon any other way.
- Modules under `apps/web/src/lib/` (except `src/lib/server/`) use relative imports only, no `@/` alias, so `scripts/demo-e2e.ts` can import them through `tsx`.
- Demo Pools use a maturity a few minutes out (console default: close in 10 minutes, maturity in 20; script: 4 and 6).
- Errors go to GlitchTip org `personal`, project `f2f-web` (`NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN`, `SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT=devnet`). Analytics go to the shared PostHog EU cloud with the web project's own key (`NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_KEY`) and person property `app_env` (`dev`/`prod`).
- Hosting: one Railway service `web` in project `field-to-finance` (id `6be3d83c-91d0-466e-9521-40b7c80863fc`, workspace "Yesset's Projects", environment `production`), built from `apps/web/Dockerfile`, with bucket `f2f-evidence`. No `railway.json`/`railway.toml`. No SCAS worker service.
- pnpm 12 enforces a minimum release age. If `pnpm install` refuses a pinned version as too new, let it add the `minimumReleaseAgeExclude` entry to `pnpm-workspace.yaml` and commit it with the task.
- UI house rules (skill `ui-design`): one brand colour, one sans family, at most 6 type sizes on the landing page and a 24px ceiling in app screens, no nested cards, semantic colour only by meaning, four interaction states plus a visible focus ring for every control, hover motion only under `@media (hover: hover)`, motion respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

## Review Focus

- **Double click on "Verify all fields" or two admin tabs attesting the same Field.** Both reads see the same `attestation_count`, so the second `record_attestation` would hit an existing Attestation PDA. Expected: stub attestations run one at a time in the process and land with consecutive sequences. Test: `createSerialQueue` and the concurrent `attestStub` test in Task 6.
- **Investor without KYC presses Subscribe** (the demo beat). Simulation fails with `AccountNotInitialized` on `investor_record` (or `InvestorNotApproved` after a revoke). Expected: "This wallet hasn't passed KYC" with a "Complete KYC" way out, not raw logs; logs stay behind a disclosure. Tests: `explainChainError` in Task 5 and the InvestPanel test in Task 13.
- **Phantom or Privy on mainnet while the app builds devnet transactions.** The wallet's simulation fails with "Blockhash not found". Expected: "Your wallet is on a different network. Switch it to Devnet and try again." Test: `explainChainError` blockhash case in Task 5; Privy is pinned to `solana:devnet` in Task 9.
- **Uploaded GeoJSON variants:** a FeatureCollection with two fields, a MultiPolygon, altitude, an unclosed ring, a self-crossing boundary, a file that is not JSON. Expected: each Feature becomes one Field; MultiPolygon, self-crossing and non-JSON are rejected with a message naming the problem; the stored hash equals `polygonHashHex` from `@f2f/sdk`. Tests: `splitFeatures` and `uploadPolygon` in Task 4, `GeojsonUpload` in Task 14.
- **Secrets and the 128-byte URI limit at startup.** A malformed keypair variable must fail with the variable name but not its value; a `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` longer than 50 characters would make every evidence URI exceed 128 bytes. Expected: both fail at env parsing with a clear message. Tests: `parseKeypair` and `parseServerEnv` in Task 1.

## Visual direction (brief for tastemaker)

Used only if `.tastemaker/style-lock.md` is missing (it exists on `main` since `835c6f9`; the style-lock wins). Agri-fintech for farmers in northern Kazakhstan and crypto-native investors: calm, trustworthy, earthy, "a trustworthy field ledger, not a crypto casino". One brand colour (deep forest green), warm paper neutrals, semantic colours separate from the brand (success leaf green with icon and label, warning amber, danger brick, info slate blue), one sans family with tabular numerals, real satellite imagery instead of stock photos, light theme only.

### Task 1: `apps/web` scaffold, Nx project, env schemas, keypair parsing

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/package.json`, `apps/web/project.json`, `apps/web/tsconfig.json`, `apps/web/next.config.ts`, `apps/web/postcss.config.mjs`, `apps/web/vitest.config.ts`, `apps/web/.env.example`, `apps/web/next-env.d.ts` (generated by `next build`, committed as generated)
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx` (temporary minimal), `apps/web/src/app/page.tsx` (temporary), `apps/web/src/app/globals.css` (temporary), `apps/web/src/app/api/health/route.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/env.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/public-env.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/keys.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/errors.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/server/env.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/test/setup.ts`, `apps/web/test/server-only-stub.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/test/env.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/keys.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `@f2f/sdk` (workspace package; nothing used yet).
- Produces:
  - `MAX_PUBLIC_BASE_URL_LEN = 50`; `serverEnvSchema`; `type ServerEnv`; `parseServerEnv(source: Record<string, string | undefined>): ServerEnv` (throws `Error("Invalid server environment: <path>: <message>; ...")`).
  - `publicEnvSchema`; `type PublicEnv`; `parsePublicEnv(source): PublicEnv`; `publicEnv: PublicEnv` (in `public-env.ts`).
  - `class MissingSecretError extends Error`; `parseKeypair(name: string, raw: string | undefined): Keypair`.
  - `class RequestError extends Error { status: number }`.
  - `serverEnv(): ServerEnv` (server-only, memoized).
  - `GET /api/health` → `{ ok: true, version: string, cluster: string }`.

`apps/web/next.config.ts`:
```ts
import path from "node:path";
import type { NextConfig } from "next";

// Nx runs next from apps/web, so the monorepo root is two levels up.
const root = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "../..");

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  output: "standalone",
  outputFileTracingRoot: root,
  turbopack: { root },
  // @f2f/sdk ships TypeScript source.
  transpilePackages: ["@f2f/sdk"],
  // Anchor is CommonJS and server-only; load it with Node's require at runtime.
  serverExternalPackages: ["@anchor-lang/core"],
  poweredByHeader: false,
};

export default nextConfig;
```

`apps/web/postcss.config.mjs`:
```js
export default { plugins: { "@tailwindcss/postcss": {} } };
```

`apps/web/vitest.config.ts`:
```ts
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";
import { defineConfig } from "vitest/config";

const here = (p: string) => fileURLToPath(new URL(p, import.meta.url));

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      "@": here("./src"),
      // The real package throws outside React Server Components.
      "server-only": here("./test/server-only-stub.ts"),
    },
  },
  test: {
    include: ["test/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}"],
    environment: "node",
    setupFiles: ["test/setup.ts"],
  },
});
```

`apps/web/test/env.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MAX_PUBLIC_BASE_URL_LEN, parsePublicEnv, parseServerEnv } from "../src/lib/env";

describe("parseServerEnv", () => {
  it("fills devnet defaults", () => {
    const env = parseServerEnv({});
    expect(env.SOLANA_RPC_URL).toBe("https://api.devnet.solana.com");
    expect(env.F2F_PROGRAM_ID).toBe("9syreF6kWfmMfoCkTSE8f7p3kRY1Bh7TSDjGAr9s6Jfm");
    expect(env.USDC_MINT).toBe("Fhfr7QZ6PpZF4ug1kVdnA7dfMGaSJawGL6dhwNMFvTjv");
    expect(env.PUBLIC_BASE_URL).toBe("http://localhost:3000");
    expect(env.BUCKET_FORCE_PATH_STYLE).toBe(false);
    expect(env.ADMIN_PASSWORD).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("treats blank strings as unset", () => {
    const env = parseServerEnv({ ADMIN_PASSWORD: "  ", SOLANA_RPC_URL: "" });
    expect(env.ADMIN_PASSWORD).toBeUndefined();
    expect(env.SOLANA_RPC_URL).toBe("https://api.devnet.solana.com");
  });

  it("strips a trailing slash from PUBLIC_BASE_URL", () => {
    expect(parseServerEnv({ PUBLIC_BASE_URL: "https://f2f.example/" }).PUBLIC_BASE_URL).toBe("https://f2f.example");
  });

  it("rejects a PUBLIC_BASE_URL that would push evidence URIs past 128 bytes", () => {
    const long = `https://${"a".repeat(MAX_PUBLIC_BASE_URL_LEN)}.app`;
    expect(() => parseServerEnv({ PUBLIC_BASE_URL: long })).toThrow(/PUBLIC_BASE_URL/);
  });

  it("rejects a short ADMIN_PASSWORD without echoing it", () => {
    let message = "";
    try {
      parseServerEnv({ ADMIN_PASSWORD: "hunter2" });
    } catch (e) {
      message = (e as Error).message;
    }
    expect(message).toMatch(/ADMIN_PASSWORD/);
    expect(message).not.toContain("hunter2");
  });

  it("rejects a malformed program id", () => {
    expect(() => parseServerEnv({ F2F_PROGRAM_ID: "not-a-key" })).toThrow(/F2F_PROGRAM_ID/);
  });

  it("parses BUCKET_FORCE_PATH_STYLE", () => {
    expect(parseServerEnv({ BUCKET_FORCE_PATH_STYLE: "true" }).BUCKET_FORCE_PATH_STYLE).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("parsePublicEnv", () => {
  it("defaults to devnet, EU PostHog and app_env dev", () => {
    const env = parsePublicEnv({});
    expect(env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL).toBe("https://api.devnet.solana.com");
    expect(env.NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_HOST).toBe("https://eu.i.posthog.com");
    expect(env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV).toBe("dev");
    expect(env.NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIVY_APP_ID).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("rejects an unknown app env", () => {
    expect(() => parsePublicEnv({ NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV: "staging" })).toThrow(/NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV/);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { Keypair } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MissingSecretError, parseKeypair } from "../src/lib/keys";

describe("parseKeypair", () => {
  it("parses a solana-keygen JSON byte array", () => {
    const kp = Keypair.generate();
    const parsed = parseKeypair("F2F_ISSUER_KEYPAIR", JSON.stringify(Array.from(kp.secretKey)));

  it("never echoes a malformed value", () => {
    const secretish = "[1,2,3,SECRET-MATERIAL";
    for (const raw of [secretish, "[1,2,3]", '{"a":1}', JSON.stringify(new Array(64).fill(300))]) {

      expect(message).not.toContain("SECRET-MATERIAL");
      expect(message).not.toContain("300");
    }
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/env.ts`:
```ts
import { z } from "zod";

const DEVNET_RPC = "https://api.devnet.solana.com";
const PROGRAM_ID = "9syreF6kWfmMfoCkTSE8f7p3kRY1Bh7TSDjGAr9s6Jfm";
const TEST_USDC_MINT = "Fhfr7QZ6PpZF4ug1kVdnA7dfMGaSJawGL6dhwNMFvTjv";

/** 128-byte on-chain URI limit minus "/api/evidence/" (14) and a 64-char hash. */
export const MAX_PUBLIC_BASE_URL_LEN = 50;

const blank = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === "string" && v.trim() === "" ? undefined : v);
const optional = z.preprocess(blank, z.string().optional());
const pubkey = (fallback: string) =>
  z.preprocess(blank, z.string().regex(/^[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,44}$/, "must be a base58 public key").default(fallback));
const url = (fallback: string) => z.preprocess(blank, z.url().default(fallback));

export const serverEnvSchema = z.object({
  SOLANA_RPC_URL: url(DEVNET_RPC),
  SOLANA_CLUSTER: z.preprocess(blank, z.enum(["devnet", "localnet"]).default("devnet")),
  F2F_PROGRAM_ID: pubkey(PROGRAM_ID),
  USDC_MINT: pubkey(TEST_USDC_MINT),
  F2F_DEPLOYER_KEYPAIR: optional,
  F2F_ISSUER_KEYPAIR: optional,
  F2F_COMPLIANCE_KEYPAIR: optional,
  SCAS_SECRET_KEY: optional,
  BUCKET_NAME: optional,
  BUCKET_ENDPOINT: z.preprocess(blank, z.url().optional()),
  BUCKET_REGION: z.preprocess(blank, z.string().default("auto")),
  BUCKET_ACCESS_KEY_ID: optional,
  BUCKET_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY: optional,
  BUCKET_FORCE_PATH_STYLE: z
    .preprocess(blank, z.enum(["true", "false"]).default("false"))
    .transform((v) => v === "true"),
  PUBLIC_BASE_URL: url("http://localhost:3000")
    .transform((v) => v.replace(/\/+$/, ""))
    .refine((v) => v.length <= MAX_PUBLIC_BASE_URL_LEN, {
      message: `must be at most ${MAX_PUBLIC_BASE_URL_LEN} characters so evidence URIs fit the program's 128-byte limit`,
    }),
  ADMIN_PASSWORD: z.preprocess(blank, z.string().min(8, "must be at least 8 characters").optional()),
  SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT: z.preprocess(blank, z.string().default("development")),
  FAUCET_USDC: z.preprocess(blank, z.coerce.number().int().positive().max(100_000).default(1_000)),
});

export type ServerEnv = z.infer<typeof serverEnvSchema>;

function issues(error: z.ZodError): string {
  // Zod messages never contain the input value; keep it that way (no custom messages with values).
  return error.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join(".")}: ${i.message}`).join("; ");
}

export function parseServerEnv(source: Record<string, string | undefined>): ServerEnv {
  const r = serverEnvSchema.safeParse(source);
  if (!r.success) throw new Error(`Invalid server environment: ${issues(r.error)}`);
  return r.data;
}

export const publicEnvSchema = z.object({
  NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL: url(DEVNET_RPC),
  NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_CLUSTER: z.preprocess(blank, z.enum(["devnet", "localnet"]).default("devnet")),
  NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIVY_APP_ID: optional,
  NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_KEY: optional,
  NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_HOST: url("https://eu.i.posthog.com"),
  NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV: z.preprocess(blank, z.enum(["dev", "prod"]).default("dev")),
  NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN: optional,
});

export type PublicEnv = z.infer<typeof publicEnvSchema>;

export function parsePublicEnv(source: Record<string, string | undefined>): PublicEnv {
  const r = publicEnvSchema.safeParse(source);
  if (!r.success) throw new Error(`Invalid public environment: ${issues(r.error)}`);
  return r.data;
}
```

```ts
import { Keypair } from "@solana/web3.js";

export class MissingSecretError extends Error {
  override name = "MissingSecretError";
}

/** Parses a solana-keygen JSON byte array. Errors name the variable and never include its value. */
export function parseKeypair(name: string, raw: string | undefined): Keypair {
  if (!raw || raw.trim() === "") throw new MissingSecretError(`${name} is not set`);
  let bytes: unknown;
  try {
    bytes = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    throw new Error(`${name} is not valid JSON; expected the 64-byte array from solana-keygen`);
  }
  if (
    !Array.isArray(bytes) ||
    bytes.length !== 64 ||
    !bytes.every((b) => Number.isInteger(b) && b >= 0 && b <= 255)
  ) {
    throw new Error(`${name} must be a JSON array of 64 bytes`);
  }
  return Keypair.fromSecretKey(Uint8Array.from(bytes as number[]));
}
```

```ts
/** An error whose message is safe to show to the caller, with the HTTP status to use. */
export class RequestError extends Error {
  override name = "RequestError";
  constructor(
    message: string,
    readonly status: number,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}
```

`apps/web/test/fixtures/accounts.ts`:
```ts
import { AnchorProvider, Program, Wallet } from "@anchor-lang/core";
import { F2F_IDL, type F2f } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { Connection, Keypair, PublicKey } from "@solana/web3.js";
import BN from "bn.js";

/** Never contacted: every builder uses accountsStrict and every read is stubbed. */
export const offlineConnection = new Connection("http://127.0.0.1:1", "confirmed");

export function offlineProgram(signer: Keypair = Keypair.generate()): Program<F2f> {
  return new Program<F2f>(F2F_IDL, new AnchorProvider(offlineConnection, new Wallet(signer), { commitment: "confirmed" }));
}

export type AccountName = "pool" | "farmProject" | "field" | "attestation" | "investorRecord" | "config";

/** Encodes with the real IDL and decodes back, so fixtures have exactly Anchor's runtime shape. */
export async function roundTrip<T>(program: Program<F2f>, name: AccountName, value: object): Promise<{ data: Buffer; account: T }> {
  const data = await program.coder.accounts.encode(name, value);
  return { data, account: program.coder.accounts.decode(name, data) as T };
}

export const key = () => Keypair.generate().publicKey;

export function poolValue(o: Partial<Record<string, unknown>> = {}) {
  return {
    poolId: new BN(7),
    issuer: key(),
    mint: key(),
    vault: key(),
    crop: "wheat",
    season: 2027,
    targetAmount: new BN(2_000_000_000),
    raisedAmount: new BN(500_000_000),
    yieldBps: 800,
    premiumBps: 550,
    subscriptionCloseTs: new BN(1_791_000_000),
    maturityTs: new BN(1_791_000_600),
    projectCount: 1,
    disbursedCount: 0,
    settledCount: 0,
    status: { open: {} },
    bump: 254,
    mintBump: 253,
    ...o,
  };
}

export function projectValue(o: Partial<Record<string, unknown>> = {}) {
  return {
    producer: key(),
    projectId: new BN(1_759_500_000_000),
    pool: null as PublicKey | null,
    crop: "wheat",
    season: 2027,
    expectedKg: new BN(400_000),
    tokenizedKg: new BN(120_000),
    requestedAmount: new BN(2_000_000_000),
    fieldCount: 2,
    verifiedFieldCount: 1,
    harvestAttested: false,
    disbursed: false,
    settlement: null as unknown,
    bump: 255,
    ...o,
  };
}

export function fieldValue(o: Partial<Record<string, unknown>> = {}) {
  return {
    project: key(),
    index: 0,
    polygonHash: Array.from({ length: 32 }, (_, i) => i),
    areaHaX100: 7611,
    verified: true,
    attestationCount: 1,
    bump: 252,
    ...o,
  };
}

export function attestationValue(o: Partial<Record<string, unknown>> = {}) {
  return {
    field: key(),
    project: key(),
    sequence: 0,
    kind: { fieldVerified: {} },
    verdict: { pass: {} },
    evidenceHash: Array.from({ length: 32 }, () => 0xab),
    evidenceUri: "https://f2f.example/api/evidence/" + "ab".repeat(32),
    ndviMeanX10k: 0,
    sceneDate: new BN(1_791_000_100),
    signer: key(),
    recordedAt: new BN(1_791_000_101),
    bump: 251,
    ...o,
  };
}
```

`apps/web/test/format.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  explorerAddress,
  explorerTx,
  formatDateUtc,
  formatHa,
  formatTonnes,
  formatUsd,
  formatYield,
  parseUsd,
  relativeTime,
  shortKey,
  withYield,
} from "../src/lib/format";

describe("money", () => {
  it("formats base units as USD", () => {
    expect(formatUsd(1_250_000_000n)).toBe("$1,250.00");
    expect(formatUsd("2000000000")).toBe("$2,000.00");
    expect(formatUsd(0n)).toBe("$0.00");
    expect(formatUsd(999_995n)).toBe("$1.00");
    expect(formatUsd(1_234_567n)).toBe("$1.23");
    expect(formatUsd(2_500_600_000n, { cents: false })).toBe("$2,501");
  });

  it("parses dollar input exactly, without floats", () => {
    expect(parseUsd("1,000.50")).toBe(1_000_500_000n);
    expect(parseUsd("$250")).toBe(250_000_000n);
    expect(parseUsd(" 0.000001 ")).toBe(1n);
    expect(parseUsd("0.1")).toBe(100_000n);
  });

  it("rejects ambiguous or invalid dollar input", () => {
    for (const bad of ["", "-5", "1.0000001", "1e3", "abc", "1.2.3", "$"]) {
      expect(() => parseUsd(bad), bad).toThrow(RangeError);
    }
  });

  it("computes principal plus yield like the program (floor)", () => {
    expect(withYield(1_000_000_000n, 800)).toBe(1_080_000_000n);
    expect(withYield(1n, 800)).toBe(1n);
    expect(withYield(3n, 5_000)).toBe(4n);
  });

  it("formats yield with one decimal", () => {
    expect(formatYield(800)).toBe("8.0%");
    expect(formatYield(550)).toBe("5.5%");
  });
});

describe("units and time", () => {
  it("formats tonnes and hectares", () => {
    expect(formatTonnes("1500000")).toBe("1,500 t");
    expect(formatTonnes(120_500n)).toBe("120.5 t");
    expect(formatHa(7611)).toBe("76.11 ha");
  });

  it("formats dates in UTC and relative time", () => {
    expect(formatDateUtc(1_791_000_000)).toBe("Oct 3, 2026, 4:00 AM UTC");
    const now = 1_791_000_000_000;
    expect(relativeTime(1_791_000_240, now)).toBe("in 4 minutes");
    expect(relativeTime(1_791_000_000 - 3 * 3600, now)).toBe("3 hours ago");
    expect(relativeTime(1_791_000_000 + 2 * 86400, now)).toBe("in 2 days");
  });
});

describe("explorer links", () => {
  it("builds devnet links and short keys", () => {
    expect(explorerTx("5sig")).toBe("https://explorer.solana.com/tx/5sig?cluster=devnet");
    expect(explorerAddress("Abc", "devnet")).toBe("https://explorer.solana.com/address/Abc?cluster=devnet");
    expect(explorerTx("5sig", "localnet")).toBe(
      "https://explorer.solana.com/tx/5sig?cluster=custom&customUrl=http%3A%2F%2F127.0.0.1%3A8899",
    );
    expect(shortKey("9syreF6kWfmMfoCkTSE8f7p3kRY1Bh7TSDjGAr9s6Jfm")).toBe("9syr…6Jfm");
    expect(shortKey("short")).toBe("short");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { attestationValue, fieldValue, offlineProgram, poolValue, projectValue, roundTrip } from "./fixtures/accounts";

const program = offlineProgram();
const addr = Keypair.generate().publicKey;

describe("account views", () => {
  it("maps a Pool to strings and numbers", async () => {
    const { account } = await roundTrip<PoolAccount>(program, "pool", poolValue());
    const v = toPoolView(addr, account, null);
    expect(v).toMatchObject({
      address: addr.toBase58(),
      poolId: "7",
      crop: "wheat",
      season: 2027,
      targetAmount: "2000000000",
      raisedAmount: "500000000",
      yieldBps: 800,
      premiumBps: 550,
      subscriptionCloseTs: 1_791_000_000,
      maturityTs: 1_791_000_600,
      status: "Open",
      instrument: null,
    });
  });

  it("maps a FarmProject with and without a pool and settlement", async () => {
    const pool = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
    const a = await roundTrip<ProjectAccount>(program, "farmProject", projectValue());
    expect(toProjectView(addr, a.account)).toMatchObject({ pool: null, settlement: null, expectedKg: "400000", tokenizedKg: "120000" });
    const b = await roundTrip<ProjectAccount>(
      program,
      "farmProject",
      projectValue({ pool, settlement: { cashRepaidWithPremium: {} }, disbursed: true }),
    );
    expect(toProjectView(addr, b.account)).toMatchObject({ pool: pool.toBase58(), settlement: "CashRepaidWithPremium", disbursed: true });
  });

  it("maps a Field polygon hash to hex", async () => {
    const { account } = await roundTrip<FieldAccount>(program, "field", fieldValue());
    const v = toFieldView(addr, account);
    expect(v.polygonHash).toBe("000102030405060708090a0b0c0d0e0f101112131415161718191a1b1c1d1e1f");
    expect(v).toMatchObject({ index: 0, areaHaX100: 7611, verified: true, attestationCount: 1 });
  });

  it("maps an Attestation", async () => {
    const { account } = await roundTrip<AttestationAccount>(
      program,
      "attestation",
      attestationValue({ kind: { harvest: {} }, verdict: { pass: {} }, sequence: 3, recordedAt: new BN(1_791_000_222) }),
    );
    const v = toAttestationView(addr, account);
    expect(v).toMatchObject({ kind: "Harvest", verdict: "Pass", sequence: 3, recordedAt: 1_791_000_222, evidenceHash: "ab".repeat(32) });
  });
});
```

`apps/web/test/filters.test.ts`:
```ts
import bs58 from "bs58";
import { Keypair } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { attestationsOfField, fieldsOfProject, projectsByProducer, projectsInPool, projectsWithoutPool } from "../src/lib/chain/filters";
import { attestationValue, fieldValue, offlineProgram, projectValue, roundTrip } from "./fixtures/accounts";

const program = offlineProgram();

function matches(data: Buffer, filter: { memcmp: { offset: number; bytes: string } }): boolean {
  const want = Buffer.from(bs58.decode(filter.memcmp.bytes));
  return data.subarray(filter.memcmp.offset, filter.memcmp.offset + want.length).equals(want);
}

describe("memcmp filters match real account bytes", () => {
  it("finds projects by producer and by pool membership", async () => {
    const producer = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
    const pool = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
    const inPool = await roundTrip(program, "farmProject", projectValue({ producer, pool }));
    const free = await roundTrip(program, "farmProject", projectValue({ producer }));
    const f = (x: unknown) => x as { memcmp: { offset: number; bytes: string } };
    expect(matches(inPool.data, f(projectsByProducer(producer)))).toBe(true);
    expect(matches(inPool.data, f(projectsInPool(pool)))).toBe(true);
    expect(matches(free.data, f(projectsInPool(pool)))).toBe(false);
    expect(matches(free.data, f(projectsWithoutPool()))).toBe(true);
    expect(matches(inPool.data, f(projectsWithoutPool()))).toBe(false);
  });

  it("finds fields by project and attestations by field", async () => {
    const project = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
    const field = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
    const fd = await roundTrip(program, "field", fieldValue({ project }));
    const at = await roundTrip(program, "attestation", attestationValue({ field }));
    expect(matches(fd.data, fieldsOfProject(project) as never)).toBe(true);
    expect(matches(at.data, attestationsOfField(field) as never)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/test/token-metadata.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { parseTokenMetadata } from "../src/lib/chain/token-metadata";

function encode(name: string, symbol: string, uri: string): Uint8Array {
  const parts: number[] = [...new Array(64).fill(1)]; // update authority + mint
  for (const s of [name, symbol, uri]) {
    const b = new TextEncoder().encode(s);
    const len = new Uint8Array(4);
    new DataView(len.buffer).setUint32(0, b.length, true);
    parts.push(...len, ...b);
  }
  parts.push(0, 0, 0, 0); // empty additional_metadata vec
  return Uint8Array.from(parts);
}

describe("parseTokenMetadata", () => {
  it("reads name, symbol and uri", () => {
    expect(parseTokenMetadata(encode("Wheat 2027", "WHEAT-2027", "https://f2f.example/api/instrument/7"))).toEqual({
      name: "Wheat 2027",
      symbol: "WHEAT-2027",
      uri: "https://f2f.example/api/instrument/7",
    });
  });

  it("fails on truncated data", () => {
    expect(() => parseTokenMetadata(encode("Wheat", "W", "u").slice(0, 70))).toThrow(/truncated/);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/test/ttl-cache.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createTtlCache } from "../src/lib/ttl-cache";

describe("createTtlCache", () => {
  it("reuses a value within the TTL and dedupes in-flight loads", async () => {
    let t = 0;
    const cache = createTtlCache(5_000, () => t);
    const load = vi.fn(async () => 42);
    await Promise.all([cache.get("a", load), cache.get("a", load)]);
    expect(load).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    t = 4_999;
    await cache.get("a", load);
    expect(load).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    t = 5_000;
    await cache.get("a", load);
    expect(load).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it("does not cache failures and can be cleared", async () => {
    const cache = createTtlCache(5_000, () => 0);
    await expect(cache.get("x", async () => Promise.reject(new Error("rpc down")))).rejects.toThrow("rpc down");
    expect(await cache.get("x", async () => 1)).toBe(1);
    cache.clear();
    expect(await cache.get("x", async () => 2)).toBe(2);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { poolPda } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { ACCOUNT_SIZE, AccountLayout, AccountState, TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID, TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID } from "@solana/spl-token";
import { Keypair, PublicKey, type AccountInfo } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createChainReader } from "../src/lib/chain/reader";
import { fieldValue, offlineConnection, offlineProgram, poolValue, projectValue, roundTrip } from "./fixtures/accounts";

function tokenAccount(mint: PublicKey, owner: PublicKey, amount: bigint, frozen: boolean, programId: PublicKey): AccountInfo<Buffer> {
  const data = Buffer.alloc(ACCOUNT_SIZE);
  AccountLayout.encode(
    {
      mint,
      owner,
      amount,
      delegateOption: 0,
      delegate: PublicKey.default,
      state: frozen ? AccountState.Frozen : AccountState.Initialized,
      isNativeOption: 0,
      isNative: 0n,
      delegatedAmount: 0n,
      closeAuthorityOption: 0,
      closeAuthority: PublicKey.default,
    },
    data,
  );
  return { data, owner: programId, lamports: 2_039_280, executable: false, rentEpoch: 0 };
}

async function setup() {
  const program = offlineProgram();
  const usdcMint = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
  const poolAddr = poolPda(program.programId, 7n);
  const pool = await roundTrip(program, "pool", poolValue());
  const project = await roundTrip(program, "farmProject", projectValue({ pool: poolAddr }));
  const projectAddr = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
  const field = await roundTrip(program, "field", fieldValue({ project: projectAddr }));
  vi.spyOn(program.account.pool, "all").mockResolvedValue([{ publicKey: poolAddr, account: pool.account }] as never);
  vi.spyOn(program.account.pool, "fetchNullable").mockResolvedValue(pool.account as never);
  vi.spyOn(program.account.farmProject, "all").mockResolvedValue([{ publicKey: projectAddr, account: project.account }] as never);
  vi.spyOn(program.account.field, "all").mockResolvedValue([{ publicKey: Keypair.generate().publicKey, account: field.account }] as never);
  vi.spyOn(program.account.investorRecord, "fetchNullable").mockResolvedValue(null);
  const connection = offlineConnection;
  vi.spyOn(connection, "getMultipleAccountsInfo").mockResolvedValue([null]);
  vi.spyOn(connection, "getBalance").mockResolvedValue(5_000_000);
  const reader = createChainReader({ program, connection, usdcMint });
  return { program, connection, reader, poolAddr, pool, usdcMint };
}

describe("ChainReader", () => {
  it("returns a pool with its projects and fields", async () => {
    const { reader, poolAddr } = await setup();
    const detail = await reader.getPool("7");
    expect(detail?.pool.address).toBe(poolAddr.toBase58());
    expect(detail?.projects).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(detail?.projects[0]?.fields[0]?.areaHaX100).toBe(7611);
  });

  it("returns null for a malformed pool id instead of throwing", async () => {
    const { reader } = await setup();
    expect(await reader.getPool("abc")).toBeNull();
    expect(await reader.getField("not-a-key")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("caches reads until invalidated", async () => {
    const { reader, program } = await setup();
    await reader.listPools();
    await reader.listPools();
    expect(program.account.pool.all).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    reader.invalidate();
    await reader.listPools();
    expect(program.account.pool.all).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it("reports investor positions with the frozen flag and USDC balance", async () => {
    const { reader, connection, pool, usdcMint } = await setup();
    const wallet = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
    const mint = pool.account.mint;
    vi.mocked(connection.getMultipleAccountsInfo).mockImplementation(async (keys) =>
      keys.length === 1
        ? [null] // instrument mints in listPools
        : [tokenAccount(mint, wallet, 0n, true, TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID), tokenAccount(usdcMint, wallet, 1_000_000_000n, false, TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID)],
    );
    const state = await reader.investor(wallet.toBase58());
    expect(state.approved).toBe(false);
    expect(state.usdcBalance).toBe("1000000000");
    expect(state.solLamports).toBe(5_000_000);
    expect(state.positions[0]).toMatchObject({ poolId: "7", exists: true, frozen: true, balance: "0" });
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/format.ts`:
```ts
export const USDC_DECIMALS = 6;
const SCALE = 1_000_000n;

const group = (digits: string) => digits.replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ",");

/** Integer USDC base units to "$1,234.56". Rounds half up. Never uses floats. */
export function formatUsd(baseUnits: bigint | string, opts: { cents?: boolean } = {}): string {
  const v = BigInt(baseUnits);
  const neg = v < 0n;
  const abs = neg ? -v : v;
  let dollars = abs / SCALE;
  const micros = abs % SCALE;
  const sign = neg ? "-" : "";
  if (opts.cents === false) {
    if (micros >= SCALE / 2n) dollars += 1n;
    return `${sign}$${group(dollars.toString())}`;
  }
  let cents = (micros + 5_000n) / 10_000n;
  if (cents === 100n) {
    dollars += 1n;
    cents = 0n;
  }
  return `${sign}$${group(dollars.toString())}.${cents.toString().padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

/** "1,000.50" or "$250" to base units. At most 6 decimals; no negatives or exponents. */
export function parseUsd(input: string): bigint {
  const s = input.trim().replace(/^\$/, "").replace(/,/g, "");
  if (!/^\d+(\.\d{0,6})?$/.test(s)) {
    throw new RangeError("Enter an amount in dollars, for example 250 or 250.50");
  }
  const [whole, frac = ""] = s.split(".");
  return BigInt(whole as string) * SCALE + BigInt(frac.padEnd(USDC_DECIMALS, "0"));
}

/** Mirrors math::with_yield in the program: floor(amount × (10 000 + bps) / 10 000). */
export function withYield(amount: bigint, yieldBps: number): bigint {
  return (amount * (10_000n + BigInt(yieldBps))) / 10_000n;
}

export const formatYield = (bps: number) => `${(bps / 100).toFixed(1)}%`;

export function formatTonnes(kg: bigint | string): string {
  const t = Number(BigInt(kg)) / 1000;
  return `${t.toLocaleString("en-US", { maximumFractionDigits: 1 })} t`;
}

export const formatHa = (areaHaX100: number) => `${(areaHaX100 / 100).toFixed(2)} ha`;

const dateFmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { dateStyle: "medium", timeStyle: "short", timeZone: "UTC" });
export const formatDateUtc = (unixSeconds: number) => `${dateFmt.format(new Date(unixSeconds * 1000))} UTC`;

const rel = new Intl.RelativeTimeFormat("en", { numeric: "auto" });
export function relativeTime(unixSeconds: number, nowMs: number = Date.now()): string {
  const diff = unixSeconds - Math.floor(nowMs / 1000);
  const abs = Math.abs(diff);
  if (abs < 60) return rel.format(diff, "second");
  if (abs < 3600) return rel.format(Math.round(diff / 60), "minute");
  if (abs < 86_400) return rel.format(Math.round(diff / 3600), "hour");
  return rel.format(Math.round(diff / 86_400), "day");
}

function clusterQuery(cluster: string): string {
  if (cluster === "localnet") return `cluster=custom&customUrl=${encodeURIComponent("http://127.0.0.1:8899")}`;
  return `cluster=${cluster}`;
}

export const explorerTx = (sig: string, cluster = "devnet") => `https://explorer.solana.com/tx/${sig}?${clusterQuery(cluster)}`;
export const explorerAddress = (addr: string, cluster = "devnet") =>
  `https://explorer.solana.com/address/${addr}?${clusterQuery(cluster)}`;

export const shortKey = (key: string) => (key.length <= 12 ? key : `${key.slice(0, 4)}…${key.slice(-4)}`);
```

`apps/web/src/lib/ttl-cache.ts`:
```ts
export interface TtlCache {
  get<T>(key: string, load: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
  clear(): void;
}

/** In-process read cache. Devnet's public RPC rate-limits getProgramAccounts, so pages share results for a few seconds. */
export function createTtlCache(ttlMs: number, now: () => number = Date.now): TtlCache {
  const entries = new Map<string, { at: number; value: Promise<unknown> }>();
  return {
    get<T>(key: string, load: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
      const hit = entries.get(key);
      if (hit && now() - hit.at < ttlMs) return hit.value as Promise<T>;
      const value = load();
      entries.set(key, { at: now(), value });
      value.catch(() => {
        if (entries.get(key)?.value === value) entries.delete(key);
      });
      return value;
    },
    clear() {
      entries.clear();
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/chain/enums.ts`:
```ts
export const POOL_STATUSES = ["Draft", "Open", "Funded", "Refunding", "Redeeming"] as const;
export type PoolStatusName = (typeof POOL_STATUSES)[number];

export const SETTLEMENT_RULES = ["Delivered", "CoveredFromOtherFields", "BoughtOnMarket", "CashRepaidWithPremium"] as const;
export type SettlementRuleName = (typeof SETTLEMENT_RULES)[number];

/** ADR 0006 Shortfall rules in plain words. */
export const SETTLEMENT_LABELS: Record<SettlementRuleName, string> = {
  Delivered: "Delivered in full",
  CoveredFromOtherFields: "Volume shortfall covered from the Producer's other fields",
  BoughtOnMarket: "Quality shortfall covered by buying grain of the contracted class",
  CashRepaidWithPremium: "Repaid in USD with the fixed premium",
};

const lowerFirst = (s: string) => s.charAt(0).toLowerCase() + s.slice(1);

function fromAnchor<T extends string>(value: unknown, names: readonly T[]): T {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") throw new TypeError("not an Anchor enum value");
  const variant = Object.keys(value)[0];
  const match = names.find((n) => lowerFirst(n) === variant);
  if (!match) throw new TypeError(`unknown enum variant: ${String(variant)}`);
  return match;
}

export const poolStatusFromAnchor = (v: unknown): PoolStatusName => fromAnchor(v, POOL_STATUSES);
export const settlementFromAnchor = (v: unknown): SettlementRuleName => fromAnchor(v, SETTLEMENT_RULES);
```

```ts
import type { IdlAccounts } from "@anchor-lang/core";
import { kindFromAnchor, toHex, verdictFromAnchor, type AttestationKindName, type F2f, type VerdictName } from "@f2f/sdk";
import type { PublicKey } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { poolStatusFromAnchor, settlementFromAnchor, type PoolStatusName, type SettlementRuleName } from "./enums";

type Accounts = IdlAccounts<F2f>;
export type ConfigAccount = Accounts["config"];
export type PoolAccount = Accounts["pool"];
export type ProjectAccount = Accounts["farmProject"];
export type FieldAccount = Accounts["field"];
export type AttestationAccount = Accounts["attestation"];
export type InvestorRecordAccount = Accounts["investorRecord"];

export interface ConfigView { admin: string; issuer: string; scas: string; compliance: string; usdcMint: string }
export interface InstrumentView { name: string; symbol: string; uri: string; supply: string; decimals: number }
export interface PoolView {
  address: string;
  poolId: string;
  issuer: string;
  mint: string;
  vault: string;
  crop: string;
  season: number;
  targetAmount: string;
  raisedAmount: string;
  yieldBps: number;
  premiumBps: number;
  subscriptionCloseTs: number;
  maturityTs: number;
  projectCount: number;
  disbursedCount: number;
  settledCount: number;
  status: PoolStatusName;
  instrument: InstrumentView | null;
}
export interface ProjectView {
  address: string;
  producer: string;
  projectId: string;
  pool: string | null;
  crop: string;
  season: number;
  expectedKg: string;
  tokenizedKg: string;
  requestedAmount: string;
  fieldCount: number;
  verifiedFieldCount: number;
  harvestAttested: boolean;
  disbursed: boolean;
  settlement: SettlementRuleName | null;
}
export interface FieldView {
  address: string;
  project: string;
  index: number;
  polygonHash: string;
  areaHaX100: number;
  verified: boolean;
  attestationCount: number;
}
export interface AttestationView {
  address: string;
  field: string;
  project: string;
  sequence: number;
  kind: AttestationKindName;
  verdict: VerdictName;
  evidenceHash: string;
  evidenceUri: string;
  ndviMeanX10k: number;
  sceneDate: number;
  signer: string;
  recordedAt: number;
}
export type ProjectWithFields = ProjectView & { fields: FieldView[] };
export interface PoolDetail { pool: PoolView; projects: ProjectWithFields[] }
export interface FieldDetail { field: FieldView; project: ProjectView; pool: PoolView | null; attestations: AttestationView[] }
export interface PositionView {
  poolId: string;
  pool: string;
  symbol: string;
  status: PoolStatusName;
  yieldBps: number;
  ata: string;
  exists: boolean;
  frozen: boolean;
  balance: string;
}
export interface InvestorState {
  wallet: string;
  approved: boolean;
  approvedAt: number | null;
  usdcBalance: string;
  solLamports: number;
  positions: PositionView[];
}

export const toConfigView = (a: ConfigAccount): ConfigView => ({
  admin: a.admin.toBase58(),
  issuer: a.issuer.toBase58(),
  scas: a.scas.toBase58(),
  compliance: a.compliance.toBase58(),
  usdcMint: a.usdcMint.toBase58(),
});

export function toPoolView(address: PublicKey, a: PoolAccount, instrument: InstrumentView | null): PoolView {
  return {
    address: address.toBase58(),
    poolId: a.poolId.toString(),
    issuer: a.issuer.toBase58(),
    mint: a.mint.toBase58(),
    vault: a.vault.toBase58(),
    crop: a.crop,
    season: a.season,
    targetAmount: a.targetAmount.toString(),
    raisedAmount: a.raisedAmount.toString(),
    yieldBps: a.yieldBps,
    premiumBps: a.premiumBps,
    subscriptionCloseTs: a.subscriptionCloseTs.toNumber(),
    maturityTs: a.maturityTs.toNumber(),
    projectCount: a.projectCount,
    disbursedCount: a.disbursedCount,
    settledCount: a.settledCount,
    status: poolStatusFromAnchor(a.status),
    instrument,
  };
}

export function toProjectView(address: PublicKey, a: ProjectAccount): ProjectView {
  return {
    address: address.toBase58(),
    producer: a.producer.toBase58(),
    projectId: a.projectId.toString(),
    pool: a.pool ? a.pool.toBase58() : null,
    crop: a.crop,
    season: a.season,
    expectedKg: a.expectedKg.toString(),
    tokenizedKg: a.tokenizedKg.toString(),
    requestedAmount: a.requestedAmount.toString(),
    fieldCount: a.fieldCount,
    verifiedFieldCount: a.verifiedFieldCount,
    harvestAttested: a.harvestAttested,
    disbursed: a.disbursed,
    settlement: a.settlement ? settlementFromAnchor(a.settlement) : null,
  };
}

export function toFieldView(address: PublicKey, a: FieldAccount): FieldView {
  return {
    address: address.toBase58(),
    project: a.project.toBase58(),
    index: a.index,
    polygonHash: toHex(Uint8Array.from(a.polygonHash)),
    areaHaX100: a.areaHaX100,
    verified: a.verified,
    attestationCount: a.attestationCount,
  };
}

export function toAttestationView(address: PublicKey, a: AttestationAccount): AttestationView {
  return {
    address: address.toBase58(),
    field: a.field.toBase58(),
    project: a.project.toBase58(),
    sequence: a.sequence,
    kind: kindFromAnchor(a.kind),
    verdict: verdictFromAnchor(a.verdict),
    evidenceHash: toHex(Uint8Array.from(a.evidenceHash)),
    evidenceUri: a.evidenceUri,
    ndviMeanX10k: a.ndviMeanX10k,
    sceneDate: a.sceneDate.toNumber(),
    signer: a.signer.toBase58(),
    recordedAt: a.recordedAt.toNumber(),
  };
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/chain/filters.ts`:
```ts
import type { GetProgramAccountsFilter, PublicKey } from "@solana/web3.js";
import bs58 from "bs58";

/** Byte offsets after the 8-byte Anchor discriminator; pinned by test/filters.test.ts against real encodings. */
export const OFFSETS = {
  projectProducer: 8,
  /** FarmProject.pool is Option<Pubkey>: tag byte at 48 (producer 32 + project_id 8), key at 49. */
  projectPoolTag: 48,
  fieldProject: 8,
  attestationField: 8,
} as const;

const at = (offset: number, bytes: Uint8Array): GetProgramAccountsFilter => ({ memcmp: { offset, bytes: bs58.encode(bytes) } });

export const projectsByProducer = (producer: PublicKey) => at(OFFSETS.projectProducer, producer.toBytes());
export const projectsInPool = (pool: PublicKey) => at(OFFSETS.projectPoolTag, Uint8Array.from([1, ...pool.toBytes()]));
export const projectsWithoutPool = () => at(OFFSETS.projectPoolTag, Uint8Array.from([0]));
export const fieldsOfProject = (project: PublicKey) => at(OFFSETS.fieldProject, project.toBytes());
export const attestationsOfField = (field: PublicKey) => at(OFFSETS.attestationField, field.toBytes());
```

`apps/web/src/lib/chain/token-metadata.ts`:
```ts
/**
 * Token-2022 TokenMetadata extension value: update_authority (32), mint (32),
 * then name, symbol, uri as u32-LE-length-prefixed UTF-8, then additional metadata.
 */
export function parseTokenMetadata(data: Uint8Array): { name: string; symbol: string; uri: string } {
  const view = new DataView(data.buffer, data.byteOffset, data.byteLength);
  let offset = 64;
  const next = (): string => {
    if (offset + 4 > data.length) throw new RangeError("token metadata truncated");
    const len = view.getUint32(offset, true);
    offset += 4;
    if (offset + len > data.length) throw new RangeError("token metadata truncated");
    const s = new TextDecoder().decode(data.subarray(offset, offset + len));
    offset += len;
    return s;
  };
  return { name: next(), symbol: next(), uri: next() };
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/chain/program.ts`:
```ts
import { AnchorProvider, Program, Wallet } from "@anchor-lang/core";
import { F2F_IDL, type F2f } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { Keypair, type Connection, type PublicKey } from "@solana/web3.js";

/** A throwaway signer is fine for reads and for building instructions; sends go through chain/send.ts. */
export function createProgram(connection: Connection, programId: PublicKey, signer: Keypair = Keypair.generate()) {
  const provider = new AnchorProvider(connection, new Wallet(signer), { commitment: "confirmed" });
  return new Program<F2f>({ ...F2F_IDL, address: programId.toBase58() } as F2f, provider);
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/chain/reader.ts`:
```ts
import type { Program } from "@anchor-lang/core";
import { investorPda, poolPda, type F2f } from "@f2f/sdk";
import {
  ExtensionType,
  TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID,
  TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
  getAssociatedTokenAddressSync,
  getExtensionData,
  unpackAccount,
  unpackMint,
} from "@solana/spl-token";
import { PublicKey, type Connection } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { RequestError } from "../errors";
import { createTtlCache } from "../ttl-cache";
import { attestationsOfField, fieldsOfProject, projectsByProducer, projectsInPool, projectsWithoutPool } from "./filters";
import { parseTokenMetadata } from "./token-metadata";
import {
  toAttestationView,
  toConfigView,
  toFieldView,
  toPoolView,
  toProjectView,
  type ConfigView,
  type FieldDetail,
  type InstrumentView,
  type InvestorState,
  type PoolDetail,
  type PoolView,
  type ProjectAccount,
  type ProjectWithFields,
} from "./views";

export interface ChainReader {
  config(): Promise<ConfigView>;
  listPools(): Promise<PoolView[]>;
  getPool(poolId: string): Promise<PoolDetail | null>;
  getField(address: string): Promise<FieldDetail | null>;
  projectsByProducer(producer: string): Promise<ProjectWithFields[]>;
  unpooledProjects(): Promise<ProjectWithFields[]>;
  investor(wallet: string): Promise<InvestorState>;
  invalidate(): void;
}

export interface ReaderDeps {
  program: Program<F2f>;
  connection: Connection;
  usdcMint: PublicKey;
  ttlMs?: number;
}

function parseKey(s: string): PublicKey | null {
  try {
    return new PublicKey(s);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function createChainReader({ program, connection, usdcMint, ttlMs = 5_000 }: ReaderDeps): ChainReader {
  const cache = createTtlCache(ttlMs);
  const programId = program.programId;

  async function instruments(mints: PublicKey[]): Promise<(InstrumentView | null)[]> {
    if (mints.length === 0) return [];
    const infos = await connection.getMultipleAccountsInfo(mints, "confirmed");
    return infos.map((info, i) => {
      if (!info) return null;
      const mint = unpackMint(mints[i] as PublicKey, info, TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID);
      const ext = getExtensionData(ExtensionType.TokenMetadata, mint.tlvData);
      const meta = ext ? parseTokenMetadata(ext) : { name: "", symbol: "", uri: "" };
      return { ...meta, supply: mint.supply.toString(), decimals: mint.decimals };
    });
  }

  async function withFields(rows: { publicKey: PublicKey; account: ProjectAccount }[]): Promise<ProjectWithFields[]> {
    return Promise.all(
      rows.map(async ({ publicKey, account }) => {
        const fields = await program.account.field.all([fieldsOfProject(publicKey)]);
        return {
          ...toProjectView(publicKey, account),
          fields: fields.map((f) => toFieldView(f.publicKey, f.account)).sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index),
        };
      }),
    );
  }

  const listPools = () =>
    cache.get("pools", async () => {
      const rows = await program.account.pool.all();
      const meta = await instruments(rows.map((r) => r.account.mint));
      return rows
        .map((r, i) => toPoolView(r.publicKey, r.account, meta[i] ?? null))
        .sort((a, b) => (BigInt(b.poolId) > BigInt(a.poolId) ? 1 : -1));
    });

  return {
    config: () =>
      cache.get("config", async () => {
        const [address] = PublicKey.findProgramAddressSync([new TextEncoder().encode("config")], programId);
        return toConfigView(await program.account.config.fetch(address));
      }),

    listPools,

    getPool: (poolId) =>
      cache.get(`pool:${poolId}`, async () => {
        if (!/^\d{1,20}$/.test(poolId)) return null;
        const address = poolPda(programId, BigInt(poolId));
        const account = await program.account.pool.fetchNullable(address);
        if (!account) return null;
        const [meta] = await instruments([account.mint]);
        const projects = await withFields(await program.account.farmProject.all([projectsInPool(address)]));
        return { pool: toPoolView(address, account, meta ?? null), projects };
      }),

    getField: (address) =>
      cache.get(`field:${address}`, async () => {
        const key = parseKey(address);
        if (!key) return null;
        const field = await program.account.field.fetchNullable(key);
        if (!field) return null;
        const project = await program.account.farmProject.fetch(field.project);
        let pool: PoolView | null = null;
        if (project.pool) {
          const p = await program.account.pool.fetchNullable(project.pool);
          if (p) pool = toPoolView(project.pool, p, (await instruments([p.mint]))[0] ?? null);
        }
        const attestations = (await program.account.attestation.all([attestationsOfField(key)]))
          .map((a) => toAttestationView(a.publicKey, a.account))
          .sort((a, b) => a.sequence - b.sequence);
        return { field: toFieldView(key, field), project: toProjectView(field.project, project), pool, attestations };
      }),

    projectsByProducer: (producer) =>
      cache.get(`producer:${producer}`, async () => {
        const key = parseKey(producer);
        if (!key) throw new RequestError("wallet is not a valid public key", 400);
        const rows = await withFields(await program.account.farmProject.all([projectsByProducer(key)]));
        return rows.sort((a, b) => (BigInt(b.projectId) > BigInt(a.projectId) ? 1 : -1));
      }),

    unpooledProjects: () =>
      cache.get("unpooled", async () => withFields(await program.account.farmProject.all([projectsWithoutPool()]))),

    investor: (wallet) =>
      cache.get(`investor:${wallet}`, async () => {
        const owner = parseKey(wallet);
        if (!owner) throw new RequestError("wallet is not a valid public key", 400);
        const pools = await listPools();
        const record = await program.account.investorRecord.fetchNullable(investorPda(programId, owner));
        const atas = pools.map((p) => getAssociatedTokenAddressSync(new PublicKey(p.mint), owner, false, TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID));
        const usdcAta = getAssociatedTokenAddressSync(usdcMint, owner, false, TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID);
        const [infos, lamports] = await Promise.all([
          connection.getMultipleAccountsInfo([...atas, usdcAta], "confirmed"),
          connection.getBalance(owner, "confirmed"),
        ]);
        const positions = pools.map((p, i) => {
          const info = infos[i];
          const ata = atas[i] as PublicKey;
          const acc = info ? unpackAccount(ata, info, TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID) : null;
          return {
            poolId: p.poolId,
            pool: p.address,
            symbol: p.instrument?.symbol ?? `POOL-${p.poolId}`,
            status: p.status,
            yieldBps: p.yieldBps,
            ata: ata.toBase58(),
            exists: acc !== null,
            frozen: acc?.isFrozen ?? true,
            balance: (acc?.amount ?? 0n).toString(),
          };
        });
        const usdcInfo = infos[atas.length];
        const usdc = usdcInfo ? unpackAccount(usdcAta, usdcInfo, TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID).amount : 0n;
        return {
          wallet: owner.toBase58(),
          approved: record?.approved ?? false,
          approvedAt: record?.approved ? record.approvedAt.toNumber() : null,
          usdcBalance: usdc.toString(),
          solLamports: lamports,
          positions,
        };
      }),

    invalidate: () => cache.clear(),
  };
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/server/context.ts`:
```ts
import "server-only";
import { Connection, PublicKey } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { createProgram } from "../chain/program";
import { createChainReader, type ChainReader } from "../chain/reader";
import { serverEnv } from "./env";

let connection: Connection | undefined;
let reader: ChainReader | undefined;

export function getConnection(): Connection {
  connection ??= new Connection(serverEnv().SOLANA_RPC_URL, "confirmed");
  return connection;
}

export const getProgramId = () => new PublicKey(serverEnv().F2F_PROGRAM_ID);
export const getUsdcMint = () => new PublicKey(serverEnv().USDC_MINT);

export function getReader(): ChainReader {
  reader ??= createChainReader({
    program: createProgram(getConnection(), getProgramId()),
    connection: getConnection(),
    usdcMint: getUsdcMint(),
  });
  return reader;
}

/** Call after any transaction this server sent or confirmed. */
export function invalidateReads(): void {
  reader?.invalidate();
}
```

### Task 3: Design system: consume the style-lock, tokens, shadcn, app shell, shared components

**Skills:** load `ui-design`, then read `.tastemaker/style-lock.md` (committed in `835c6f9`). It is law: colours, Instrument Sans + JetBrains Mono, the 12/14/16/20/24 app scale, 6px controls / 10px panels / 999px pills, flat surfaces with hairlines, no em dashes in UI copy, gold `#c9a227` only on forest or ink fills. Do not run tastemaker. Only if the file is missing (for example on a branch cut before `835c6f9`), run `tastemaker` in build mode with the "Visual direction" brief and commit what it writes.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/components.json`, `apps/web/src/components/ui/*` (generated by shadcn), `apps/web/src/lib/utils.ts` (generated by shadcn)
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/site-header.tsx`, `site-footer.tsx`, `state.tsx`, `status-badge.tsx`, `explorer-link.tsx`, `scas-stub-badge.tsx`, `logo-mark.tsx`

- Create: `apps/web/src/app/not-found.tsx`
- Test: `apps/web/test/components-basic.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `.tastemaker/style-lock.md`; `PoolStatusName` from `src/lib/chain/enums.ts`; `explorerTx`, `explorerAddress`, `shortKey` from `src/lib/format.ts` (Task 2).
- Produces: Tailwind colour utilities every later task uses: `background`, `foreground`, `card` (surface), `card-foreground`, `muted` (inlay), `muted-foreground`, `border`, `input`, `ring`, `primary` (forest), `primary-hover`, `primary-foreground`, `secondary`, `secondary-foreground`, `accent` (inlay hover), `accent-foreground`, `gold` (only on forest/ink), `gold-text`, `ink`, `on-dark`, `destructive`, `success`, `warning` (gold text), `info`; font utilities `font-sans` (Instrument Sans) and `font-mono` (JetBrains Mono); class `tabular`. Components:
  - `EmptyState({ title, body, action? }: { title: string; body: string; action?: React.ReactNode })`
  - `ErrorState({ title, body, retryHref?, onRetry? }: { title: string; body: string; retryHref?: string; onRetry?: () => void })`

This plan uses `Verified by SCAS stub: external provider pending` everywhere in the UI. If the user insists on the em dash, change only `SCAS_STUB_LABEL`.

```css
  --card: #fbfaf5;         /* surface */
  --muted: #e1e4d3;        /* inlay */
  --foreground: #0d2019;   /* text primary */

  --border: #b2b9a8;       /* decorative hairlines only */

  --on-dark: #dae2d0;
  --destructive: #a3352b;
  --success: #2f6b3f;
  --info: #2d5a7b;
}

@layer base {
  * { @apply border-border; }
  html { -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%; }
  body { @apply bg-background text-foreground antialiased; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.6; }
  h1, h2, h3 { letter-spacing: -0.02em; line-height: 1.15; }
  :focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--ring); outline-offset: 2px; }
  .dark-band :focus-visible { outline-color: #e6ca8b; }
  .tabular { font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *, *::before, *::after { animation-duration: 0.01ms !important; transition-duration: 0.01ms !important; }
}
```

```tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ExplorerLink } from "../src/components/explorer-link";
import { SCAS_STUB_LABEL, ScasStubBadge } from "../src/components/scas-stub-badge";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "../src/components/state";
import { PoolStatusBadge } from "../src/components/status-badge";

describe("shared components", () => {
  it("links a transaction to the devnet explorer in a new tab", () => {
    render(<ExplorerLink kind="tx" value="5sig" />);
    const link = screen.getByRole("link");
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute("href", "https://explorer.solana.com/tx/5sig?cluster=devnet");
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute("target", "_blank");
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute("rel", "noreferrer");
  });

  it("shortens addresses in mono", () => {
    render(<ExplorerLink kind="address" value="9syreF6kWfmMfoCkTSE8f7p3kRY1Bh7TSDjGAr9s6Jfm" />);
    const link = screen.getByRole("link");
    expect(link).toHaveTextContent("9syr…6Jfm");
    expect(link.className).toContain("font-mono");
  });

  it("shows the stub badge text without em dashes", () => {
    render(<ScasStubBadge />);
    expect(screen.getByText(SCAS_STUB_LABEL)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(SCAS_STUB_LABEL).toBe("Verified by SCAS stub: external provider pending");
    expect(SCAS_STUB_LABEL).not.toContain("—");
  });

  it("labels every pool status", () => {
    const labels = { Draft: "Draft", Open: "Open for subscription", Funded: "Funded", Refunding: "Refunding", Redeeming: "Redeeming" };
    for (const [status, label] of Object.entries(labels)) {
      const { unmount } = render(<PoolStatusBadge status={status as keyof typeof labels} />);
      expect(screen.getByText(label)).toBeInTheDocument();
      unmount();
    }
  });

  it("renders empty and error states with a way out", () => {
    render(<EmptyState title="No pools yet" body="Pools appear here once the Issuer opens one." />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "No pools yet" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    render(<ErrorState title="Could not load pools" body="Devnet did not answer." retryHref="/pools" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Try again" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/pools");
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/components/explorer-link.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
import { explorerAddress, explorerTx, shortKey } from "../lib/format";

export function ExplorerLink({
  kind,
  value,
  cluster = "devnet",
  label,
}: {
  kind: "tx" | "address";
  value: string;
  cluster?: string;
  label?: string;
}) {
  const href = kind === "tx" ? explorerTx(value, cluster) : explorerAddress(value, cluster);
  return (
    <a
      href={href}
      target="_blank"
      rel="noreferrer"
      title={value}
      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-sm font-mono text-[13px] text-foreground underline decoration-border underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-foreground"
    >
      {label ?? shortKey(value)}
      <ExternalLink aria-hidden strokeWidth={1.5} className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
      <span className="sr-only">(opens Solana Explorer)</span>
    </a>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { CircleCheck, CircleDashed, CircleDot, RotateCcw, Wallet, type LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import type { PoolStatusName } from "../lib/chain/enums";

const STYLE: Record<PoolStatusName, { label: string; className: string; Icon: LucideIcon }> = {
  Draft: { label: "Draft", className: "border-border bg-muted text-muted-foreground", Icon: CircleDashed },
  Open: { label: "Open for subscription", className: "border-primary bg-primary text-gold", Icon: CircleDot },
  Funded: { label: "Funded", className: "border-success text-success", Icon: CircleCheck },
  Refunding: { label: "Refunding", className: "border-warning text-warning", Icon: RotateCcw },
  Redeeming: { label: "Redeeming", className: "border-info text-info", Icon: Wallet },
};

export function PoolStatusBadge({ status }: { status: PoolStatusName }) {
  const s = STYLE[status];
  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${s.className}`}>
      <s.Icon aria-hidden strokeWidth={1.5} className="size-3.5" />
      {s.label}
    </span>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/state.tsx`:
```tsx
import { AlertTriangle, Inbox } from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

const retryCls =
  "inline-flex h-9 items-center rounded-md border border-border bg-card px-3 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-muted";

export function EmptyState({ title, body, action }: { title: string; body: string; action?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border bg-card p-6">
      <Inbox aria-hidden strokeWidth={1.5} className="size-5 text-muted-foreground" />
      <h2 className="text-base font-semibold">{title}</h2>
      <p className="max-w-prose text-sm text-muted-foreground">{body}</p>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ErrorState({ title, body, retryHref, onRetry }: { title: string; body: string; retryHref?: string; onRetry?: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div role="alert" className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border border-destructive bg-card p-6">
      <AlertTriangle aria-hidden strokeWidth={1.5} className="size-5 text-destructive" />
      <h2 className="text-base font-semibold">{title}</h2>
      <p className="max-w-prose text-sm text-muted-foreground">{body}</p>
      {retryHref ? (
        <Link href={retryHref} className={retryCls}>
          Try again
        </Link>
      ) : onRetry ? (
        <button type="button" onClick={onRetry} className={retryCls}>
          Try again
        </button>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { LogoMark } from "./logo-mark";

const NAV = [
  { href: "/pools", label: "Pools" },
  { href: "/portfolio", label: "Portfolio" },
  { href: "/farmer", label: "Farmer" },
  { href: "/admin", label: "Admin" },
];

export function SiteHeader({ walletSlot }: { walletSlot?: ReactNode }) {
  const path = usePathname();
  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-border bg-background">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 max-w-6xl items-center justify-between gap-4 px-4">
        <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md font-semibold tracking-tight">
          <LogoMark />
          <span>Field-to-Finance</span>
        </Link>
        <nav aria-label="Main" className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm">
          {NAV.map((n) => {
            const active = path === n.href || path.startsWith(`${n.href}/`);
            return (
              <Link
                key={n.href}
                href={n.href}
                aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                className={`rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 hover:bg-muted ${
                  active ? "font-medium text-primary underline decoration-2 underline-offset-[18px]" : "text-muted-foreground"
                }`}
              >
                {n.label}
              </Link>
            );
          })}
          <div className="ml-2">{walletSlot}</div>
        </nav>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/test/store.test.ts`:
```ts
import { sha256, toHex } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MemoryStore, evidenceKey, polygonKey, readHashed, serveHashed } from "../src/lib/store";

const enc = (s: string) => new TextEncoder().encode(s);

describe("store", () => {
  it("lays out keys like the stopped worker did", () => {
    expect(polygonKey("ab".repeat(32))).toBe(`polygons/${"ab".repeat(32)}.json`);
    expect(evidenceKey("cd".repeat(32))).toBe(`evidence/${"cd".repeat(32)}.json`);
  });

  it("returns stored bytes only when their sha256 matches", async () => {
    const store = new MemoryStore();
    const body = enc('{"a":1}');
    const hash = toHex(sha256(body));
    await store.put(evidenceKey(hash), body, "application/json");
    expect(await readHashed(store, evidenceKey(hash), hash)).toEqual(body);
    expect(await readHashed(store, evidenceKey("00".repeat(32)), "00".repeat(32))).toBeNull();
    await store.put(evidenceKey("11".repeat(32)), body, "application/json");
    await expect(readHashed(store, evidenceKey("11".repeat(32)), "11".repeat(32))).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 502 });
  });

  it("serves JSON with an immutable cache header, 400 on a bad hash, 404 when missing", async () => {
    const store = new MemoryStore();
    const body = enc('{"b":2}');
    const hash = toHex(sha256(body));
    await store.put(polygonKey(hash), body, "application/json");
    const ok = await serveHashed(store, polygonKey(hash), hash);
    expect(ok.status).toBe(200);
    expect(ok.headers.get("cache-control")).toContain("immutable");
    expect(await ok.text()).toBe('{"b":2}');
    expect((await serveHashed(store, polygonKey("x"), "x")).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await serveHashed(store, polygonKey("22".repeat(32)), "22".repeat(32))).status).toBe(404);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { GeoJsonInputError, MAX_FIELDS_PER_UPLOAD, splitFeatures } from "../src/lib/geojson";
import { FIELD_A, FIELD_B, MULTI } from "./fixtures/polygons";

describe("splitFeatures", () => {
  it("turns a FeatureCollection into one item per field", () => {
    expect(splitFeatures({ type: "FeatureCollection", features: [FIELD_A, FIELD_B] })).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("accepts a bare Feature or Polygon", () => {
    expect(splitFeatures(FIELD_A)).toEqual([FIELD_A]);
    expect(splitFeatures(FIELD_A.geometry)).toEqual([FIELD_A.geometry]);
  });

  it("rejects MultiPolygon with a message that says what to do", () => {
    expect(() => splitFeatures(MULTI)).toThrow(/MultiPolygon.*one Polygon per field/);
    expect(() => splitFeatures({ type: "FeatureCollection", features: [{ type: "Feature", geometry: MULTI }] })).toThrow(GeoJsonInputError);
  });

  it("rejects empty, non-GeoJSON and too many fields", () => {
    expect(() => splitFeatures({ type: "FeatureCollection", features: [] })).toThrow(/no fields/);
    expect(() => splitFeatures("hello")).toThrow(GeoJsonInputError);
    const many = Array.from({ length: MAX_FIELDS_PER_UPLOAD + 1 }, () => FIELD_A);
    expect(() => splitFeatures({ type: "FeatureCollection", features: many })).toThrow(/at most 4/);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { polygonHashHex } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { checkPolygon } from "../src/lib/geometry";
import { loadPolygon, uploadPolygon } from "../src/lib/polygons";
import { MemoryStore, polygonKey } from "../src/lib/store";
import { BOWTIE, FIELD_A, MULTI } from "./fixtures/polygons";

describe("checkPolygon", () => {
  it("computes a geodesic area in hectares", () => {
    const c = checkPolygon(FIELD_A.geometry as never);
    expect(c.valid).toBe(true);
    expect(c.areaHa).toBeGreaterThan(55);
    expect(c.areaHa).toBeLessThan(57);
  });

  it("rejects a self-crossing boundary", () => {
    expect(checkPolygon(BOWTIE as never)).toMatchObject({ valid: false, problems: ["the boundary crosses itself"] });
  });

  it("rejects coordinates outside WGS84 ranges", () => {
    const bad = { type: "Polygon", coordinates: [[[200, 53], [201, 53], [201, 54], [200, 53]]] };
    expect(checkPolygon(bad as never).problems[0]).toMatch(/longitude, latitude/);
  });
});

describe("uploadPolygon", () => {
  it("stores the canonical polygon under the sdk polygon hash", async () => {
    const store = new MemoryStore();
    const up = await uploadPolygon(store, FIELD_A);
    expect(up.polygonHash).toBe(polygonHashHex(FIELD_A));
    expect(up.areaHaX100).toBe(Math.round(up.areaHa * 100));
    expect(store.objects.has(polygonKey(up.polygonHash))).toBe(true);
    expect(await loadPolygon(store, up.polygonHash)).toEqual(up.polygon);
  });

  it("hashes altitude, unclosed rings and extra decimals the same way", async () => {
    const store = new MemoryStore();
    const ring = FIELD_A.geometry.coordinates[0] as number[][];
    const variant = {
      type: "Polygon",
      coordinates: [ring.slice(0, -1).map(([x, y]) => [(x as number) + 1e-9, y, 210])],
    };
    expect((await uploadPolygon(store, variant)).polygonHash).toBe((await uploadPolygon(store, FIELD_A)).polygonHash);
  });

  it("rejects malformed and invalid polygons with 422", async () => {
    const store = new MemoryStore();
    await expect(uploadPolygon(store, MULTI)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 422 });
    await expect(uploadPolygon(store, BOWTIE)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 422, message: "the boundary crosses itself" });
    await expect(uploadPolygon(store, null)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 422 });
    expect(store.objects.size).toBe(0);
  });
});
```
