- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`apps/web/src/lib/store.ts`:
```ts
import { GetObjectCommand, PutObjectCommand, S3Client } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
import { sha256, toHex } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { RequestError } from "./errors";

export interface BlobStore {
  put(key: string, body: Uint8Array, contentType: string): Promise<void>;
  get(key: string): Promise<Uint8Array | null>;
}

export class MemoryStore implements BlobStore {
  readonly objects = new Map<string, Uint8Array>();
  async put(key: string, body: Uint8Array): Promise<void> {
    this.objects.set(key, body.slice());
  }
  async get(key: string): Promise<Uint8Array | null> {
    return this.objects.get(key)?.slice() ?? null;
  }
}

export interface S3StoreOptions {
  bucket: string;
  endpoint: string;
  region: string;
  accessKeyId: string;
  secretAccessKey: string;
  forcePathStyle: boolean;
}

/** Railway buckets are private, S3-compatible and virtual-host style by default. */
export class S3Store implements BlobStore {
  private readonly client: S3Client;
  constructor(private readonly opts: S3StoreOptions) {
    this.client = new S3Client({
      endpoint: opts.endpoint,
      region: opts.region,
      forcePathStyle: opts.forcePathStyle,
      credentials: { accessKeyId: opts.accessKeyId, secretAccessKey: opts.secretAccessKey },
    });
  }
  async put(key: string, body: Uint8Array, contentType: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.client.send(new PutObjectCommand({ Bucket: this.opts.bucket, Key: key, Body: body, ContentType: contentType }));
  }
  async get(key: string): Promise<Uint8Array | null> {
    try {
      const res = await this.client.send(new GetObjectCommand({ Bucket: this.opts.bucket, Key: key }));
      return res.Body ? await res.Body.transformToByteArray() : null;
    } catch (e) {
      const name = (e as { name?: string }).name;
      if (name === "NoSuchKey" || name === "NotFound") return null;
      throw e;
    }
  }
}

export const polygonKey = (hashHex: string) => `polygons/${hashHex}.json`;
export const evidenceKey = (hashHex: string) => `evidence/${hashHex}.json`;
export const HEX64 = /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/;

/** Content-addressed read: the bytes must hash to the name they are stored under. */
export async function readHashed(store: BlobStore, key: string, hashHex: string): Promise<Uint8Array | null> {
  const bytes = await store.get(key);
  if (!bytes) return null;
  if (toHex(sha256(bytes)) !== hashHex) throw new RequestError(`stored object ${key} does not match its hash`, 502);
  return bytes;
}

export async function serveHashed(store: BlobStore, key: string, hashHex: string): Promise<Response> {
  if (!HEX64.test(hashHex)) return Response.json({ error: "hash must be 64 lowercase hex characters" }, { status: 400 });
  try {
    const bytes = await readHashed(store, key, hashHex);
    if (!bytes) return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
    return new Response(bytes.slice(), {
      status: 200,
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "cache-control": "public, max-age=31536000, immutable" },
    });
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof RequestError) return Response.json({ error: e.message }, { status: e.status });
    throw e;
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/geometry.ts`:
```ts
import { area } from "@turf/area";
import { kinks } from "@turf/kinks";
import type { PolygonGeometry } from "@f2f/sdk";
import type { Feature, Polygon } from "geojson";

/** Field.area_ha_x100 is a u32; this cap is a sanity limit for a demo field, not a program limit. */
export const MAX_FIELD_AREA_HA = 100_000;

export interface GeometryCheck {
  valid: boolean;
  areaHa: number;
  problems: string[];
}

export function checkPolygon(p: PolygonGeometry): GeometryCheck {
  const inRange = p.coordinates.every((ring) => ring.every(([lon, lat]) => lon >= -180 && lon <= 180 && lat >= -90 && lat <= 90));
  if (!inRange) return { valid: false, areaHa: 0, problems: ["coordinates must be [longitude, latitude] in WGS84 degrees"] };
  const feature: Feature<Polygon> = { type: "Feature", properties: {}, geometry: p };
  if (kinks(feature).features.length > 0) return { valid: false, areaHa: 0, problems: ["the boundary crosses itself"] };
  const areaHa = area(feature) / 10_000;
  const problems: string[] = [];
  if (areaHa < 0.01) problems.push("the area is below 0.01 ha");
  if (areaHa > MAX_FIELD_AREA_HA) problems.push(`the area is above ${MAX_FIELD_AREA_HA} ha`);
  return { valid: problems.length === 0, areaHa, problems };
}
```
If `@turf/area` 7.4.0 has no named `area` export in your install, use its default export (`import area from "@turf/area"`); same for `kinks`.

`apps/web/src/lib/geojson.ts`:
```ts
/** Client-safe: used in the browser before upload. One Polygon per Field. */
export const MAX_FIELDS_PER_UPLOAD = 4;

export class GeoJsonInputError extends Error {
  override name = "GeoJsonInputError";
}

function geometryType(item: unknown): unknown {
  const o = item as { type?: unknown; geometry?: { type?: unknown } } | null;
  return o?.type === "Feature" ? o.geometry?.type : o?.type;
}

export function splitFeatures(input: unknown): unknown[] {
  if (!input || typeof input !== "object") throw new GeoJsonInputError("The file is not GeoJSON.");
  const o = input as { type?: unknown; features?: unknown };
  const items = o.type === "FeatureCollection" ? (Array.isArray(o.features) ? o.features : []) : [input];
  if (items.length === 0) throw new GeoJsonInputError("The file has no fields.");
  if (items.length > MAX_FIELDS_PER_UPLOAD) {
    throw new GeoJsonInputError(`Upload at most ${MAX_FIELDS_PER_UPLOAD} fields per project.`);
  }
  items.forEach((item, i) => {
    const t = geometryType(item);
    if (t === "MultiPolygon") {
      throw new GeoJsonInputError(`Field ${i + 1} is a MultiPolygon. Split it into one Polygon per field.`);
    }
    if (t !== "Polygon") throw new GeoJsonInputError(`Field ${i + 1} is not a Polygon.`);
  });
  return items;
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/polygons.ts`:
```ts
import { PolygonFormatError, canonicalJson, normalizePolygon, sha256, toHex, type PolygonGeometry } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { RequestError } from "./errors";
import { checkPolygon } from "./geometry";
import { polygonKey, readHashed, type BlobStore } from "./store";

export interface PolygonUpload {
  polygonHash: string;
  areaHa: number;
  areaHaX100: number;
  polygon: PolygonGeometry;
}

/** Validates, stores polygons/<hash>.json and returns the hash that register_field must use. */
export async function uploadPolygon(store: BlobStore, geojson: unknown): Promise<PolygonUpload> {
  let polygon: PolygonGeometry;
  try {
    polygon = normalizePolygon(geojson);
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof PolygonFormatError) throw new RequestError(e.message, 422);
    throw e;
  }
  const check = checkPolygon(polygon);
  if (!check.valid) throw new RequestError(check.problems.join("; "), 422);
  // Same bytes polygonHash() hashes: sha256(canonicalJson(normalizePolygon(input))).
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJson(polygon));
  const polygonHash = toHex(sha256(bytes));
  await store.put(polygonKey(polygonHash), bytes, "application/json");
  return { polygonHash, areaHa: Math.round(check.areaHa * 100) / 100, areaHaX100: Math.round(check.areaHa * 100), polygon };
}

export async function loadPolygon(store: BlobStore, hashHex: string): Promise<PolygonGeometry | null> {
  const bytes = await readHashed(store, polygonKey(hashHex), hashHex);
  return bytes ? (JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) as PolygonGeometry) : null;
}
```

Append to `apps/web/src/lib/server/context.ts`:
```ts
import { S3Store, type BlobStore } from "../store";

let store: BlobStore | undefined;

export function getStore(): BlobStore {
  if (store) return store;
  const e = serverEnv();
  const missing = (["BUCKET_NAME", "BUCKET_ENDPOINT", "BUCKET_ACCESS_KEY_ID", "BUCKET_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY"] as const).filter((k) => !e[k]);
  if (missing.length) throw new Error(`Bucket is not configured: set ${missing.join(", ")}`);
  store = new S3Store({
    bucket: e.BUCKET_NAME as string,
    endpoint: e.BUCKET_ENDPOINT as string,
    region: e.BUCKET_REGION,
    accessKeyId: e.BUCKET_ACCESS_KEY_ID as string,
    secretAccessKey: e.BUCKET_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY as string,
    forcePathStyle: e.BUCKET_FORCE_PATH_STYLE,
  });
  return store;
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/api/polygons/route.ts`:
```ts
import { RequestError } from "@/lib/errors";
import { uploadPolygon } from "@/lib/polygons";
import { getStore } from "@/lib/server/context";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
const MAX_BYTES = 256 * 1024;

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const text = await req.text();
  if (text.length > MAX_BYTES) return Response.json({ error: "GeoJSON is larger than 256 KB" }, { status: 413 });
  let body: unknown;
  try {
    body = JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    return Response.json({ error: "body is not JSON" }, { status: 400 });
  }
  try {
    return Response.json(await uploadPolygon(getStore(), body));
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof RequestError) return Response.json({ error: e.message }, { status: e.status });
    throw e;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { getStore } from "@/lib/server/context";
import { polygonKey, serveHashed } from "@/lib/store";

export async function GET(_req: Request, ctx: { params: Promise<{ hash: string }> }) {
  const { hash } = await ctx.params;
  return serveHashed(getStore(), polygonKey(hash), hash);
}
```

- Consumes: `@f2f/sdk` PDA helpers (`configPda`, `poolPda`, `mintPda`, `projectPda`, `fieldPda`, `attestationPda`, `investorPda`), `toAnchorEnum`, `type F2f`, `type AttestationKindName`, `type VerdictName`; `SettlementRuleName` (Task 2); `offlineProgram` fixture.
- Produces:
  - `ix.ts` (all return `Promise<TransactionInstruction>` unless noted): `hexToBytes(hex: string): Uint8Array` (sync, throws `RangeError`), `instrumentAta(mint, owner): PublicKey`, `usdcAta(usdcMint, owner, allowOffCurve?): PublicKey`, `createInstrumentAtaIx(payer, owner, mint): TransactionInstruction` (sync), `createUsdcAtaIx(payer, owner, usdcMint): TransactionInstruction` (sync), `mintUsdcIx(usdcMint, destination, authority, amount: bigint): TransactionInstruction` (sync), `registerProjectIx`, `registerFieldIx`, `recordAttestationIx`, `createPoolIx`, `addProjectIx`, `openPoolIx`, `closeSubscriptionIx`, `approveInvestorIx`, `revokeInvestorIx`, `subscribeIx`, `refundIx`, `redeemIx`, `disburseIx`, `recordSettlementIx`, `fundRedemptionIx`, `sweepIx`. Argument objects are listed in Step 3.

  - `explain.ts` (pure, browser-safe, no imports): `type ErrorAction = "kyc" | "faucet" | "switch-network" | "retry" | "none"`, `interface ExplainedError { code: string; title: string; detail: string; action: ErrorAction }`, `explainChainError(input: { logs?: readonly string[] | null; error?: unknown }): ExplainedError`, `extractLogs(e: unknown): string[]`, `messageOf(e: unknown): string`.

```ts
import { attestationPda, fieldPda, investorPda, mintPda, poolPda, projectPda } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { ASSOCIATED_TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID, TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID, TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID, getAssociatedTokenAddressSync } from "@solana/spl-token";
import { Keypair, type TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  approveInvestorIx,
  createInstrumentAtaIx,
  createPoolIx,
  hexToBytes,
  recordAttestationIx,
  recordSettlementIx,
  registerFieldIx,
  registerProjectIx,
  revokeInvestorIx,
  subscribeIx,
} from "../src/lib/chain/ix";
import { offlineProgram } from "./fixtures/accounts";

const program = offlineProgram();
const pid = program.programId;
const k = () => Keypair.generate().publicKey;
const norm = (s: string) => s.replace(/_/g, "").toLowerCase();

function decode(ix: TransactionInstruction) {
  const d = program.coder.instruction.decode(ix.data);
  if (!d) throw new Error("not an f2f instruction");
  return { name: norm(d.name), data: d.data as Record<string, unknown> };
}

describe("instruction builders", () => {
  it("registers a project at its PDA", async () => {
    const producer = k();
    const ix = await registerProjectIx(program, { producer, projectId: 42n, crop: "wheat", season: 2027, expectedKg: 400_000n, requestedAmount: 2_000_000_000n });
    expect(ix.programId.equals(pid)).toBe(true);
    expect(ix.keys[1]?.pubkey.equals(projectPda(pid, producer, 42n))).toBe(true);
    const d = decode(ix);
    expect(d.name).toBe("registerproject");
    expect(String(d.data.requestedAmount ?? d.data.requested_amount)).toBe("2000000000");
  });

  it("registers a field with the polygon hash bytes", async () => {
    const producer = k();
    const project = k();
    const hash = "ab".repeat(32);
    const ix = await registerFieldIx(program, { producer, project, index: 1, polygonHash: hash, areaHaX100: 7611 });
    expect(ix.keys[2]?.pubkey.equals(fieldPda(pid, project, 1))).toBe(true);
    expect(Buffer.from(ix.data).includes(Buffer.from(hash, "hex"))).toBe(true);
  });

  it("rejects a malformed polygon hash", () => {
    expect(() => hexToBytes("xyz")).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(() => hexToBytes("ab".repeat(31))).toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it("records an attestation at attestation_count", async () => {
    const field = k();
    const ix = await recordAttestationIx(program, {
      scas: k(),
      project: k(),
      field,
      sequence: 3,
      kind: "Harvest",
      verdict: "Pass",
      evidenceHash: new Uint8Array(32).fill(1),
      evidenceUri: "https://f2f.example/api/evidence/" + "01".repeat(32),
      ndviMeanX10k: 0,
      sceneDate: 1_791_000_000,
    });
    expect(ix.keys.some((m) => m.pubkey.equals(attestationPda(pid, field, 3)))).toBe(true);
    const d = decode(ix);
    expect(Object.keys(d.data.kind as object)[0]).toBe("harvest");
  });

  it("creates a pool with its mint PDA and the pool's USDC vault", async () => {
    const usdcMint = k();
    const ix = await createPoolIx(program, {
      issuer: k(),
      usdcMint,
      poolId: 7n,
      crop: "wheat",
      season: 2027,
      name: "Wheat 2027",
      symbol: "WHEAT-2027",
      uri: "https://f2f.example/api/instrument/7",
      yieldBps: 800,
      premiumBps: 550,
      subscriptionCloseTs: 1_791_000_600,
      maturityTs: 1_791_001_200,
    });
    const pool = poolPda(pid, 7n);
    const keys = ix.keys.map((m) => m.pubkey.toBase58());
    expect(keys).toContain(pool.toBase58());
    expect(keys).toContain(mintPda(pid, pool).toBase58());
    expect(keys).toContain(getAssociatedTokenAddressSync(usdcMint, pool, true, TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID).toBase58());
    expect(keys).toContain(TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID.toBase58());
  });

  it("subscribes with the investor record PDA, the Token-2022 instrument account and the programs to create it", async () => {
    const investor = k();
    const mint = k();
    const ix = await subscribeIx(program, { investor, pool: k(), mint, vault: k(), usdcMint: k(), amount: 250_000_000n });
    const keys = ix.keys.map((m) => m.pubkey.toBase58());
    expect(keys).toContain(investorPda(pid, investor).toBase58());
    expect(keys).toContain(getAssociatedTokenAddressSync(mint, investor, false, TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID).toBase58());
    expect(keys).toContain(ASSOCIATED_TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID.toBase58());
    expect(ix.keys[0]?.isWritable).toBe(true); // investor pays rent for the instrument account
    expect(String(decode(ix).data.amount)).toBe("250000000");
  });

  it("approves an investor platform-wide with only the record accounts", async () => {
    const investor = k();
    const ix = await approveInvestorIx(program, { compliance: k(), investor });
    expect(ix.keys).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(ix.keys.some((m) => m.pubkey.equals(investorPda(pid, investor)))).toBe(true);
  });

  it("revokes without an instrument account when the investor never subscribed", async () => {
    const investor = k();
    const mint = k();
    const without = await revokeInvestorIx(program, { compliance: k(), pool: k(), mint, investor, hasInstrumentAccount: false });
    const ata = getAssociatedTokenAddressSync(mint, investor, false, TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID);
    expect(without.keys.some((m) => m.pubkey.equals(ata))).toBe(false);
    const withAta = await revokeInvestorIx(program, { compliance: k(), pool: k(), mint, investor, hasInstrumentAccount: true });
    expect(withAta.keys.some((m) => m.pubkey.equals(ata))).toBe(true);
  });

  it("encodes the settlement rule", async () => {
    const ix = await recordSettlementIx(program, { issuer: k(), pool: k(), project: k(), rule: "CoveredFromOtherFields" });
    expect(Object.keys(decode(ix).data.rule as object)[0]).toBe("coveredFromOtherFields");
  });

  it("creates the instrument ATA idempotently under Token-2022", () => {
    const ix = createInstrumentAtaIx(k(), k(), k());
    expect(ix.programId.equals(ASSOCIATED_TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID)).toBe(true);
    expect(ix.keys.some((m) => m.pubkey.equals(TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID))).toBe(true);
    expect(ix.data[0]).toBe(1); // CreateIdempotent
  });
});
```

`apps/web/test/send.test.ts`:
```ts
import { Keypair, SystemProgram, VersionedTransaction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ChainTxError, buildV0, bytesToBase64, sendServerTx, simulate } from "../src/lib/chain/send";

const blockhash = Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58();
const ix = (from = Keypair.generate().publicKey) => SystemProgram.transfer({ fromPubkey: from, toPubkey: Keypair.generate().publicKey, lamports: 1 });

describe("send", () => {
  it("builds an unsigned v0 transaction paid by the payer", async () => {
    const payer = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
    const conn = { getLatestBlockhash: vi.fn(async () => ({ blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight: 99 })) };
    const u = await buildV0(conn as never, payer, [ix(payer)]);
    expect(u.lastValidBlockHeight).toBe(99);
    expect(u.tx.message.recentBlockhash).toBe(blockhash);
    expect(u.tx.message.staticAccountKeys[0]?.equals(payer)).toBe(true);
    const back = VersionedTransaction.deserialize(Buffer.from(bytesToBase64(u.tx.serialize()), "base64"));
    expect(back.message.recentBlockhash).toBe(blockhash);
  });

  it("returns simulation errors and logs", async () => {
    const conn = { simulateTransaction: vi.fn(async () => ({ value: { err: { InstructionError: [1, { Custom: 3012 }] }, logs: ["Program log: x"] } })) };
    const payer = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
    const u = await buildV0({ getLatestBlockhash: async () => ({ blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight: 1 }) } as never, payer, [ix(payer)]);
    const r = await simulate(conn as never, u.tx);
    expect(r.err).toEqual({ InstructionError: [1, { Custom: 3012 }] });
    expect(r.logs).toEqual(["Program log: x"]);
    expect(conn.simulateTransaction).toHaveBeenCalledWith(u.tx, expect.objectContaining({ sigVerify: false }));
  });

  it("wraps preflight failures with their logs and no signature", async () => {
    const signer = Keypair.generate();
    const conn = {
      getLatestBlockhash: async () => ({ blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight: 1 }),
      sendRawTransaction: vi.fn(async () => {
        throw Object.assign(new Error("Simulation failed"), { logs: ["Program log: Error Code: PoolNotOpen."] });
      }),
    };
    const err = await sendServerTx(conn as never, [signer], [ix(signer.publicKey)]).catch((e) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ChainTxError);
    expect(err.logs).toEqual(["Program log: Error Code: PoolNotOpen."]);
    expect(err.signature).toBeNull();
  });

  it("reports an on-chain failure with the signature and fetched logs", async () => {
    const signer = Keypair.generate();
    const conn = {
      getLatestBlockhash: async () => ({ blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight: 1 }),
      sendRawTransaction: vi.fn(async () => "5sig"),
      confirmTransaction: vi.fn(async () => ({ value: { err: { InstructionError: [0, { Custom: 6007 }] } } })),
      getTransaction: vi.fn(async () => ({ meta: { logMessages: ["Program log: Error Code: PoolNotOpen."] } })),
    };
    const err = await sendServerTx(conn as never, [signer], [ix(signer.publicKey)]).catch((e) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ChainTxError);
    expect(err.signature).toBe("5sig");
    expect(err.logs[0]).toContain("PoolNotOpen");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { explainChainError, extractLogs } from "../src/lib/chain/explain";

const PID = "9syreF6kWfmMfoCkTSE8f7p3kRY1Bh7TSDjGAr9s6Jfm";

describe("explainChainError", () => {
  it("turns a missing InvestorRecord into a KYC message (the demo beat)", () => {
    const e = explainChainError({
      logs: [
        `Program ${PID} invoke [1]`,
        "Program log: Instruction: Subscribe",
        "Program log: AnchorError caused by account: investor_record. Error Code: AccountNotInitialized. Error Number: 3012. Error Message: The program expected this account to be already initialized.",
        `Program ${PID} failed: custom program error: 0xbc4`,
      ],
    });
    expect(e).toMatchObject({ code: "KycRequired", title: "This wallet hasn't passed KYC", action: "kyc" });
  });

  it("maps a revoked investor to KYC as well", () => {
    const e = explainChainError({
      logs: ["Program log: AnchorError thrown in programs/f2f/src/instructions/subscribe.rs:40. Error Code: InvestorNotApproved. Error Number: 6024. Error Message: x."],
    });
    expect(e).toMatchObject({ code: "InvestorNotApproved", action: "kyc" });
  });

  it("explains a frozen token account", () => {
    const e = explainChainError({
      logs: [
        "Program TokenzQdBNbLqP5VEhdkAS6EPFLC1PHnBqCXEpPxuEb invoke [1]",
        "Program log: Instruction: TransferChecked",
        "Program log: Error: Account is frozen",
        "Program TokenzQdBNbLqP5VEhdkAS6EPFLC1PHnBqCXEpPxuEb failed: custom program error: 0x11",
      ],
    });
    expect(e).toMatchObject({ code: "AccountFrozen", action: "kyc" });
  });

  it("points to the faucet for missing USDC, a missing USDC account, or missing SOL", () => {
    expect(explainChainError({ logs: ["Program log: Error: insufficient funds", "Program Tokenkeg failed: custom program error: 0x1"] })).toMatchObject({ code: "InsufficientUsdc", action: "faucet" });
    expect(explainChainError({ logs: ["Program log: AnchorError caused by account: investor_usdc. Error Code: AccountNotInitialized. Error Number: 3012."] })).toMatchObject({ code: "InsufficientUsdc", action: "faucet" });
    expect(explainChainError({ error: "AccountNotFound" })).toMatchObject({ code: "NoSol", action: "faucet" });
  });

  it("tells the user to switch networks when the wallet does not know the blockhash", () => {
    expect(explainChainError({ error: new Error("Transaction simulation failed: Blockhash not found") })).toMatchObject({
      code: "WrongNetworkOrExpired",
      title: "Your wallet is on a different network",
      action: "switch-network",
    });
  });

  it("recognises a cancelled signature", () => {
    expect(explainChainError({ error: { name: "WalletSignTransactionError", message: "User rejected the request." } })).toMatchObject({ code: "UserRejected", action: "none" });
  });

  it("maps known program errors and keeps unknown codes", () => {
    expect(explainChainError({ logs: ["Program log: AnchorError occurred. Error Code: ExceedsTarget. Error Number: 6023."] })).toMatchObject({
      code: "ExceedsTarget",
      title: "That is more than the pool still needs",
    });
    expect(explainChainError({ logs: ["Program log: Error Code: SomethingNew."] })).toMatchObject({ code: "SomethingNew", title: "The program rejected this transaction" });
    expect(explainChainError({})).toMatchObject({ code: "Unknown", action: "retry" });
  });

  it("finds logs nested in wallet errors", () => {
    expect(extractLogs({ error: { logs: ["a", "b"] } })).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
    expect(extractLogs({ transactionLogs: ["c"] })).toEqual(["c"]);
    expect(extractLogs(null)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/chain/ix.ts`:
```ts
import type { Program } from "@anchor-lang/core";
import {
  attestationPda,
  configPda,
  fieldPda,
  investorPda,
  mintPda,
  poolPda,
  projectPda,
  toAnchorEnum,
  type AttestationKindName,
  type F2f,
  type VerdictName,
} from "@f2f/sdk";
import {
  ASSOCIATED_TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
  TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID,
  TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
  createAssociatedTokenAccountIdempotentInstruction,
  createMintToInstruction,
  getAssociatedTokenAddressSync,
} from "@solana/spl-token";
import { SystemProgram, type PublicKey, type TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import BN from "bn.js";
import type { SettlementRuleName } from "./enums";

type P = Program<F2f>;
const bn = (v: bigint | number) => new BN(v.toString());
// Anchor's generated enum types are unions of object literals; the runtime shape is { variant: {} }.
const anchorEnum = (name: string) => toAnchorEnum(name) as never;
const system = SystemProgram.programId;

export function hexToBytes(hex: string): Uint8Array {
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(hex)) throw new RangeError("expected 64 lowercase hex characters");
  return Uint8Array.from(hex.match(/../g)!.map((h) => parseInt(h, 16)));
}

export const instrumentAta = (mint: PublicKey, owner: PublicKey) => getAssociatedTokenAddressSync(mint, owner, false, TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID);
export const usdcAta = (usdcMint: PublicKey, owner: PublicKey, allowOffCurve = false) =>
  getAssociatedTokenAddressSync(usdcMint, owner, allowOffCurve, TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID);

export const createInstrumentAtaIx = (payer: PublicKey, owner: PublicKey, mint: PublicKey) =>
  createAssociatedTokenAccountIdempotentInstruction(payer, instrumentAta(mint, owner), owner, mint, TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID);
export const createUsdcAtaIx = (payer: PublicKey, owner: PublicKey, usdcMint: PublicKey) =>
  createAssociatedTokenAccountIdempotentInstruction(payer, usdcAta(usdcMint, owner), owner, usdcMint, TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID);
export const mintUsdcIx = (usdcMint: PublicKey, destination: PublicKey, authority: PublicKey, amount: bigint) =>
  createMintToInstruction(usdcMint, destination, authority, amount, [], TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID);

export function registerProjectIx(
  p: P,
  a: { producer: PublicKey; projectId: bigint; crop: string; season: number; expectedKg: bigint; requestedAmount: bigint },
): Promise<TransactionInstruction> {
  return p.methods
    .registerProject(bn(a.projectId), a.crop, a.season, bn(a.expectedKg), bn(a.requestedAmount))
    .accountsStrict({ producer: a.producer, project: projectPda(p.programId, a.producer, a.projectId), systemProgram: system })
    .instruction();
}

export function registerFieldIx(
  p: P,
  a: { producer: PublicKey; project: PublicKey; index: number; polygonHash: string; areaHaX100: number },
): Promise<TransactionInstruction> {
  return p.methods
    .registerField(Array.from(hexToBytes(a.polygonHash)), a.areaHaX100)
    .accountsStrict({ producer: a.producer, project: a.project, field: fieldPda(p.programId, a.project, a.index), systemProgram: system })
    .instruction();
}

export function recordAttestationIx(
  p: P,
  a: {
    scas: PublicKey;
    project: PublicKey;
    field: PublicKey;
    sequence: number;
    kind: AttestationKindName;
    verdict: VerdictName;
    evidenceHash: Uint8Array;
    evidenceUri: string;
    ndviMeanX10k: number;
    sceneDate: number;
  },
): Promise<TransactionInstruction> {
  return p.methods
    .recordAttestation(anchorEnum(a.kind), anchorEnum(a.verdict), Array.from(a.evidenceHash), a.evidenceUri, a.ndviMeanX10k, bn(a.sceneDate))
    .accountsStrict({
      scas: a.scas,
      config: configPda(p.programId),
      project: a.project,
      field: a.field,
      attestation: attestationPda(p.programId, a.field, a.sequence),
      systemProgram: system,
    })
    .instruction();
}

export function createPoolIx(
  p: P,
  a: {
    issuer: PublicKey;
    usdcMint: PublicKey;
    poolId: bigint;
    crop: string;
    season: number;
    name: string;
    symbol: string;
    uri: string;
    yieldBps: number;
    premiumBps: number;
    subscriptionCloseTs: number;
    maturityTs: number;
  },
): Promise<TransactionInstruction> {
  const pool = poolPda(p.programId, a.poolId);
  return p.methods
    .createPool(bn(a.poolId), {
      crop: a.crop,
      season: a.season,
      name: a.name,
      symbol: a.symbol,
      uri: a.uri,
      yieldBps: a.yieldBps,
      premiumBps: a.premiumBps,
      subscriptionCloseTs: bn(a.subscriptionCloseTs),
      maturityTs: bn(a.maturityTs),
    })
    .accountsStrict({
      issuer: a.issuer,
      config: configPda(p.programId),
      pool,
      mint: mintPda(p.programId, pool),
      usdcMint: a.usdcMint,
      vault: usdcAta(a.usdcMint, pool, true),
      tokenProgram: TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID,
      usdcTokenProgram: TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
      associatedTokenProgram: ASSOCIATED_TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
      systemProgram: system,
    })
    .instruction();
}

export const addProjectIx = (p: P, a: { issuer: PublicKey; pool: PublicKey; project: PublicKey }) =>
  p.methods.addProject().accountsStrict({ issuer: a.issuer, config: configPda(p.programId), pool: a.pool, project: a.project }).instruction();

export const openPoolIx = (p: P, a: { issuer: PublicKey; pool: PublicKey }) =>
  p.methods.openPool().accountsStrict({ issuer: a.issuer, config: configPda(p.programId), pool: a.pool }).instruction();

export const closeSubscriptionIx = (p: P, a: { caller: PublicKey; pool: PublicKey }) =>
  p.methods.closeSubscription().accountsStrict({ caller: a.caller, pool: a.pool }).instruction();

/** Platform-level KYC (ADR 0011): one approval per wallet covers every pool. No pool, mint or token account. */
export const approveInvestorIx = (p: P, a: { compliance: PublicKey; investor: PublicKey }) =>
  p.methods
    .approveInvestor()
    .accountsStrict({
      compliance: a.compliance,
      config: configPda(p.programId),
      investor: a.investor,
      investorRecord: investorPda(p.programId, a.investor),
      systemProgram: system,
    })
    .instruction();

/** Clears approval; freezes this pool's Instrument account only if it exists (`investor_instrument` is optional). */
export const revokeInvestorIx = (
  p: P,
  a: { compliance: PublicKey; pool: PublicKey; mint: PublicKey; investor: PublicKey; hasInstrumentAccount: boolean },
) =>
  p.methods
    .revokeInvestor()
    .accountsStrict({
      compliance: a.compliance,
      config: configPda(p.programId),
      pool: a.pool,
      mint: a.mint,
      investor: a.investor,
      investorRecord: investorPda(p.programId, a.investor),
      investorInstrument: a.hasInstrumentAccount ? instrumentAta(a.mint, a.investor) : null,
      tokenProgram: TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID,
    })
    .instruction();

type HolderArgs = { investor: PublicKey; pool: PublicKey; mint: PublicKey; vault: PublicKey; usdcMint: PublicKey; amount: bigint };

/** ADR 0011: subscribe creates the investor's Instrument account (init_if_needed) and thaws it when the record is approved. */
export const subscribeIx = (p: P, a: HolderArgs) =>
  p.methods
    .subscribe(bn(a.amount))
    .accountsStrict({
      investor: a.investor,
      pool: a.pool,
      investorRecord: investorPda(p.programId, a.investor),
      mint: a.mint,
      investorInstrument: instrumentAta(a.mint, a.investor),
      usdcMint: a.usdcMint,
      investorUsdc: usdcAta(a.usdcMint, a.investor),
      vault: a.vault,
      tokenProgram: TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID,
      usdcTokenProgram: TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
      associatedTokenProgram: ASSOCIATED_TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
      systemProgram: system,
    })
    .instruction();

const holderAccounts = (a: HolderArgs) => ({
  investor: a.investor,
  pool: a.pool,
  mint: a.mint,
  investorInstrument: instrumentAta(a.mint, a.investor),
  usdcMint: a.usdcMint,
  investorUsdc: usdcAta(a.usdcMint, a.investor),
  vault: a.vault,
  tokenProgram: TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID,
  usdcTokenProgram: TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
});

export const refundIx = (p: P, a: HolderArgs) => p.methods.refund(bn(a.amount)).accountsStrict(holderAccounts(a)).instruction();
export const redeemIx = (p: P, a: HolderArgs) => p.methods.redeem(bn(a.amount)).accountsStrict(holderAccounts(a)).instruction();

export const disburseIx = (
  p: P,
  a: { issuer: PublicKey; pool: PublicKey; project: PublicKey; producer: PublicKey; usdcMint: PublicKey; vault: PublicKey },
) =>
  p.methods
    .disburse()
    .accountsStrict({
      issuer: a.issuer,
      config: configPda(p.programId),
      pool: a.pool,
      project: a.project,
      producer: a.producer,
      usdcMint: a.usdcMint,
      vault: a.vault,
      producerUsdc: usdcAta(a.usdcMint, a.producer),
      usdcTokenProgram: TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
      associatedTokenProgram: ASSOCIATED_TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
      systemProgram: system,
    })
    .instruction();

export const recordSettlementIx = (p: P, a: { issuer: PublicKey; pool: PublicKey; project: PublicKey; rule: SettlementRuleName }) =>
  p.methods
    .recordSettlement(anchorEnum(a.rule))
    .accountsStrict({ issuer: a.issuer, config: configPda(p.programId), pool: a.pool, project: a.project })
    .instruction();

export const fundRedemptionIx = (p: P, a: { issuer: PublicKey; pool: PublicKey; usdcMint: PublicKey; vault: PublicKey; amount: bigint }) =>
  p.methods
    .fundRedemption(bn(a.amount))
    .accountsStrict({
      issuer: a.issuer,
      config: configPda(p.programId),
      pool: a.pool,
      usdcMint: a.usdcMint,
      issuerUsdc: usdcAta(a.usdcMint, a.issuer),
      vault: a.vault,
      usdcTokenProgram: TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
    })
    .instruction();

export const sweepIx = (p: P, a: { issuer: PublicKey; pool: PublicKey; mint: PublicKey; usdcMint: PublicKey; vault: PublicKey }) =>
  p.methods
    .sweep()
    .accountsStrict({
      issuer: a.issuer,
      config: configPda(p.programId),
      pool: a.pool,
      mint: a.mint,
      usdcMint: a.usdcMint,
      vault: a.vault,
      issuerUsdc: usdcAta(a.usdcMint, a.issuer),
      usdcTokenProgram: TOKEN_PROGRAM_ID,
    })
    .instruction();
```

`apps/web/src/lib/chain/explain.ts`:
```ts
export type ErrorAction = "kyc" | "faucet" | "switch-network" | "retry" | "none";

export interface ExplainedError {
  code: string;
  title: string;
  detail: string;
  action: ErrorAction;
}

type Entry = [title: string, detail: string, action?: ErrorAction];

/** F2fError variants from anchor/programs/f2f/src/error.rs in plain words. No em dashes (style-lock). */
const PROGRAM: Record<string, Entry> = {
  Unauthorized: ["This key is not allowed to do that", "The signer does not hold the role this step needs."],
  InvalidPremium: ["Premium must be between 5% and 6%", "Set the premium between 500 and 600 basis points."],
  InvalidYield: ["Yield is too high", "Fixed yield can be at most 50%."],
  InvalidSchedule: ["Check the dates", "Subscription must close in the future and before maturity."],
  StringTooLong: ["A text field is too long", "Crop up to 16 characters, name up to 32, symbol up to 10."],
  ZeroAmount: ["Enter an amount above zero", "The amount must be greater than zero."],
  PoolNotDraft: ["The pool is no longer a draft", "Projects can only be added before the pool opens."],
  PoolNotOpen: ["The pool is not open for subscription", "Subscriptions are accepted only while the pool is open.", "retry"],
  PoolNotFunded: ["The pool is not funded", "This step needs the pool to be Funded."],
  PoolNotRefunding: ["Refunds are not open", "Refunds open only if the pool closes under target."],
  PoolNotRedeeming: ["Redemption is not open yet", "Redemption opens after maturity once the Issuer has funded principal plus yield.", "retry"],
  NoProjects: ["Add a project first", "A pool needs at least one verified project before it opens."],
  ProjectAlreadyInPool: ["The project is already in a pool", "A Farm Project joins at most one pool."],
  ProjectNotInPool: ["The project is not in this pool", "Pick a project that belongs to this pool."],
  CropMismatch: ["Crop or season does not match the pool", "Only projects with the pool's crop and season can join."],
  NoFields: ["The project has no fields", "Register at least one field."],
  TooManyFields: ["Too many fields", "A project can have at most 8 fields."],
  FieldsNotVerified: ["Some fields are not verified yet", "Every field needs a passing SCAS check before the project joins a pool."],
  ProjectLocked: ["The project is locked", "Fields cannot be added after the project joins a pool."],
  FieldMismatch: ["The field does not belong to this project", "Check the project and field index."],
  InvalidNdvi: ["Vegetation index out of range", "NDVI must be between -1 and 1."],
  SubscriptionClosed: ["Subscription has closed", "The subscription window for this pool has ended."],
  SubscriptionStillOpen: ["Subscription is still open", "Close the pool when it is full or after the close time."],
  ExceedsTarget: ["That is more than the pool still needs", "Lower the amount to the remaining capacity."],
  InvestorNotApproved: ["This wallet hasn't passed KYC", "Its KYC approval is missing or was revoked, so it cannot hold the Instrument.", "kyc"],
  AlreadyDisbursed: ["Already disbursed", "This project has already been paid."],
  NotDisbursed: ["Disburse first", "Settlement is recorded only after the Producer was paid."],
  HarvestNotAttested: ["No harvest attestation yet", "Record a Harvest attestation for this project before settlement."],
  AlreadySettled: ["Already settled", "This project already has a settlement rule."],
  NotAllDisbursed: ["Disburse every project first", "Redemption deposits are accepted only after every project is paid."],
  InstrumentOutstanding: ["Instrument still outstanding", "Sweep is possible only after every investor has redeemed."],
  MathOverflow: ["Amount too large", "The amount overflows the program's arithmetic."],
};

export function messageOf(e: unknown): string {
  if (e == null) return "";
  if (typeof e === "string") return e;
  if (e instanceof Error) return e.message;
  if (typeof e === "object" && typeof (e as { message?: unknown }).message === "string") return (e as { message: string }).message;
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(e);
  } catch {
    return String(e);
  }
}

export function extractLogs(e: unknown, depth = 0): string[] {
  if (!e || typeof e !== "object" || depth > 3) return [];
  const o = e as Record<string, unknown>;
  for (const k of ["logs", "transactionLogs"]) {
    const v = o[k];
    if (Array.isArray(v) && v.every((x) => typeof x === "string")) return v as string[];
  }
  for (const k of ["error", "cause", "simulationResponse"]) {
    const r = extractLogs(o[k], depth + 1);
    if (r.length) return r;
  }
  return [];
}

export function explainChainError(input: { logs?: readonly string[] | null; error?: unknown }): ExplainedError {
  const text = [...(input.logs ?? []), messageOf(input.error)].join("\n");

  if (/caused by account: investor_record\. Error Code: AccountNotInitialized/.test(text)) {
    return {
      code: "KycRequired",
      title: "This wallet hasn't passed KYC",
      detail: "Instrument accounts start frozen and only KYC-approved wallets can subscribe. Complete KYC, then subscribe again.",
      action: "kyc",
    };
  }
  if (/Account is frozen|custom program error: 0x11\b/.test(text)) {
    return {
      code: "AccountFrozen",
      title: "This token account is frozen",
      detail: "Instrument accounts start frozen. Only a KYC-approved wallet can receive or send the Instrument.",
      action: "kyc",
    };
  }
  if (/User rejected|rejected the request|Approval Denied/i.test(text)) {
    return { code: "UserRejected", title: "Signature cancelled", detail: "Nothing was sent. You can try again.", action: "none" };
  }
  if (/Blockhash not found|BlockhashNotFound|block height exceeded/i.test(text)) {
    return {
      code: "WrongNetworkOrExpired",
      title: "Your wallet is on a different network",
      detail: "Switch your wallet to Devnet and try again. If it already is on Devnet, the transaction expired; try again.",
      action: "switch-network",
    };
  }
  if (/Attempt to debit an account but found no record of a prior credit|AccountNotFound|insufficient lamports/i.test(text)) {
    return { code: "NoSol", title: "Not enough devnet SOL for fees", detail: "Get free devnet SOL from the faucet, then try again.", action: "faucet" };
  }
  if (
    /Error: insufficient funds|custom program error: 0x1\b/.test(text) ||
    /caused by account: investor_usdc\. Error Code: AccountNotInitialized/.test(text)
  ) {
    return { code: "InsufficientUsdc", title: "Not enough test USDC", detail: "Get test USDC from the faucet, then try again.", action: "faucet" };
  }
  const m = /Error Code: (\w+)/.exec(text);
  if (m) {
    const code = m[1] as string;
    const known = PROGRAM[code];
    if (known) return { code, title: known[0], detail: known[1], action: known[2] ?? "none" };
    return { code, title: "The program rejected this transaction", detail: `Program error ${code}.`, action: "none" };
  }
  return {
    code: "Unknown",
    title: "Something went wrong",
    detail: messageOf(input.error) || "The transaction did not go through. Try again.",
    action: "retry",
  };
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/chain/send.ts`:
```ts
import { TransactionMessage, VersionedTransaction, type Connection, type Keypair, type PublicKey, type TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { extractLogs, messageOf } from "./explain";

export interface UnsignedTx {
  tx: VersionedTransaction;
  blockhash: string;
  lastValidBlockHeight: number;
}

export async function buildV0(
  connection: Pick<Connection, "getLatestBlockhash">,
  payer: PublicKey,
  ixs: TransactionInstruction[],
): Promise<UnsignedTx> {
  const { blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight } = await connection.getLatestBlockhash("confirmed");
  const message = new TransactionMessage({ payerKey: payer, recentBlockhash: blockhash, instructions: ixs }).compileToV0Message();
  return { tx: new VersionedTransaction(message), blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight };
}

export interface SimulationResult {
  err: unknown | null;
  logs: string[];
}

/** Simulates an unsigned transaction so the user sees a program rejection before their wallet opens. */
export async function simulate(connection: Pick<Connection, "simulateTransaction">, tx: VersionedTransaction): Promise<SimulationResult> {
  const r = await connection.simulateTransaction(tx, { sigVerify: false, replaceRecentBlockhash: false, commitment: "confirmed" });
  return { err: r.value.err ?? null, logs: r.value.logs ?? [] };
}

export class ChainTxError extends Error {
  override name = "ChainTxError";
  constructor(
    message: string,
    readonly logs: string[],
    readonly signature: string | null,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

export async function confirmOrThrow(
  connection: Pick<Connection, "confirmTransaction" | "getTransaction">,
  signature: string,
  blockhash: string,
  lastValidBlockHeight: number,
): Promise<void> {
  const res = await connection.confirmTransaction({ signature, blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight }, "confirmed");
  if (res.value.err) {
    const t = await connection.getTransaction(signature, { commitment: "confirmed", maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 0 });
    throw new ChainTxError(`transaction failed: ${JSON.stringify(res.value.err)}`, t?.meta?.logMessages ?? [], signature);
  }
}

/** Signs with server-held keys (first signer pays), sends with preflight and waits for confirmation. */
export async function sendServerTx(connection: Connection, signers: Keypair[], ixs: TransactionInstruction[]): Promise<string> {
  const payer = signers[0];
  if (!payer) throw new Error("sendServerTx needs at least one signer");
  const { tx, blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight } = await buildV0(connection, payer.publicKey, ixs);
  tx.sign(signers);
  let signature: string;
  try {
    signature = await connection.sendRawTransaction(tx.serialize(), { skipPreflight: false, preflightCommitment: "confirmed", maxRetries: 3 });
  } catch (e) {
    throw new ChainTxError(messageOf(e), extractLogs(e), null);
  }
  await confirmOrThrow(connection, signature, blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight);
  return signature;
}

/** For the demo script only: lands a failing transaction on-chain so it has an explorer link. */
export async function sendSkippingPreflight(
  connection: Connection,
  signers: Keypair[],
  ixs: TransactionInstruction[],
): Promise<{ signature: string; err: unknown | null; logs: string[] }> {
  const payer = signers[0];
  if (!payer) throw new Error("sendSkippingPreflight needs at least one signer");
  const { tx, blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight } = await buildV0(connection, payer.publicKey, ixs);
  tx.sign(signers);
  const signature = await connection.sendRawTransaction(tx.serialize(), { skipPreflight: true, maxRetries: 3 });
  const res = await connection.confirmTransaction({ signature, blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight }, "confirmed");
  const t = await connection.getTransaction(signature, { commitment: "confirmed", maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 0 });
  return { signature, err: res.value.err, logs: t?.meta?.logMessages ?? [] };
}

export const bytesToBase64 = (bytes: Uint8Array) => Buffer.from(bytes).toString("base64");
```

### Task 6: SCAS stub: evidence, serialized attestations, evidence route

SCAS is an external provider integrated later (Task 19 records the decision). Until then the web server plays a stub SCAS: it writes an honest evidence document that says it is a stub, stores it in the bucket under its SHA-256, and signs `record_attestation` with `SCAS_SECRET_KEY` (the `Config.scas` key).

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/serial.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/scas-stub.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/api/evidence/[hash]/route.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/lib/server/context.ts` (append roles, `sendAs`, `getBuildProgram`, `stubDeps`)
- Test: `apps/web/test/scas-stub.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `canonicalJson`, `sha256`, `toHex`, `fieldPda`, `MAX_EVIDENCE_URI_LEN`, `type AttestationKindName`, `type F2f` from `@f2f/sdk`; `recordAttestationIx` (Task 5); `BlobStore`, `evidenceKey`, `serveHashed` (Task 4); `FieldAccount`, `ProjectWithFields` (Task 2); `RequestError`; `sendServerTx` (Task 5); `parseKeypair` (Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `serial.ts`: `interface SerialQueue { run<T>(task: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> }`, `createSerialQueue(): SerialQueue`.
  - `scas-stub.ts`: `SCAS_STUB_PROVIDER = "scas-stub"`, `SCAS_STUB_NOTE = "External SCAS provider integration pending"`, `STUB_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA = "f2f-scas-stub-evidence/1"`, `interface StubEvidence`, `encodeEvidence(doc): { bytes: Uint8Array; hash: Uint8Array; hashHex: string }`, `evidenceUri(publicBaseUrl, hashHex): string`, `interface StubDeps`, `interface AttestStubInput { project: PublicKey; fieldIndex: number; kind: AttestationKindName }`, `interface AttestStubResult`, `attestStub(deps, input): Promise<AttestStubResult>`, `interface BatchResult { results: AttestStubResult[]; failures: { field: string; error: string }[] }`, `verifyAllFields(deps, projects: ProjectWithFields[]): Promise<BatchResult>`, `attestStage(deps, projects: ProjectWithFields[], kind: Exclude<AttestationKindName, "FieldVerified">): Promise<BatchResult>`.
  - `server/context.ts`: `type Role = "issuer" | "compliance" | "scas" | "deployer"`, `roleKeypair(role): Keypair`, `sendAs(role, ixs): Promise<string>`, `getBuildProgram(): Program<F2f>`, `stubDeps(): StubDeps`.
  - `GET /api/evidence/:hash` → the evidence JSON, hash-checked, immutable cache.

Evidence document (the coordinator's fields plus `schema`, `subject` and `signer` so two fields never share an evidence hash and a reader can tell where it belongs):
```json
{
  "schema": "f2f-scas-stub-evidence/1",
  "provider": "scas-stub",
  "note": "External SCAS provider integration pending",
  "kind": "FieldVerified",
  "verdict": "Pass",
  "polygonHash": "<64 hex>",
  "fieldAreaHaX100": 7611,
  "recordedAt": "2026-10-05T10:00:00.000Z",
  "subject": { "cluster": "devnet", "programId": "9syr…", "project": "<base58>", "field": "<base58>", "fieldIndex": 0, "sequence": 0 },
  "signer": "<SCAS public key>"
}
```

`apps/web/test/scas-stub.test.ts`:
```ts
import { attestationPda, canonicalJson, fieldPda, sha256, toHex } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { Keypair, type PublicKey, type TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { FieldAccount, ProjectWithFields } from "../src/lib/chain/views";
import { SCAS_STUB_NOTE, attestStub, evidenceUri, verifyAllFields, type StubDeps, type StubEvidence } from "../src/lib/scas-stub";
import { createSerialQueue } from "../src/lib/serial";
import { MemoryStore, evidenceKey, serveHashed } from "../src/lib/store";
import { fieldValue, offlineProgram, roundTrip } from "./fixtures/accounts";

const program = offlineProgram();
const NOW = new Date("2026-10-05T10:00:00.000Z");
const sleep = (ms: number) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

async function makeDeps(opts: { exists?: boolean } = {}) {
  const project = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
  const store = new MemoryStore();
  let count = 0;
  const sent: TransactionInstruction[][] = [];
  const { account } = await roundTrip<FieldAccount>(program, "field", fieldValue({ project, index: 0, attestationCount: 0 }));
  const deps: StubDeps = {
    program,
    readField: vi.fn(async (_a: PublicKey) => (opts.exists === false ? null : ({ ...account, attestationCount: count } as FieldAccount))),
    send: vi.fn(async (ixs: TransactionInstruction[]) => {
      sent.push(ixs);
      await sleep(5); // the chain takes time; a second read during this window must not reuse the sequence
      count += 1;
      return `sig${count}`;
    }),
    store,
    scas: Keypair.generate().publicKey,
    publicBaseUrl: "https://f2f-web.up.railway.app",
    cluster: "devnet",
    now: () => NOW,
    queue: createSerialQueue(),
  };
  return { deps, project, store, sent, account };
}

describe("createSerialQueue", () => {
  it("runs tasks one after another and survives failures", async () => {
    const q = createSerialQueue();
    const order: string[] = [];
    const a = q.run(async () => {
      await sleep(10);
      order.push("a");
    });
    const b = q.run(async () => {
      order.push("b");
      throw new Error("boom");
    });
    const c = q.run(async () => order.push("c"));
    await a;
    await expect(b).rejects.toThrow("boom");
    await c;
    expect(order).toEqual(["a", "b", "c"]);
  });
});

describe("attestStub", () => {
  it("stores honest stub evidence and signs record_attestation with verdict Pass", async () => {
    const { deps, project, store, sent, account } = await makeDeps();
    const r = await attestStub(deps, { project, fieldIndex: 0, kind: "FieldVerified" });
    const field = fieldPda(program.programId, project, 0);

    const bytes = store.objects.get(evidenceKey(r.evidenceHash));
    expect(bytes).toBeDefined();
    const doc = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) as StubEvidence;
    expect(doc).toMatchObject({
      provider: "scas-stub",
      note: SCAS_STUB_NOTE,
      kind: "FieldVerified",
      verdict: "Pass",
      polygonHash: toHex(Uint8Array.from(account.polygonHash)),
      fieldAreaHaX100: 7611,
      recordedAt: "2026-10-05T10:00:00.000Z",
      subject: { field: field.toBase58(), fieldIndex: 0, sequence: 0, cluster: "devnet" },
    });
    expect(toHex(sha256(canonicalJson(doc)))).toBe(r.evidenceHash);
    expect(r.evidenceUri).toBe(`https://f2f-web.up.railway.app/api/evidence/${r.evidenceHash}`);

    const ix = sent[0]?.[0] as TransactionInstruction;
    expect(ix.keys.some((m) => m.pubkey.equals(attestationPda(program.programId, field, 0)))).toBe(true);
    const decoded = program.coder.instruction.decode(ix.data)!;
    const data = decoded.data as Record<string, unknown>;
    expect(Object.keys(data.verdict as object)[0]).toBe("pass");
    expect(Number(data.ndviMeanX10k ?? data.ndvi_mean_x10k)).toBe(0);
    expect(String(data.sceneDate ?? data.scene_date)).toBe(String(NOW.getTime() / 1000));
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ signature: "sig1", sequence: 0, verdict: "Pass" });
  });

  it("serializes concurrent attestations on the same field", async () => {
    const { deps, project } = await makeDeps();
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([
      attestStub(deps, { project, fieldIndex: 0, kind: "FieldVerified" }),
      attestStub(deps, { project, fieldIndex: 0, kind: "Sowing" }),
    ]);
    expect([a.sequence, b.sequence]).toEqual([0, 1]);
  });

  it("refuses a missing field before storing or signing anything", async () => {
    const { deps, project, store } = await makeDeps({ exists: false });
    await expect(attestStub(deps, { project, fieldIndex: 0, kind: "FieldVerified" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    expect(store.objects.size).toBe(0);
    expect(deps.send).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("serves stored evidence through the hashed route helper", async () => {
    const { deps, project, store } = await makeDeps();
    const r = await attestStub(deps, { project, fieldIndex: 0, kind: "Harvest" });
    const res = await serveHashed(store, evidenceKey(r.evidenceHash), r.evidenceHash);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(((await res.json()) as StubEvidence).kind).toBe("Harvest");
  });
});

describe("evidenceUri", () => {
  it("refuses URIs longer than the program allows", () => {
    expect(() => evidenceUri(`https://${"a".repeat(60)}.app`, "ab".repeat(32))).toThrow(/128/);
  });
});

describe("verifyAllFields", () => {
  it("attests only unverified fields of unpooled projects and reports failures", async () => {
    const { deps, project } = await makeDeps();
    const base = { producer: "", projectId: "1", crop: "wheat", season: 2027, expectedKg: "1", tokenizedKg: "0", requestedAmount: "1", fieldCount: 2, verifiedFieldCount: 1, harvestAttested: false, disbursed: false, settlement: null };
    const f = (index: number, verified: boolean) => ({ address: Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58(), project: project.toBase58(), index, polygonHash: "00".repeat(32), areaHaX100: 1, verified, attestationCount: 0 });
    const projects: ProjectWithFields[] = [
      { ...base, address: project.toBase58(), pool: null, fields: [f(0, false), f(1, true)] },
      { ...base, address: Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58(), pool: "x", fields: [f(0, false)] },
    ];
    const out = await verifyAllFields(deps, projects);
    expect(out.results).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(out.failures).toHaveLength(0);
    vi.mocked(deps.send).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("rpc down"));
    const again = await verifyAllFields(deps, projects);
    expect(again.failures[0]?.error).toBe("rpc down");
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/serial.ts`:
```ts
export interface SerialQueue {
  run<T>(task: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
}

/** One task at a time, in call order. A failed task does not block the next one. */
export function createSerialQueue(): SerialQueue {
  let tail: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
  return {
    run<T>(task: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
      const result = tail.then(() => task());
      tail = result.catch(() => undefined);
      return result;
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/scas-stub.ts`:
```ts
import type { Program } from "@anchor-lang/core";
import { MAX_EVIDENCE_URI_LEN, attestationPda, canonicalJson, fieldPda, sha256, toHex, type AttestationKindName, type F2f } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { PublicKey, type TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { recordAttestationIx } from "./chain/ix";
import type { FieldAccount, ProjectWithFields } from "./chain/views";
import { RequestError } from "./errors";
import { createSerialQueue, type SerialQueue } from "./serial";
import { evidenceKey, type BlobStore } from "./store";

export const SCAS_STUB_PROVIDER = "scas-stub";
export const SCAS_STUB_NOTE = "External SCAS provider integration pending";
export const STUB_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA = "f2f-scas-stub-evidence/1";

export interface StubEvidence {
  schema: typeof STUB_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA;
  provider: typeof SCAS_STUB_PROVIDER;
  note: string;
  kind: AttestationKindName;
  verdict: "Pass";
  polygonHash: string;
  fieldAreaHaX100: number;
  recordedAt: string;
  subject: { cluster: string; programId: string; project: string; field: string; fieldIndex: number; sequence: number };
  signer: string;
}

export function encodeEvidence(doc: StubEvidence): { bytes: Uint8Array; hash: Uint8Array; hashHex: string } {
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJson(doc));
  const hash = sha256(bytes);
  return { bytes, hash, hashHex: toHex(hash) };
}

export function evidenceUri(publicBaseUrl: string, hashHex: string): string {
  const uri = `${publicBaseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "")}/api/evidence/${hashHex}`;
  if (uri.length > MAX_EVIDENCE_URI_LEN) {
    throw new Error(`evidence URI is ${uri.length} bytes; the program allows ${MAX_EVIDENCE_URI_LEN}. Shorten PUBLIC_BASE_URL.`);
  }
  return uri;
}

export interface StubDeps {
  /** Used to build instructions; its provider never signs. */
  program: Program<F2f>;
  readField(address: PublicKey): Promise<FieldAccount | null>;
  /** Signs with the SCAS key and confirms. */
  send(ixs: TransactionInstruction[]): Promise<string>;
  store: BlobStore;
  scas: PublicKey;
  publicBaseUrl: string;
  cluster: string;
  now?: () => Date;
  queue?: SerialQueue;
}

export interface AttestStubInput {
  project: PublicKey;
  fieldIndex: number;
  kind: AttestationKindName;
}

export interface AttestStubResult {
  kind: AttestationKindName;
  verdict: "Pass";
  project: string;
  field: string;
  fieldIndex: number;
  sequence: number;
  attestation: string;
  signature: string;
  evidenceHash: string;
  evidenceUri: string;
}

/** One queue per process: attestation PDAs are keyed by attestation_count, so sends on a field must not overlap. */
const processQueue = createSerialQueue();

export function attestStub(deps: StubDeps, input: AttestStubInput): Promise<AttestStubResult> {
  return (deps.queue ?? processQueue).run(async () => {
    const programId = deps.program.programId;
    const field = fieldPda(programId, input.project, input.fieldIndex);
    const account = await deps.readField(field);
    if (!account) {
      throw new RequestError(`Field ${input.fieldIndex} of project ${input.project.toBase58()} does not exist`, 404);
    }
    const now = (deps.now ?? (() => new Date()))();
    const sequence = account.attestationCount;
    const doc: StubEvidence = {
      schema: STUB_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA,
      provider: SCAS_STUB_PROVIDER,
      note: SCAS_STUB_NOTE,
      kind: input.kind,
      verdict: "Pass",
      polygonHash: toHex(Uint8Array.from(account.polygonHash)),
      fieldAreaHaX100: account.areaHaX100,
      recordedAt: now.toISOString(),
      subject: {
        cluster: deps.cluster,
        programId: programId.toBase58(),
        project: input.project.toBase58(),
        field: field.toBase58(),
        fieldIndex: input.fieldIndex,
        sequence,
      },
      signer: deps.scas.toBase58(),
    };
    const enc = encodeEvidence(doc);
    const uri = evidenceUri(deps.publicBaseUrl, enc.hashHex);
    // Evidence first: an Attestation must never point at a missing document.
    await deps.store.put(evidenceKey(enc.hashHex), enc.bytes, "application/json");
    const ix = await recordAttestationIx(deps.program, {
      scas: deps.scas,
      project: input.project,
      field,
      sequence,
      kind: input.kind,
      verdict: "Pass",
      evidenceHash: enc.hash,
      evidenceUri: uri,
      ndviMeanX10k: 0,
      sceneDate: Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000),
    });
    const signature = await deps.send([ix]);
    return {
      kind: input.kind,
      verdict: "Pass",
      project: input.project.toBase58(),
      field: field.toBase58(),
      fieldIndex: input.fieldIndex,
      sequence,
      attestation: attestationPda(programId, field, sequence).toBase58(),
      signature,
      evidenceHash: enc.hashHex,
      evidenceUri: uri,
    };
  });
}

export interface BatchResult {
  results: AttestStubResult[];
  failures: { field: string; error: string }[];
}

async function runBatch(deps: StubDeps, targets: { project: string; field: string; index: number }[], kind: AttestationKindName): Promise<BatchResult> {
  const out: BatchResult = { results: [], failures: [] };
  for (const t of targets) {
    try {
      out.results.push(await attestStub(deps, { project: new PublicKey(t.project), fieldIndex: t.index, kind }));
    } catch (e) {
      out.failures.push({ field: t.field, error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Admin "Verify all fields": FieldVerified for every unverified field of every project not yet in a pool. */
export function verifyAllFields(deps: StubDeps, projects: ProjectWithFields[]): Promise<BatchResult> {
  const targets = projects
    .filter((p) => p.pool === null)
    .flatMap((p) => p.fields.filter((f) => !f.verified).map((f) => ({ project: p.address, field: f.address, index: f.index })));
  return runBatch(deps, targets, "FieldVerified");
}

/** Admin "Attest season stage": one Attestation of `kind` for every field of the given projects. */
export function attestStage(deps: StubDeps, projects: ProjectWithFields[], kind: Exclude<AttestationKindName, "FieldVerified">): Promise<BatchResult> {
  const targets = projects.flatMap((p) => p.fields.map((f) => ({ project: p.address, field: f.address, index: f.index })));
  return runBatch(deps, targets, kind);
}
```

```ts
import type { Program } from "@anchor-lang/core";
import type { F2f } from "@f2f/sdk";
import type { Keypair, TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { sendServerTx } from "../chain/send";
import { parseKeypair } from "../keys";
import type { StubDeps } from "../scas-stub";

export type Role = "issuer" | "compliance" | "scas" | "deployer";
const ROLE_ENV = {
  issuer: "F2F_ISSUER_KEYPAIR",
  compliance: "F2F_COMPLIANCE_KEYPAIR",
  scas: "SCAS_SECRET_KEY",
  deployer: "F2F_DEPLOYER_KEYPAIR",
} as const;
const keypairs = new Map<Role, Keypair>();

export function roleKeypair(role: Role): Keypair {
  let kp = keypairs.get(role);
  if (!kp) {
    const name = ROLE_ENV[role];
    kp = parseKeypair(name, serverEnv()[name]);
    keypairs.set(role, kp);
  }
  return kp;
}

export async function sendAs(role: Role, ixs: TransactionInstruction[]): Promise<string> {
  try {
    return await sendServerTx(getConnection(), [roleKeypair(role)], ixs);
  } finally {
    invalidateReads();
  }
}

let buildProgram: Program<F2f> | undefined;
export function getBuildProgram(): Program<F2f> {
  buildProgram ??= createProgram(getConnection(), getProgramId());
  return buildProgram;
}

export function stubDeps(): StubDeps {
  const program = getBuildProgram();
  return {
    program,
    readField: (address) => program.account.field.fetchNullable(address),
    send: (ixs) => sendAs("scas", ixs),
    store: getStore(),
    scas: roleKeypair("scas").publicKey,
    publicBaseUrl: serverEnv().PUBLIC_BASE_URL,
    cluster: serverEnv().SOLANA_CLUSTER,
  };
}
```

### Task 7: Server-built user transactions, tx routes, wallet read routes

Users sign in the browser; the server builds and simulates. A program rejection (for example the non-KYC subscribe) comes back as an explained error before the wallet opens.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/chain/user-tx.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/api/tx/[action]/route.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/api/tx/confirm/route.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/api/producer/[wallet]/route.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/api/investor/[wallet]/route.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/test/user-tx.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `registerProjectIx`, `registerFieldIx`, `subscribeIx`, `refundIx`, `redeemIx`, `createUsdcAtaIx` (Task 5); `buildV0`, `simulate`, `confirmOrThrow`, `ChainTxError`, `bytesToBase64` (Task 5); `explainChainError` (Task 5); `BlobStore`, `polygonKey`, `HEX64` (Task 4); `PoolView` (Task 2); `getReader`, `getConnection`, `getBuildProgram`, `getStore`, `getUsdcMint`, `invalidateReads` (context).
- Produces:
  - `TX_ACTIONS = ["register-project", "subscribe", "refund", "redeem"] as const`, `type TxAction`.
  - `interface UserTxDeps { program: Program<F2f>; store: BlobStore; usdcMint: PublicKey; getPool(poolId: string): Promise<PoolView | null>; now?: () => number }`.
  - `interface BuiltUserTx { payer: PublicKey; instructions: TransactionInstruction[]; meta: Record<string, string> }`.
  - `buildUserTx(deps, action: TxAction, body: unknown): Promise<BuiltUserTx>` (throws `RequestError` 400/404/422).
  - Request bodies: `register-project` `{ signer, crop, season, expectedKg: string, requestedAmount: string, fields: { polygonHash, areaHaX100 }[] }`; `subscribe` / `refund` / `redeem` `{ signer, poolId: string, amount: string }`. Amounts are decimal strings of base units (USDC) or kilograms.
  - `POST /api/tx/:action` → `200 { ok: true, tx: base64, blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight, meta }` | `422 { ok: false, error: ExplainedError, logs }` (simulation rejected) | `400/404 { ok: false, error, logs: [] }`.
  - `POST /api/tx/confirm` body `{ signature, blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight }` → `{ ok: true }` | `422 { ok: false, error, logs, signature }`.
  - `GET /api/producer/:wallet` → `{ projects: ProducerProject[] }` where `type ProducerProject = ProjectWithFields & { poolId: string | null; poolStatus: PoolStatusName | null; symbol: string | null }`.
  - `GET /api/investor/:wallet` → `InvestorState`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the builder**

`apps/web/src/lib/chain/user-tx.ts`:
```ts
import type { Program } from "@anchor-lang/core";
import { projectPda, type F2f } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { PublicKey, type TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { z } from "zod";
import { RequestError } from "../errors";
import { HEX64, polygonKey, type BlobStore } from "../store";
import { createUsdcAtaIx, redeemIx, refundIx, registerFieldIx, registerProjectIx, subscribeIx } from "./ix";
import type { PoolView } from "./views";

export const TX_ACTIONS = ["register-project", "subscribe", "refund", "redeem"] as const;
export type TxAction = (typeof TX_ACTIONS)[number];

const pubkey = z.string().regex(/^[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,44}$/, "must be a base58 public key");
const positive = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^\d{1,20}$/, "must be a whole number of base units")
  .transform((v) => BigInt(v))
  .refine((v) => v > 0n, "must be above zero");
const poolId = z.string().regex(/^\d{1,20}$/, "must be a pool id");

const registerProject = z.object({
  signer: pubkey,
  crop: z.string().trim().min(1).max(16),
  season: z.number().int().min(2025).max(2100),
  expectedKg: positive,
  requestedAmount: positive,
  fields: z
    .array(z.object({ polygonHash: z.string().regex(HEX64), areaHaX100: z.number().int().positive().max(4_294_967_295) }))
    .min(1)
    .max(4),
});
const holder = z.object({ signer: pubkey, poolId, amount: positive });

export interface UserTxDeps {
  program: Program<F2f>;
  store: BlobStore;
  usdcMint: PublicKey;
  getPool(poolId: string): Promise<PoolView | null>;
  now?: () => number;
}

export interface BuiltUserTx {
  payer: PublicKey;
  instructions: TransactionInstruction[];
  meta: Record<string, string>;
}

function parse<T>(schema: z.ZodType<T>, body: unknown): T {
  const r = schema.safeParse(body);
  if (!r.success) throw new RequestError(r.error.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join(".")}: ${i.message}`).join("; "), 400);
  return r.data;
}

export async function buildUserTx(deps: UserTxDeps, action: TxAction, body: unknown): Promise<BuiltUserTx> {
  const { program } = deps;
  if (action === "register-project") {
    const b = parse(registerProject, body);
    for (const [i, f] of b.fields.entries()) {
      if (!(await deps.store.get(polygonKey(f.polygonHash)))) {
        throw new RequestError(`Field ${i + 1} boundary was not uploaded. Upload the GeoJSON again.`, 422);
      }
    }
    const producer = new PublicKey(b.signer);
    // Unique per producer for a demo; the project PDA includes the producer key.
    const projectId = BigInt((deps.now ?? Date.now)());
    const project = projectPda(program.programId, producer, projectId);
    const instructions = [
      await registerProjectIx(program, { producer, projectId, crop: b.crop, season: b.season, expectedKg: b.expectedKg, requestedAmount: b.requestedAmount }),
      ...(await Promise.all(
        b.fields.map((f, index) => registerFieldIx(program, { producer, project, index, polygonHash: f.polygonHash, areaHaX100: f.areaHaX100 })),
      )),
    ];
    return { payer: producer, instructions, meta: { projectId: projectId.toString(), project: project.toBase58() } };
  }

  const b = parse(holder, body);
  const pool = await deps.getPool(b.poolId);
  if (!pool) throw new RequestError(`Pool ${b.poolId} does not exist`, 404);
  const investor = new PublicKey(b.signer);
  const args = {
    investor,
    pool: new PublicKey(pool.address),
    mint: new PublicKey(pool.mint),
    vault: new PublicKey(pool.vault),
    usdcMint: deps.usdcMint,
    amount: b.amount,
  };
  if (action === "subscribe") {
    // ADR 0011: subscribe creates and thaws the Instrument account itself. A wallet without an approved
    // InvestorRecord fails with AccountNotInitialized or InvestorNotApproved: the demo beat.
    return { payer: investor, instructions: [await subscribeIx(program, args)], meta: { poolId: b.poolId } };
  }
  const ix = action === "refund" ? await refundIx(program, args) : await redeemIx(program, args);
  return { payer: investor, instructions: [createUsdcAtaIx(investor, investor, deps.usdcMint), ix], meta: { poolId: b.poolId } };
}
```

```ts
import * as Sentry from "@sentry/nextjs";
import { explainChainError } from "@/lib/chain/explain";
import { buildV0, bytesToBase64, simulate } from "@/lib/chain/send";
import { TX_ACTIONS, buildUserTx, type TxAction } from "@/lib/chain/user-tx";
import { RequestError } from "@/lib/errors";
import { getBuildProgram, getConnection, getReader, getStore, getUsdcMint } from "@/lib/server/context";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const fail = (status: number, code: string, title: string, detail: string) =>
  Response.json({ ok: false, error: { code, title, detail, action: "none" }, logs: [] }, { status });

export async function POST(req: Request, ctx: { params: Promise<{ action: string }> }) {
  const { action } = await ctx.params;
  if (!(TX_ACTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(action)) return fail(404, "UnknownAction", "Unknown action", action);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  try {
    const built = await buildUserTx(
      { program: getBuildProgram(), store: getStore(), usdcMint: getUsdcMint(), getPool: async (id) => (await getReader().getPool(id))?.pool ?? null },
      action as TxAction,
      body,
    );
    const connection = getConnection();
    const { tx, blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight } = await buildV0(connection, built.payer, built.instructions);
    const sim = await simulate(connection, tx);
    if (sim.err) {
      return Response.json({ ok: false, error: explainChainError({ logs: sim.logs, error: sim.err }), logs: sim.logs }, { status: 422 });
    }
    return Response.json({ ok: true, tx: bytesToBase64(tx.serialize()), blockhash, lastValidBlockHeight, meta: built.meta });
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof RequestError) return fail(e.status, "BadRequest", "Check the form", e.message);
    Sentry.captureException(e);
    return fail(500, "ServerError", "The server could not build this transaction", "Try again in a moment.");
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/api/tx/confirm/route.ts`:
```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { explainChainError } from "@/lib/chain/explain";
import { ChainTxError, confirmOrThrow } from "@/lib/chain/send";
import { getConnection, invalidateReads } from "@/lib/server/context";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const Body = z.object({
  signature: z.string().regex(/^[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{64,90}$/),
  blockhash: z.string().min(32).max(44),
  lastValidBlockHeight: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
});

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const b = Body.safeParse(await req.json().catch(() => null));
  if (!b.success) {
    return Response.json({ ok: false, error: { code: "BadRequest", title: "Invalid confirmation request", detail: "", action: "none" }, logs: [] }, { status: 400 });
  }
  try {
    await confirmOrThrow(getConnection(), b.data.signature, b.data.blockhash, b.data.lastValidBlockHeight);
    return Response.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (e) {
    const logs = e instanceof ChainTxError ? e.logs : [];
    return Response.json({ ok: false, error: explainChainError({ logs, error: e }), logs, signature: b.data.signature }, { status: 422 });
  } finally {
    invalidateReads();
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/api/producer/[wallet]/route.ts`:
```ts
import { RequestError } from "@/lib/errors";
import { getReader } from "@/lib/server/context";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(_req: Request, ctx: { params: Promise<{ wallet: string }> }) {
  const { wallet } = await ctx.params;
  try {
    const reader = getReader();
    const [projects, pools] = await Promise.all([reader.projectsByProducer(wallet), reader.listPools()]);
    const byAddress = new Map(pools.map((p) => [p.address, p]));
    return Response.json({
      projects: projects.map((p) => {
        const pool = p.pool ? byAddress.get(p.pool) : undefined;
        return { ...p, poolId: pool?.poolId ?? null, poolStatus: pool?.status ?? null, symbol: pool?.instrument?.symbol ?? null };
      }),
    });
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof RequestError) return Response.json({ error: e.message }, { status: e.status });
    throw e;
  }
}
```

- Produces:
  - `admin-ops.ts`: `interface OpsContext` (below), `createPoolInput` (zod), `type CreatePoolInput`, `createPool(ctx, input, publicBaseUrl): Promise<{ signature: string; poolId: string; pool: string }>`, `addProject(ctx, poolId: bigint, project: PublicKey)`, `openPool(ctx, poolId)`, `closeSubscription(ctx, poolId)`, `disburseAll(ctx, poolId): Promise<string[]>`, `recordSettlement(ctx, poolId, project, rule)`, `fundRedemption(ctx, poolId): Promise<{ signatures: string[]; deposited: bigint; required: bigint; maturityPassed: boolean }>`, `sweep(ctx, poolId)`, `approveInvestor(ctx, wallet)` (platform-level, ADR 0011), `revokeInvestor(ctx, poolId, wallet)` (each single-tx op returns `Promise<string>`), `nextPoolId(existing: string[]): string`, `roleMismatches(config: ConfigView, keys: Partial<Record<"issuer" | "compliance" | "scas", string>>): string[]`.
  - `cooldown.ts`: `interface Cooldown { remaining(key: string): number; mark(key: string): void }`, `createCooldown(ms: number, now?: () => number): Cooldown`.

  - `POST /api/kyc` `{ wallet, fullName, country, email, eligible: true, demoConsent: true }` → `{ ok: true, signature }` | `{ ok: false, error }`. One approval covers every pool (ADR 0011). The form values are validated and discarded; nothing is stored or logged.
  - `POST /api/faucet` `{ wallet, usdc: boolean }` → `{ ok: true, signature, solLamports, usdc }` | `429/400 { ok: false, error }`.
  - `app/admin/actions.ts` server actions, each returning `ActionResult = { ok: true; message: string; signatures: string[] } | { ok: false; error: ExplainedError; logs: string[] }`: `loginAction(prev, formData)`, `logoutAction()`, `createPoolAction(input)`, `addProjectAction(poolId, project)`, `openPoolAction(poolId)`, `closeSubscriptionAction(poolId)`, `disburseAllAction(poolId)`, `verifyAllFieldsAction()`, `attestStageAction(poolId, kind)`, `recordSettlementAction(poolId, project, rule)`, `fundRedemptionAction(poolId)`, `sweepAction(poolId)`, `approveInvestorAction(wallet)`, `revokeInvestorAction(poolId, wallet)`.

```ts
export interface OpsContext {
  program: Program<F2f>;                 // builds instructions and reads accounts
  usdcMint: PublicKey;
  roles: { readonly issuer: PublicKey; readonly compliance: PublicKey; readonly deployer: PublicKey };
  send(role: "issuer" | "compliance" | "deployer", ixs: TransactionInstruction[]): Promise<string>;
  loadPool(poolId: bigint): Promise<PoolAccount>;   // throws RequestError 404
  loadProjects(pool: PublicKey): Promise<{ address: PublicKey; account: ProjectAccount }[]>;
  tokenBalance(account: PublicKey): Promise<bigint>; // 0n when the account does not exist
  accountExists(account: PublicKey): Promise<boolean>;
  now(): number;                                     // unix seconds
}
```
