```ts
import { investorPda, poolPda } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID, getAssociatedTokenAddressSync } from "@solana/spl-token";
import { Keypair, type PublicKey, type TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import BN from "bn.js";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { PoolAccount, ProjectAccount } from "../src/lib/chain/views";
import {
  approveInvestor,
  createPool,
  createPoolInput,
  disburseAll,
  fundRedemption,
  nextPoolId,
  revokeInvestor,
  roleMismatches,
  type OpsContext,
} from "../src/lib/chain/admin-ops";
import { offlineProgram, poolValue, projectValue, roundTrip } from "./fixtures/accounts";

const program = offlineProgram();
const norm = (s: string) => s.replace(/_/g, "").toLowerCase();
const names = (ixs: TransactionInstruction[]) =>
  ixs.map((ix) => (ix.programId.equals(program.programId) ? norm(program.coder.instruction.decode(ix.data)!.name) : ix.programId.toBase58()));

async function ctx(over: { pool?: Record<string, unknown>; projects?: Record<string, unknown>[]; balances?: Map<string, bigint> } = {}) {
  const sent: { role: string; ixs: TransactionInstruction[] }[] = [];
  const pool = (await roundTrip<PoolAccount>(program, "pool", poolValue(over.pool))).account;
  const projects = await Promise.all(
    (over.projects ?? [{}]).map(async (p) => ({
      address: Keypair.generate().publicKey,
      account: (await roundTrip<ProjectAccount>(program, "farmProject", projectValue(p))).account,
    })),
  );
  const c: OpsContext = {
    program,
    usdcMint: Keypair.generate().publicKey,
    roles: { issuer: Keypair.generate().publicKey, compliance: Keypair.generate().publicKey, deployer: Keypair.generate().publicKey },
    send: vi.fn(async (role, ixs) => {
      sent.push({ role, ixs });
      return `sig${sent.length}`;
    }),
    loadPool: vi.fn(async () => pool),
    loadProjects: vi.fn(async () => projects),
    tokenBalance: vi.fn(async (a: PublicKey) => over.balances?.get(a.toBase58()) ?? 0n),
    accountExists: vi.fn(async () => false),
    now: () => 1_791_000_000,
  };
  return { c, sent, pool, projects };
}

const validPool = { poolId: "7", crop: "wheat", season: 2027, name: "Wheat 2027", symbol: "WHEAT-2027", yieldBps: 800, premiumBps: 550, closeInMinutes: 10, maturityInMinutes: 20 };

describe("createPool", () => {
  it("validates premium and schedule before anything is sent", () => {
    expect(createPoolInput.safeParse({ ...validPool, premiumBps: 450 }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(createPoolInput.safeParse({ ...validPool, maturityInMinutes: 10 }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(createPoolInput.safeParse({ ...validPool, symbol: "WHEAT-2027X" }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("sends one Issuer transaction with times relative to now and the instrument URI", async () => {
    const { c, sent } = await ctx();
    const r = await createPool(c, createPoolInput.parse(validPool), "https://f2f.example");
    expect(r.pool).toBe(poolPda(program.programId, 7n).toBase58());
    expect(sent).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(sent[0]?.role).toBe("issuer");
    const d = program.coder.instruction.decode(sent[0]!.ixs[0]!.data)!.data as { args: Record<string, unknown> };
    expect(String(d.args.subscriptionCloseTs)).toBe(String(1_791_000_000 + 600));
    expect(String(d.args.maturityTs)).toBe(String(1_791_000_000 + 1200));
    expect(d.args.uri).toBe("https://f2f.example/api/instrument/7");
  });
});

describe("disburseAll", () => {
  it("pays each undisbursed project in its own Issuer transaction", async () => {
    const { c, sent } = await ctx({ pool: { status: { funded: {} } }, projects: [{ disbursed: false }, { disbursed: true }, { disbursed: false }] });
    const sigs = await disburseAll(c, 7n);
    expect(sigs).toEqual(["sig1", "sig2"]);
    expect(sent.every((s) => s.role === "issuer" && names(s.ixs)[0] === "disburse")).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("fundRedemption", () => {
  it("mints the shortfall to the Issuer, then deposits principal plus yield", async () => {
    const { c, sent } = await ctx({ pool: { raisedAmount: new BN(2_000_000_000), yieldBps: 800, maturityTs: new BN(1_790_999_000) } });
    const r = await fundRedemption(c, 7n);
    expect(r.required).toBe(2_160_000_000n);
    expect(r.deposited).toBe(2_160_000_000n);
    expect(r.maturityPassed).toBe(true);
    expect(sent.map((s) => s.role)).toEqual(["deployer", "issuer"]);
    expect(names(sent[1]!.ixs)).toEqual(["fundredemption"]);
  });

  it("only re-checks with amount 0 when the vault already holds enough", async () => {
    const { c, sent, pool } = await ctx({ pool: { raisedAmount: new BN(1_000_000), yieldBps: 800 } });
    vi.mocked(c.tokenBalance).mockImplementation(async (a) => (a.equals(pool.vault) ? 1_080_000n : 0n));
    const r = await fundRedemption(c, 7n);
    expect(r.deposited).toBe(0n);
    expect(sent.map((s) => s.role)).toEqual(["issuer"]);
    const d = program.coder.instruction.decode(sent[0]!.ixs[0]!.data)!.data as { amount: BN };
    expect(d.amount.toString()).toBe("0");
  });
});

describe("investor approval", () => {
  it("approves a wallet once for every pool, without touching any pool", async () => {
    const { c, sent, pool } = await ctx();
    const wallet = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
    await approveInvestor(c, wallet);
    expect(sent[0]?.role).toBe("compliance");
    expect(sent[0]!.ixs[0]!.keys.some((m) => m.pubkey.equals(investorPda(program.programId, wallet)))).toBe(true);
    expect(sent[0]!.ixs[0]!.keys.some((m) => m.pubkey.equals(pool.mint))).toBe(false);
    expect(c.loadPool).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("revokes and freezes the pool account only when it exists", async () => {
    const { c, sent, pool } = await ctx();
    const wallet = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
    const ata = getAssociatedTokenAddressSync(pool.mint, wallet, false, TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID);
    await revokeInvestor(c, 7n, wallet);
    expect(sent[0]!.ixs[0]!.keys.some((m) => m.pubkey.equals(ata))).toBe(false);
    vi.mocked(c.accountExists).mockResolvedValue(true);
    await revokeInvestor(c, 7n, wallet);
    expect(sent[1]!.ixs[0]!.keys.some((m) => m.pubkey.equals(ata))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("helpers", () => {
  it("picks the next pool id", () => {
    expect(nextPoolId([])).toBe("1");
    expect(nextPoolId(["3", "10", "2"])).toBe("11");
  });

  it("names role keys that differ from Config", () => {
    const config = { admin: "A", issuer: "I", scas: "S", compliance: "C", usdcMint: "U" };
    expect(roleMismatches(config, { issuer: "I", scas: "S", compliance: "C" })).toEqual([]);
    expect(roleMismatches(config, { scas: "X" })).toEqual(["SCAS key in this deployment is X, but Config.scas on devnet is S"]);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/test/faucet.test.ts`:
```ts
import { Keypair, SystemProgram } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createCooldown } from "../src/lib/cooldown";
import { SOL_TOPUP_LAMPORTS, runFaucet, type FaucetDeps } from "../src/lib/chain/faucet";

function deps(balance: number, cooldown = createCooldown(60_000, () => 0)): FaucetDeps {
  return {
    connection: { getBalance: vi.fn(async () => balance) },
    usdcMint: Keypair.generate().publicKey,
    deployer: Keypair.generate().publicKey,
    send: vi.fn(async () => "sigF"),
    usdcAmount: 1_000_000_000n,
    cooldown,
  };
}

describe("runFaucet", () => {
  it("tops up SOL when low and mints USDC when asked", async () => {
    const d = deps(0);
    const r = await runFaucet(d, Keypair.generate().publicKey, { usdc: true });
    expect(r).toEqual({ signature: "sigF", solLamports: SOL_TOPUP_LAMPORTS, usdc: "1000000000" });
    const ixs = vi.mocked(d.send).mock.calls[0]![0];
    expect(ixs[0]?.programId.equals(SystemProgram.programId)).toBe(true);
    expect(ixs).toHaveLength(3); // transfer, create ATA, mintTo
  });

  it("does nothing when the wallet already has SOL and no USDC is asked", async () => {
    const d = deps(1_000_000_000);
    expect(await runFaucet(d, Keypair.generate().publicKey, { usdc: false })).toEqual({ signature: null, solLamports: 0, usdc: "0" });
    expect(d.send).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("enforces a per-wallet cooldown with a readable wait time", async () => {
    let t = 0;
    const d = deps(0, createCooldown(60_000, () => t));
    const w = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
    await runFaucet(d, w, { usdc: true });
    t = 15_000;
    await expect(runFaucet(d, w, { usdc: true })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429, message: "Try again in 45 s" });
    t = 60_000;
    await expect(runFaucet(d, w, { usdc: true })).resolves.toMatchObject({ signature: "sigF" });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { adminToken, isAdminToken, passwordMatches } from "../src/lib/admin-auth";

describe("admin auth", () => {
  it("derives a stable token that does not contain the password", () => {
    const t = adminToken("correct horse");
    expect(t).toBe(adminToken("correct horse"));
    expect(t).not.toContain("correct");
    expect(t).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
  });

  it("accepts only the matching token and only when a password is configured", () => {
    expect(isAdminToken("correct horse", adminToken("correct horse"))).toBe(true);
    expect(isAdminToken("correct horse", adminToken("wrong"))).toBe(false);
    expect(isAdminToken(undefined, adminToken("x"))).toBe(false);
    expect(isAdminToken("correct horse", undefined)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("compares passwords without length leaks", () => {
    expect(passwordMatches("correct horse", "correct horse")).toBe(true);
    expect(passwordMatches("correct horse", "c")).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/chain/admin-ops.ts`:
```ts
import type { Program } from "@anchor-lang/core";
import { MAX_EVIDENCE_URI_LEN, poolPda, type F2f } from "@f2f/sdk";
import type { PublicKey, TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { z } from "zod";
import { RequestError } from "../errors";
import { withYield } from "../format";
import type { SettlementRuleName } from "./enums";
import {
  addProjectIx,
  approveInvestorIx,
  closeSubscriptionIx,
  createPoolIx,
  createUsdcAtaIx,
  disburseIx,
  fundRedemptionIx,
  instrumentAta,
  mintUsdcIx,
  openPoolIx,
  recordSettlementIx,
  revokeInvestorIx,
  sweepIx,
  usdcAta,
} from "./ix";
import type { ConfigView, PoolAccount, ProjectAccount } from "./views";

export interface OpsContext {
  program: Program<F2f>;
  usdcMint: PublicKey;
  roles: { readonly issuer: PublicKey; readonly compliance: PublicKey; readonly deployer: PublicKey };
  send(role: "issuer" | "compliance" | "deployer", ixs: TransactionInstruction[]): Promise<string>;
  loadPool(poolId: bigint): Promise<PoolAccount>;
  loadProjects(pool: PublicKey): Promise<{ address: PublicKey; account: ProjectAccount }[]>;
  tokenBalance(account: PublicKey): Promise<bigint>;
  accountExists(account: PublicKey): Promise<boolean>;
  now(): number;
}

export const createPoolInput = z
  .object({
    poolId: z.string().regex(/^\d{1,20}$/, "must be a whole number"),
    crop: z.string().trim().min(1).max(16),
    season: z.number().int().min(2025).max(2100),
    name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(32),
    symbol: z.string().trim().min(1).max(10),
    yieldBps: z.number().int().min(0).max(5_000),
    premiumBps: z.number().int().min(500, "premium must be at least 5%").max(600, "premium must be at most 6%"),
    closeInMinutes: z.number().int().min(1).max(60 * 24 * 30),
    maturityInMinutes: z.number().int().min(2).max(60 * 24 * 365),
  })
  .refine((v) => v.maturityInMinutes > v.closeInMinutes, { message: "maturity must be after subscription close", path: ["maturityInMinutes"] });
export type CreatePoolInput = z.infer<typeof createPoolInput>;

const poolKey = (ctx: OpsContext, poolId: bigint) => poolPda(ctx.program.programId, poolId);

export async function createPool(ctx: OpsContext, input: CreatePoolInput, publicBaseUrl: string) {
  const poolId = BigInt(input.poolId);
  const uri = `${publicBaseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "")}/api/instrument/${input.poolId}`;
  if (uri.length > MAX_EVIDENCE_URI_LEN) throw new RequestError("Instrument URI is too long; shorten PUBLIC_BASE_URL", 422);
  const now = ctx.now();
  const ix = await createPoolIx(ctx.program, {
    issuer: ctx.roles.issuer,
    usdcMint: ctx.usdcMint,
    poolId,
    crop: input.crop,
    season: input.season,
    name: input.name,
    symbol: input.symbol,
    uri,
    yieldBps: input.yieldBps,
    premiumBps: input.premiumBps,
    subscriptionCloseTs: now + input.closeInMinutes * 60,
    maturityTs: now + input.maturityInMinutes * 60,
  });
  const signature = await ctx.send("issuer", [ix]);
  return { signature, poolId: input.poolId, pool: poolKey(ctx, poolId).toBase58() };
}

export async function addProject(ctx: OpsContext, poolId: bigint, project: PublicKey): Promise<string> {
  return ctx.send("issuer", [await addProjectIx(ctx.program, { issuer: ctx.roles.issuer, pool: poolKey(ctx, poolId), project })]);
}

export async function openPool(ctx: OpsContext, poolId: bigint): Promise<string> {
  return ctx.send("issuer", [await openPoolIx(ctx.program, { issuer: ctx.roles.issuer, pool: poolKey(ctx, poolId) })]);
}

export async function closeSubscription(ctx: OpsContext, poolId: bigint): Promise<string> {
  return ctx.send("issuer", [await closeSubscriptionIx(ctx.program, { caller: ctx.roles.issuer, pool: poolKey(ctx, poolId) })]);
}

export async function disburseAll(ctx: OpsContext, poolId: bigint): Promise<string[]> {
  const pool = poolKey(ctx, poolId);
  const account = await ctx.loadPool(poolId);
  const signatures: string[] = [];
  for (const p of await ctx.loadProjects(pool)) {
    if (p.account.disbursed) continue;
    const ix = await disburseIx(ctx.program, {
      issuer: ctx.roles.issuer,
      pool,
      project: p.address,
      producer: p.account.producer,
      usdcMint: ctx.usdcMint,
      vault: account.vault,
    });
    signatures.push(await ctx.send("issuer", [ix]));
  }
  return signatures;
}

export async function recordSettlement(ctx: OpsContext, poolId: bigint, project: PublicKey, rule: SettlementRuleName): Promise<string> {
  return ctx.send("issuer", [await recordSettlementIx(ctx.program, { issuer: ctx.roles.issuer, pool: poolKey(ctx, poolId), project, rule })]);
}

/**
 * Deposits principal plus fixed yield. On devnet the deployer (test-USDC mint authority) first mints any
 * shortfall to the Issuer: a demo convenience that stands in for the Issuer's real repayment.
 * After maturity a deposit of 0 re-checks the redemption gate.
 */
export async function fundRedemption(ctx: OpsContext, poolId: bigint) {
  const pool = poolKey(ctx, poolId);
  const account = await ctx.loadPool(poolId);
  const required = withYield(BigInt(account.raisedAmount.toString()), account.yieldBps);
  const inVault = await ctx.tokenBalance(account.vault);
  const deficit = required > inVault ? required - inVault : 0n;
  const signatures: string[] = [];
  if (deficit > 0n) {
    const issuerUsdc = usdcAta(ctx.usdcMint, ctx.roles.issuer);
    const have = await ctx.tokenBalance(issuerUsdc);
    if (have < deficit) {
      signatures.push(
        await ctx.send("deployer", [
          createUsdcAtaIx(ctx.roles.deployer, ctx.roles.issuer, ctx.usdcMint),
          mintUsdcIx(ctx.usdcMint, issuerUsdc, ctx.roles.deployer, deficit - have),
        ]),
      );
    }
  }
  signatures.push(
    await ctx.send("issuer", [
      await fundRedemptionIx(ctx.program, { issuer: ctx.roles.issuer, pool, usdcMint: ctx.usdcMint, vault: account.vault, amount: deficit }),
    ]),
  );
  return { signatures, deposited: deficit, required, maturityPassed: ctx.now() >= account.maturityTs.toNumber() };
}

export async function sweep(ctx: OpsContext, poolId: bigint): Promise<string> {
  const account = await ctx.loadPool(poolId);
  return ctx.send("issuer", [
    await sweepIx(ctx.program, { issuer: ctx.roles.issuer, pool: poolKey(ctx, poolId), mint: account.mint, usdcMint: ctx.usdcMint, vault: account.vault }),
  ]);
}

/** Platform-level KYC (ADR 0011): sets the wallet's InvestorRecord to approved; subscribe thaws per pool. */
export async function approveInvestor(ctx: OpsContext, wallet: PublicKey): Promise<string> {
  return ctx.send("compliance", [await approveInvestorIx(ctx.program, { compliance: ctx.roles.compliance, investor: wallet })]);
}

/** Clears approval and freezes the investor's account in this pool if they ever subscribed to it. */
export async function revokeInvestor(ctx: OpsContext, poolId: bigint, wallet: PublicKey): Promise<string> {
  const account = await ctx.loadPool(poolId);
  const hasInstrumentAccount = await ctx.accountExists(instrumentAta(account.mint, wallet));
  return ctx.send("compliance", [
    await revokeInvestorIx(ctx.program, { compliance: ctx.roles.compliance, pool: poolKey(ctx, poolId), mint: account.mint, investor: wallet, hasInstrumentAccount }),
  ]);
}

export function nextPoolId(existing: string[]): string {
  return (existing.reduce((m, id) => (BigInt(id) > m ? BigInt(id) : m), 0n) + 1n).toString();
}

const ROLE_LABEL = { issuer: "Issuer", compliance: "Compliance", scas: "SCAS" } as const;

export function roleMismatches(config: ConfigView, keys: Partial<Record<"issuer" | "compliance" | "scas", string>>): string[] {
  return (Object.keys(ROLE_LABEL) as (keyof typeof ROLE_LABEL)[])
    .filter((r) => keys[r] !== undefined && keys[r] !== config[r])
    .map((r) => `${ROLE_LABEL[r]} key in this deployment is ${keys[r]}, but Config.${r} on devnet is ${config[r]}`);
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/cooldown.ts`:
```ts
export interface Cooldown {
  /** Seconds left before `key` may act again; 0 when allowed. */
  remaining(key: string): number;
  mark(key: string): void;
}

export function createCooldown(ms: number, now: () => number = Date.now): Cooldown {
  const last = new Map<string, number>();
  return {
    remaining(key) {
      const at = last.get(key);
      if (at === undefined) return 0;
      const left = at + ms - now();
      return left > 0 ? Math.ceil(left / 1000) : 0;
    },
    mark(key) {
      last.set(key, now());
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/chain/faucet.ts`:
```ts
import { SystemProgram, type Connection, type PublicKey, type TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import type { Cooldown } from "../cooldown";
import { RequestError } from "../errors";
import { createUsdcAtaIx, mintUsdcIx, usdcAta } from "./ix";

export const SOL_MIN_LAMPORTS = 20_000_000;
export const SOL_TOPUP_LAMPORTS = 50_000_000;

export interface FaucetDeps {
  connection: Pick<Connection, "getBalance">;
  usdcMint: PublicKey;
  /** Test-USDC mint authority; also pays the SOL top-up. */
  deployer: PublicKey;
  send(ixs: TransactionInstruction[]): Promise<string>;
  usdcAmount: bigint;
  cooldown: Cooldown;
}

export async function runFaucet(deps: FaucetDeps, wallet: PublicKey, want: { usdc: boolean }) {
  const wait = deps.cooldown.remaining(wallet.toBase58());
  if (wait > 0) throw new RequestError(`Try again in ${wait} s`, 429);
  const ixs: TransactionInstruction[] = [];
  let solLamports = 0;
  if ((await deps.connection.getBalance(wallet, "confirmed")) < SOL_MIN_LAMPORTS) {
    ixs.push(SystemProgram.transfer({ fromPubkey: deps.deployer, toPubkey: wallet, lamports: SOL_TOPUP_LAMPORTS }));
    solLamports = SOL_TOPUP_LAMPORTS;
  }
  if (want.usdc) {
    ixs.push(createUsdcAtaIx(deps.deployer, wallet, deps.usdcMint), mintUsdcIx(deps.usdcMint, usdcAta(deps.usdcMint, wallet), deps.deployer, deps.usdcAmount));
  }
  if (ixs.length === 0) return { signature: null, solLamports: 0, usdc: "0" };
  const signature = await deps.send(ixs);
  deps.cooldown.mark(wallet.toBase58());
  return { signature, solLamports, usdc: want.usdc ? deps.usdcAmount.toString() : "0" };
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/admin-auth.ts`:
```ts
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";

export const ADMIN_COOKIE = "f2f_admin";

/** Session cookie value: an HMAC of a fixed label keyed by the password; rotating the password logs everyone out. */
export const adminToken = (password: string) => createHmac("sha256", password).update("f2f-admin-session-v1").digest("hex");

const digest = (s: string) => createHmac("sha256", "f2f-admin-compare").update(s).digest();

export const passwordMatches = (expected: string, given: string) => timingSafeEqual(digest(expected), digest(given));

export function isAdminToken(password: string | undefined, token: string | undefined): boolean {
  if (!password || !token) return false;
  return timingSafeEqual(digest(adminToken(password)), digest(token));
}
```

Append to `apps/web/src/lib/server/context.ts` (imports to the top):
```ts
import { investorPda, poolPda } from "@f2f/sdk";
import type { OpsContext } from "../chain/admin-ops";
import { projectsInPool } from "../chain/filters";
import type { FaucetDeps } from "../chain/faucet";
import { createCooldown } from "../cooldown";
import { RequestError } from "../errors";

export function opsContext(): OpsContext {
  const program = getBuildProgram();
  const connection = getConnection();
  return {
    program,
    usdcMint: getUsdcMint(),
    roles: {
      get issuer() { return roleKeypair("issuer").publicKey; },
      get compliance() { return roleKeypair("compliance").publicKey; },
      get deployer() { return roleKeypair("deployer").publicKey; },
    },
    send: (role, ixs) => sendAs(role, ixs),
    loadPool: async (poolId) => {
      const a = await program.account.pool.fetchNullable(poolPda(program.programId, poolId));
      if (!a) throw new RequestError(`Pool ${poolId} does not exist`, 404);
      return a;
    },
    loadProjects: async (pool) =>
      (await program.account.farmProject.all([projectsInPool(pool)])).map((r) => ({ address: r.publicKey, account: r.account })),
    tokenBalance: async (account) => {
      try {
        return BigInt((await connection.getTokenAccountBalance(account, "confirmed")).value.amount);
      } catch {
        return 0n;
      }
    },
    accountExists: async (account) => (await connection.getAccountInfo(account, "confirmed")) !== null,
    now: () => Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
  };
}

const faucetCooldown = createCooldown(60_000);
export function faucetDeps(): FaucetDeps {
  return {
    connection: getConnection(),
    usdcMint: getUsdcMint(),
    deployer: roleKeypair("deployer").publicKey,
    send: (ixs) => sendAs("deployer", ixs),
    usdcAmount: BigInt(serverEnv().FAUCET_USDC) * 1_000_000n,
    cooldown: faucetCooldown,
  };
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/api/kyc/route.ts`:
```ts
import { PublicKey } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { approveInvestor } from "@/lib/chain/admin-ops";
import { explainChainError } from "@/lib/chain/explain";
import { ChainTxError } from "@/lib/chain/send";
import { RequestError } from "@/lib/errors";
import { kycFormSchema } from "@/lib/kyc";
import { opsContext } from "@/lib/server/context";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const parsed = kycFormSchema.safeParse(await req.json().catch(() => null));
  if (!parsed.success) {
    // Field paths only: never echo submitted values.
    const detail = parsed.error.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join(".")}: ${i.message}`).join("; ");
    return Response.json({ ok: false, error: { code: "BadRequest", title: "Check the form", detail, action: "none" } }, { status: 400 });
  }
  try {
    const signature = await approveInvestor(opsContext(), new PublicKey(parsed.data.wallet));
    return Response.json({ ok: true, signature });
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof RequestError) {
      return Response.json({ ok: false, error: { code: "BadRequest", title: e.message, detail: "", action: "none" } }, { status: e.status });
    }
    const logs = e instanceof ChainTxError ? e.logs : [];
    return Response.json({ ok: false, error: explainChainError({ logs, error: e }), logs }, { status: 422 });
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/api/faucet/route.ts`:
```ts
import { PublicKey } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { z } from "zod";
import { runFaucet } from "@/lib/chain/faucet";
import { RequestError } from "@/lib/errors";
import { faucetDeps } from "@/lib/server/context";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
const Body = z.object({ wallet: z.string().regex(/^[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,44}$/), usdc: z.boolean() });

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const b = Body.safeParse(await req.json().catch(() => null));
  if (!b.success) return Response.json({ ok: false, error: { code: "BadRequest", title: "Connect a wallet first", detail: "", action: "none" } }, { status: 400 });
  try {
    return Response.json({ ok: true, ...(await runFaucet(faucetDeps(), new PublicKey(b.data.wallet), { usdc: b.data.usdc })) });
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof RequestError) return Response.json({ ok: false, error: { code: "Cooldown", title: e.message, detail: "", action: "none" } }, { status: e.status });
    return Response.json({ ok: false, error: { code: "FaucetFailed", title: "The faucet could not send funds", detail: "Try again in a minute.", action: "retry" } }, { status: 502 });
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/admin/actions.ts`:
```ts
"use server";

import * as Sentry from "@sentry/nextjs";
import { PublicKey } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import type { AttestationKindName } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { ADMIN_COOKIE, adminToken, isAdminToken, passwordMatches } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import {
  addProject,
  approveInvestor,
  closeSubscription,
  createPool,
  createPoolInput,
  disburseAll,
  fundRedemption,
  openPool,
  recordSettlement,
  revokeInvestor,
  sweep,
} from "@/lib/chain/admin-ops";
import { SETTLEMENT_RULES, type SettlementRuleName } from "@/lib/chain/enums";
import { explainChainError, type ExplainedError } from "@/lib/chain/explain";
import { ChainTxError } from "@/lib/chain/send";
import { RequestError } from "@/lib/errors";
import { formatUsd } from "@/lib/format";
import { attestStage, verifyAllFields } from "@/lib/scas-stub";
import { getReader, invalidateReads, opsContext, stubDeps } from "@/lib/server/context";
import { serverEnv } from "@/lib/server/env";

export type ActionResult = { ok: true; message: string; signatures: string[] } | { ok: false; error: ExplainedError; logs: string[] };

async function requireAdmin(): Promise<void> {
  const jar = await cookies();
  if (!isAdminToken(serverEnv().ADMIN_PASSWORD, jar.get(ADMIN_COOKIE)?.value)) {
    throw new RequestError("Your demo console session expired. Sign in again.", 401);
  }
}

async function run(fn: () => Promise<{ message: string; signatures: string[] }>): Promise<ActionResult> {
  try {
    await requireAdmin();
    return { ok: true, ...(await fn()) };
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof RequestError) return { ok: false, error: { code: "BadRequest", title: e.message, detail: "", action: "none" }, logs: [] };
    if (e instanceof ChainTxError) return { ok: false, error: explainChainError({ logs: e.logs, error: e }), logs: e.logs };
    Sentry.captureException(e);
    return { ok: false, error: explainChainError({ error: e }), logs: [] };
  } finally {
    invalidateReads();
  }
}

const id = (poolId: string) => {
  if (!/^\d{1,20}$/.test(poolId)) throw new RequestError("Invalid pool id", 400);
  return BigInt(poolId);
};

export async function loginAction(_prev: { error: string | null }, form: FormData): Promise<{ error: string | null }> {
  const password = serverEnv().ADMIN_PASSWORD;
  if (!password) return { error: "The demo console is disabled: ADMIN_PASSWORD is not set." };
  if (!passwordMatches(password, String(form.get("password") ?? ""))) return { error: "Wrong password." };
  (await cookies()).set(ADMIN_COOKIE, adminToken(password), {
    httpOnly: true,
    sameSite: "strict",
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    path: "/",
    maxAge: 12 * 3600,
  });
  redirect("/admin");
}

export async function logoutAction(): Promise<void> {
  (await cookies()).delete(ADMIN_COOKIE);
  redirect("/admin/login");
}

export const createPoolAction = async (input: unknown) =>
  run(async () => {
    const parsed = createPoolInput.safeParse(input);
    if (!parsed.success) throw new RequestError(parsed.error.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join(".")}: ${i.message}`).join("; "), 400);
    const r = await createPool(opsContext(), parsed.data, serverEnv().PUBLIC_BASE_URL);
    return { message: `Pool ${r.poolId} created as Draft`, signatures: [r.signature] };
  });

export const addProjectAction = async (poolId: string, project: string) =>
  run(async () => ({ message: "Project added to the pool", signatures: [await addProject(opsContext(), id(poolId), new PublicKey(project))] }));

export const openPoolAction = async (poolId: string) =>
  run(async () => ({ message: "Pool is open for subscription", signatures: [await openPool(opsContext(), id(poolId))] }));

export const closeSubscriptionAction = async (poolId: string) =>
  run(async () => ({ message: "Subscription closed", signatures: [await closeSubscription(opsContext(), id(poolId))] }));

export const disburseAllAction = async (poolId: string) =>
  run(async () => {
    const signatures = await disburseAll(opsContext(), id(poolId));
    return { message: signatures.length ? `Paid ${signatures.length} project(s)` : "Every project was already paid", signatures };
  });

export const verifyAllFieldsAction = async () =>
  run(async () => {
    const r = await verifyAllFields(stubDeps(), await getReader().unpooledProjects());
    if (r.failures.length) throw new RequestError(`${r.results.length} verified, ${r.failures.length} failed: ${r.failures[0]?.error}`, 502);
    return { message: r.results.length ? `Verified ${r.results.length} field(s) with the SCAS stub` : "No unverified fields", signatures: r.results.map((x) => x.signature) };
  });

const STAGES = ["Sowing", "Vegetation", "Harvest"] as const;
export const attestStageAction = async (poolId: string, kind: (typeof STAGES)[number]) =>
  run(async () => {
    if (!STAGES.includes(kind)) throw new RequestError("Unknown season stage", 400);
    const detail = await getReader().getPool(poolId);
    if (!detail) throw new RequestError(`Pool ${poolId} does not exist`, 404);
    const r = await attestStage(stubDeps(), detail.projects, kind as Exclude<AttestationKindName, "FieldVerified">);
    if (r.failures.length) throw new RequestError(`${r.results.length} attested, ${r.failures.length} failed: ${r.failures[0]?.error}`, 502);
    return { message: `${kind} attested for ${r.results.length} field(s)`, signatures: r.results.map((x) => x.signature) };
  });

export const recordSettlementAction = async (poolId: string, project: string, rule: string) =>
  run(async () => {
    if (!(SETTLEMENT_RULES as readonly string[]).includes(rule)) throw new RequestError("Unknown settlement rule", 400);
    return {
      message: "Settlement recorded",
      signatures: [await recordSettlement(opsContext(), id(poolId), new PublicKey(project), rule as SettlementRuleName)],
    };
  });

export const fundRedemptionAction = async (poolId: string) =>
  run(async () => {
    const r = await fundRedemption(opsContext(), id(poolId));
    const when = r.maturityPassed ? "Redemption check ran." : "Maturity has not passed yet; run this again after maturity to open redemption.";
    return { message: `Deposited ${formatUsd(r.deposited)} of ${formatUsd(r.required)}. ${when}`, signatures: r.signatures };
  });

export const sweepAction = async (poolId: string) =>
  run(async () => ({ message: "Vault remainder returned to the Issuer", signatures: [await sweep(opsContext(), id(poolId))] }));

export const approveInvestorAction = async (wallet: string) =>
  run(async () => ({ message: "Investor approved for every pool; accounts thaw on first subscription", signatures: [await approveInvestor(opsContext(), new PublicKey(wallet))] }));

export const revokeInvestorAction = async (poolId: string, wallet: string) =>
  run(async () => ({ message: "Investor revoked; account frozen", signatures: [await revokeInvestor(opsContext(), id(poolId), new PublicKey(wallet))] }));
```
`redirect()` throws a Next control-flow error; it is called outside `run`, so the `catch` in `run` never swallows it.

- Produces:
  - `tx-client.ts`: `type TxPhase = { phase: "idle" } | { phase: "building" } | { phase: "signing" } | { phase: "confirming"; signature: string } | { phase: "success"; signature: string; meta?: Record<string, string> } | { phase: "error"; error: ExplainedError; logs: string[]; signature?: string }`, `interface TxSigner { publicKey: string; signAndSend(tx: VersionedTransaction): Promise<string> }`, `type PostJson = (url: string, body: unknown) => Promise<unknown>`, `postJson: PostJson`, `base64ToBytes(b64: string): Uint8Array`, `executeTx(deps: { postJson: PostJson; signer: TxSigner }, action: TxAction, body: Record<string, unknown>, onPhase: (p: TxPhase) => void): Promise<TxPhase>`.
  - `analytics.ts`: `type AnalyticsEvent = "tx_succeeded" | "tx_failed" | "kyc_approved" | "faucet_used" | "fields_uploaded" | "farmer_login"`, `track(event, props?)` (no-op until PostHog is initialized in Task 17).
  - `useTransaction(action: TxAction, signer: TxSigner | null, opts?: { onSuccess?(p: { signature: string; meta?: Record<string, string> }): void }): { state: TxPhase; busy: boolean; run(body: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<TxPhase>; reset(): void }`.
  - `TxStatus({ state, onAction?, successLabel? }: { state: TxPhase; onAction?: (a: ErrorAction) => void; successLabel?: string })`.
  - `useJson<T>(url: string | null): { data: T | null; error: string | null; loading: boolean; reload(): void }`.
  - `WalletProviders({ children })`, `ConnectButton()`, `useAdapterSigner(): TxSigner | null`.
  - `interface FarmerSession { mode: "privy" | "adapter"; ready: boolean; authenticated: boolean; email: string | null; signer: TxSigner | null; login(): void; logout(): void }`, `FarmerSessionProvider({ children })`, `useFarmerSession(): FarmerSession`. Privy is used when `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIVY_APP_ID` is set (it is, in Infisical `dev`); otherwise the wallet adapter.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the Privy dependencies**

In `apps/web/package.json` `dependencies` add `"@privy-io/react-auth": "3.47.0"` and `"@solana/kit": "8.4.0"`, then run `pnpm install`. pnpm auto-installs Privy's required peers (`@solana-program/system`, `@solana-program/token`, `@solana-program/memo`). Warnings about optional EVM peers (`permissionless`, `@abstract-foundation/agw-client`, `@farcaster/mini-app-solana`) are expected; ignore them.

`PRIVY_APP_SECRET` (in Infisical) is not used: the server never needs to verify a Privy session, because farmers prove control of their wallet by signing the transaction. Do not add it to Railway.

`apps/web/test/tx-client.test.ts`:
```ts
import { Keypair, SystemProgram, TransactionMessage, VersionedTransaction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { executeTx, type TxPhase, type TxSigner } from "../src/lib/tx-client";

function unsignedTxBase64(): string {
  const payer = Keypair.generate().publicKey;
  const msg = new TransactionMessage({
    payerKey: payer,
    recentBlockhash: Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58(),
    instructions: [SystemProgram.transfer({ fromPubkey: payer, toPubkey: Keypair.generate().publicKey, lamports: 1 })],
  }).compileToV0Message();
  return Buffer.from(new VersionedTransaction(msg).serialize()).toString("base64");
}

const signer = (impl?: () => Promise<string>): TxSigner => ({
  publicKey: Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58(),
  signAndSend: vi.fn(impl ?? (async () => "5sig")),
});

describe("executeTx", () => {
  it("builds, signs, confirms and reports each phase", async () => {
    const postJson = vi.fn(async (url: string) =>
      url === "/api/tx/subscribe"
        ? { ok: true, tx: unsignedTxBase64(), blockhash: "b", lastValidBlockHeight: 1, meta: { poolId: "7" } }
        : { ok: true },
    );
    const phases: TxPhase["phase"][] = [];
    const s = signer();
    const final = await executeTx({ postJson, signer: s }, "subscribe", { poolId: "7", amount: "1" }, (p) => phases.push(p.phase));
    expect(phases).toEqual(["building", "signing", "confirming"]);
    expect(final).toEqual({ phase: "success", signature: "5sig", meta: { poolId: "7" } });
    expect(postJson).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/api/tx/subscribe", { poolId: "7", amount: "1", signer: s.publicKey });
    expect(postJson).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("/api/tx/confirm", { signature: "5sig", blockhash: "b", lastValidBlockHeight: 1 });
  });

  it("stops before the wallet when simulation rejects", async () => {
    const error = { code: "KycRequired", title: "This wallet hasn't passed KYC", detail: "", action: "kyc" };
    const s = signer();
    const final = await executeTx({ postJson: async () => ({ ok: false, error, logs: ["l1"] }), signer: s }, "subscribe", {}, () => {});
    expect(final).toEqual({ phase: "error", error, logs: ["l1"] });
    expect(s.signAndSend).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("explains a cancelled signature", async () => {
    const final = await executeTx(
      { postJson: async () => ({ ok: true, tx: unsignedTxBase64(), blockhash: "b", lastValidBlockHeight: 1 }), signer: signer(async () => Promise.reject(new Error("User rejected the request."))) },
      "redeem",
      {},
      () => {},
    );
    expect(final).toMatchObject({ phase: "error", error: { code: "UserRejected" } });
  });

  it("keeps the signature when the transaction fails on-chain", async () => {
    const error = { code: "PoolNotOpen", title: "x", detail: "", action: "none" };
    const postJson = vi.fn(async (url: string) =>
      url === "/api/tx/confirm" ? { ok: false, error, logs: ["l"], signature: "5sig" } : { ok: true, tx: unsignedTxBase64(), blockhash: "b", lastValidBlockHeight: 1 },
    );
    expect(await executeTx({ postJson, signer: signer() }, "subscribe", {}, () => {})).toEqual({ phase: "error", error, logs: ["l"], signature: "5sig" });
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/tx-client.ts`:
```ts
import { VersionedTransaction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { explainChainError, extractLogs, type ExplainedError } from "./chain/explain";
import type { TxAction } from "./chain/user-tx";

export type TxPhase =
  | { phase: "idle" }
  | { phase: "building" }
  | { phase: "signing" }
  | { phase: "confirming"; signature: string }
  | { phase: "success"; signature: string; meta?: Record<string, string> }
  | { phase: "error"; error: ExplainedError; logs: string[]; signature?: string };

export interface TxSigner {
  publicKey: string;
  /** Signs and submits; returns the base58 signature. */
  signAndSend(tx: VersionedTransaction): Promise<string>;
}

export type PostJson = (url: string, body: unknown) => Promise<unknown>;

const NETWORK_ERROR: ExplainedError = { code: "Network", title: "Could not reach the server", detail: "Check your connection and try again.", action: "retry" };

export const postJson: PostJson = async (url, body) => {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
    return await res.json();
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, error: NETWORK_ERROR, logs: [] };
  }
};

export function base64ToBytes(b64: string): Uint8Array {
  const bin = atob(b64);
  const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}

type Built = { ok: true; tx: string; blockhash: string; lastValidBlockHeight: number; meta?: Record<string, string> } | { ok: false; error: ExplainedError; logs: string[] };
type Confirmed = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: ExplainedError; logs: string[]; signature?: string };

export async function executeTx(
  deps: { postJson: PostJson; signer: TxSigner },
  action: TxAction,
  body: Record<string, unknown>,
  onPhase: (p: TxPhase) => void,
): Promise<TxPhase> {
  onPhase({ phase: "building" });
  const built = (await deps.postJson(`/api/tx/${action}`, { ...body, signer: deps.signer.publicKey })) as Built;
  if (!built.ok) return { phase: "error", error: built.error ?? NETWORK_ERROR, logs: built.logs ?? [] };

  onPhase({ phase: "signing" });
  let signature: string;
  try {
    signature = await deps.signer.signAndSend(VersionedTransaction.deserialize(base64ToBytes(built.tx)));
  } catch (e) {
    const logs = extractLogs(e);
    return { phase: "error", error: explainChainError({ logs, error: e }), logs };
  }

  onPhase({ phase: "confirming", signature });
  const confirmed = (await deps.postJson("/api/tx/confirm", {
    signature,
    blockhash: built.blockhash,
    lastValidBlockHeight: built.lastValidBlockHeight,
  })) as Confirmed;
  if (!confirmed.ok) return { phase: "error", error: confirmed.error, logs: confirmed.logs ?? [], signature };
  return built.meta ? { phase: "success", signature, meta: built.meta } : { phase: "success", signature };
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/analytics.ts`:
```ts
import posthog from "posthog-js";

export type AnalyticsEvent = "tx_succeeded" | "tx_failed" | "kyc_approved" | "faucet_used" | "fields_uploaded" | "farmer_login";

/** Product events only: never wallet addresses, emails, names or amounts tied to a person. */
export function track(event: AnalyticsEvent, props: Record<string, string | number | boolean> = {}): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || !posthog.__loaded) return;
  posthog.capture(event, props);
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/tx/use-transaction.ts`:
```ts
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { track } from "../../lib/analytics";
import type { TxAction } from "../../lib/chain/user-tx";
import { executeTx, postJson, type TxPhase, type TxSigner } from "../../lib/tx-client";

export function useTransaction(
  action: TxAction,
  signer: TxSigner | null,
  opts: { onSuccess?(p: { signature: string; meta?: Record<string, string> }): void } = {},
) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<TxPhase>({ phase: "idle" });
  const router = useRouter();
  const { onSuccess } = opts;

  const run = useCallback(
    async (body: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<TxPhase> => {
      if (!signer) {
        const s: TxPhase = { phase: "error", error: { code: "NoWallet", title: "Connect a wallet first", detail: "", action: "none" }, logs: [] };
        setState(s);
        return s;
      }
      const final = await executeTx({ postJson, signer }, action, body, setState);
      setState(final);
      if (final.phase === "success") {
        track("tx_succeeded", { action });
        onSuccess?.({ signature: final.signature, meta: final.meta });
        router.refresh();
      } else if (final.phase === "error") {
        track("tx_failed", { action, code: final.error.code });
      }
      return final;
    },
    [action, signer, onSuccess, router],
  );

  const busy = state.phase === "building" || state.phase === "signing" || state.phase === "confirming";
  return { state, busy, run, reset: () => setState({ phase: "idle" }) };
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/tx/tx-status.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { CircleCheck, Loader2, TriangleAlert } from "lucide-react";
import type { ErrorAction } from "../../lib/chain/explain";
import type { TxPhase } from "../../lib/tx-client";
import { ExplorerLink } from "../explorer-link";

const ACTION_LABEL: Partial<Record<ErrorAction, string>> = { kyc: "Complete KYC", faucet: "Get test funds", retry: "Try again" };
const PENDING: Record<"building" | "signing" | "confirming", string> = {
  building: "Preparing transaction",
  signing: "Approve in your wallet",
  confirming: "Confirming on devnet",
};

export function TxStatus({ state, onAction, successLabel = "Confirmed on devnet" }: { state: TxPhase; onAction?: (a: ErrorAction) => void; successLabel?: string }) {
  if (state.phase === "idle") return null;

  if (state.phase === "building" || state.phase === "signing" || state.phase === "confirming") {
    return (
      <div role="status" aria-live="polite" className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        <Loader2 aria-hidden strokeWidth={1.5} className="size-4 animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none" />
        <span>{PENDING[state.phase]}</span>
        {state.phase === "confirming" ? <ExplorerLink kind="tx" value={state.signature} /> : null}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (state.phase === "success") {
    return (
      <div role="status" aria-live="polite" className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 text-sm text-success">
        <CircleCheck aria-hidden strokeWidth={1.5} className="size-4" />
        <span className="font-medium">{successLabel}</span>
        <ExplorerLink kind="tx" value={state.signature} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const label = ACTION_LABEL[state.error.action];
  return (
    <div role="alert" className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border border-destructive bg-card p-4 text-sm">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
        <TriangleAlert aria-hidden strokeWidth={1.5} className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0 text-destructive" />
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <p className="font-medium text-foreground">{state.error.title}</p>
          {state.error.detail ? <p className="text-muted-foreground">{state.error.detail}</p> : null}
          {state.signature ? (
            <p className="text-muted-foreground">
              Failed transaction: <ExplorerLink kind="tx" value={state.signature} />
            </p>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>
      {label && onAction ? (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onAction(state.error.action)}
          className="self-start rounded-md bg-primary px-3 py-2 font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover active:scale-[0.98]"
        >
          {label}
        </button>
      ) : null}
      {state.logs.length ? (
        <details className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <summary className="cursor-pointer select-none">Program logs</summary>
          <pre className="mt-2 max-h-48 overflow-auto rounded-md bg-muted p-2 font-mono whitespace-pre-wrap">{state.logs.join("\n")}</pre>
        </details>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/use-json.ts`:
```ts
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";

export function useJson<T>(url: string | null) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<T | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [nonce, setNonce] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!url) {
      setData(null);
      return;
    }
    let cancelled = false;
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    fetch(url)
      .then(async (res) => {
        const body = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error((body as { error?: string }).error ?? `Request failed (${res.status})`);
        if (!cancelled) setData(body as T);
      })
      .catch((e: unknown) => !cancelled && setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Request failed"))
      .finally(() => !cancelled && setLoading(false));
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [url, nonce]);

  return { data, error, loading, reload: useCallback(() => setNonce((n) => n + 1), []) };
}
```

```ts
"use client";

import { useConnection, useWallet } from "@solana/wallet-adapter-react";
import { useMemo } from "react";
import type { TxSigner } from "../../lib/tx-client";

export function useAdapterSigner(): TxSigner | null {
  const { publicKey, sendTransaction } = useWallet();
  const { connection } = useConnection();
  return useMemo(
    () => (publicKey ? { publicKey: publicKey.toBase58(), signAndSend: (tx) => sendTransaction(tx, connection, { skipPreflight: false }) } : null),
    [publicKey, sendTransaction, connection],
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/wallet/farmer-session.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useWallet } from "@solana/wallet-adapter-react";
import { useWalletModal } from "@solana/wallet-adapter-react-ui";
import dynamic from "next/dynamic";
import { createContext, useContext, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { publicEnv } from "../../lib/public-env";
import type { TxSigner } from "../../lib/tx-client";
import { useAdapterSigner } from "./use-adapter-signer";

export interface FarmerSession {
  mode: "privy" | "adapter";
  ready: boolean;
  authenticated: boolean;
  email: string | null;
  signer: TxSigner | null;
  login(): void;
  logout(): void;
}

const Ctx = createContext<FarmerSession | null>(null);
export const FarmerSessionContext = Ctx;

export function useFarmerSession(): FarmerSession {
  const s = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!s) throw new Error("useFarmerSession must be used inside FarmerSessionProvider");
  return s;
}

function AdapterSession({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { connected, disconnect } = useWallet();
  const { setVisible } = useWalletModal();
  const signer = useAdapterSigner();
  return (
    <Ctx.Provider value={{ mode: "adapter", ready: true, authenticated: connected, email: null, signer, login: () => setVisible(true), logout: () => void disconnect() }}>
      {children}
    </Ctx.Provider>
  );
}

// Privy's bundle loads only on the farmer page, and only when an app id is configured.
const PrivySession = dynamic(() => import("./privy-farmer"), { ssr: false });

export function FarmerSessionProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const appId = publicEnv.NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIVY_APP_ID;
  if (!appId) return <AdapterSession>{children}</AdapterSession>;
  return (
    <PrivySession appId={appId} rpcUrl={publicEnv.NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL}>
      {children}
    </PrivySession>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/wallet/privy-farmer.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { PrivyProvider, usePrivy } from "@privy-io/react-auth";
import { useSignAndSendTransaction, useWallets } from "@privy-io/react-auth/solana";
import { createSolanaRpc, createSolanaRpcSubscriptions } from "@solana/kit";
import bs58 from "bs58";
import { useMemo, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { track } from "../../lib/analytics";
import type { TxSigner } from "../../lib/tx-client";
import { FarmerSessionContext } from "./farmer-session";

function Bridge({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { ready, authenticated, login, logout, user } = usePrivy();
  const { wallets } = useWallets();
  const { signAndSendTransaction } = useSignAndSendTransaction();
  const wallet = wallets.find((w) => w.standardWallet.name === "Privy") ?? wallets[0];

  const signer = useMemo<TxSigner | null>(
    () =>
      wallet
        ? {
            publicKey: wallet.address,
            async signAndSend(tx) {
              const { signature } = await signAndSendTransaction({ transaction: tx.serialize(), wallet, chain: "solana:devnet" });
              return bs58.encode(signature);
            },
          }
        : null,
    [wallet, signAndSendTransaction],
  );

  return (
    <FarmerSessionContext.Provider
      value={{
        mode: "privy",
        ready,
        authenticated,
        email: user?.email?.address ?? null,
        signer,
        login: () => {
          track("farmer_login");
          login();
        },
        logout: () => void logout(),
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </FarmerSessionContext.Provider>
  );
}

export default function PrivyFarmer({ appId, rpcUrl, children }: { appId: string; rpcUrl: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  const wsUrl = rpcUrl.replace(/^http/, "ws");
  return (
    <PrivyProvider
      appId={appId}
      config={{
        loginMethods: ["email"],
        appearance: { theme: "light", accentColor: "#142d23", walletChainType: "solana-only" },
        embeddedWallets: { solana: { createOnLogin: "users-without-wallets" } },
        solana: {
          rpcs: {
            "solana:devnet": { rpc: createSolanaRpc(rpcUrl), rpcSubscriptions: createSolanaRpcSubscriptions(wsUrl) },
          },
        },
      }}
    >
      <Bridge>{children}</Bridge>
    </PrivyProvider>
  );
}
```
Privy's types move between minor versions. If tsc rejects `chain` on `signAndSendTransaction` or the `embeddedWallets.solana` shape, open `node_modules/@privy-io/react-auth/dist/dts/solana.d.ts` and `index.d.ts`, keep the behavior (email login, Solana embedded wallet created on login, devnet RPC, sign-and-send on devnet) and adapt the field names to the shipped types. `apps/web/src/lib/landing-stats.ts`:
```ts
import type { PoolView } from "./chain/views";

export interface LandingStats {
  openPools: number;
  totalRaised: string;
  featured: PoolView | null;
}

export function landingStats(pools: PoolView[]): LandingStats {
  const open = pools.filter((p) => p.status === "Open").sort((a, b) => b.subscriptionCloseTs - a.subscriptionCloseTs);
  const newest = [...pools].sort((a, b) => (BigInt(b.poolId) > BigInt(a.poolId) ? 1 : -1))[0] ?? null;
  return {
    openPools: open.length,
    totalRaised: pools.reduce((s, p) => s + BigInt(p.raisedAmount), 0n).toString(),
    featured: open[0] ?? newest,
  };
}
```

```tsx
export function StatTile({ label, value, hint }: { label: string; value: string; hint?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 border-l border-border pl-4">
      <span className="text-xs tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">{label}</span>
      <span className="tabular text-2xl font-semibold">{value}</span>
      {hint ? <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{hint}</span> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { BadgeCheck, Banknote, FileSignature, Landmark, Map, ShieldCheck, Sprout } from "lucide-react";

const STEPS = [
  { Icon: Map, title: "Fields registered", body: "The farmer uploads field boundaries and asks for financing." },
  { Icon: BadgeCheck, title: "Fields verified", body: "SCAS attests each field on Solana. Evidence is public and hash-linked." },
  { Icon: FileSignature, title: "Pool issued", body: "The Issuer packages verified farms into one Instrument, for example WHEAT-2027." },
  { Icon: ShieldCheck, title: "Investors approved", body: "Instrument accounts start frozen. KYC thaws them." },
  { Icon: Banknote, title: "Pool funded", body: "Investors pay USDC and receive the Instrument in the same transaction." },
  { Icon: Sprout, title: "Season attested", body: "Sowing, vegetation and harvest are attested as the season runs." },
  { Icon: Landmark, title: "Pool settled", body: "Investors redeem principal plus the fixed yield after maturity." },
];

export function DealPath() {
  return (
    <ol className="grid gap-px overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-border bg-border sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
      {STEPS.map(({ Icon, title, body }, i) => (
        <li key={title} className="flex flex-col gap-2 bg-card p-6">
          <span className="tabular text-xs text-muted-foreground">Step {i + 1}</span>
          <Icon aria-hidden strokeWidth={1.5} className="size-5 text-primary" />
          <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">{title}</h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{body}</p>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { DealPath } from "@/components/landing/deal-path";
import { StatTile } from "@/components/landing/stat-tile";
import { PoolSummaryCard } from "@/components/pool/pool-summary-card";
import { formatUsd, formatYield } from "@/lib/format";
import { landingStats } from "@/lib/landing-stats";
import { getReader } from "@/lib/server/context";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export default async function Home() {
  const stats = await getReader()
    .listPools()
    .then(landingStats)
    .catch(() => null);

  return (
    <div className="-mt-10 flex flex-col">
      <section className="grid items-center gap-12 py-32 lg:grid-cols-[1.1fr_1fr]">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
          <h1 className="text-[40px] font-semibold sm:text-[56px]">Finance next season&apos;s harvest today.</h1>
          <p className="max-w-xl text-base text-muted-foreground">
            Farmers raise working capital against 30% of their expected harvest. Fields are verified and attested on Solana.
            Investors earn a fixed yield in USDC, paid at maturity.
          </p>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            <Link href="/pools" className="inline-flex h-11 items-center rounded-md bg-primary px-5 font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover active:scale-[0.98]">
              View open pools
            </Link>
            <Link href="/farmer" className="inline-flex h-11 items-center rounded-md px-5 font-medium text-foreground hover:bg-muted">
              Register fields
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="dark-band rounded-lg bg-ink p-6 text-on-dark">
          {stats?.featured ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <p className="text-sm text-on-dark">Live on devnet</p>
              <div className="text-foreground">
                <PoolSummaryCard pool={stats.featured} />
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-sm">The first pool opens soon. Every step will be visible on Solana Explorer.</p>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="grid gap-8 border-y border-border py-16 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <StatTile label="Open pools" value={stats ? String(stats.openPools) : "Unavailable"} />
        <StatTile label="Raised on devnet" value={stats ? formatUsd(stats.totalRaised, { cents: false }) : "Unavailable"} hint="Test USDC" />
        <StatTile label="Featured return" value={stats?.featured ? `Fixed yield ${formatYield(stats.featured.yieldBps)}` : "Unavailable"} hint="Paid at maturity" />
      </section>

      <section className="flex flex-col gap-6 py-24">
        <h2 className="text-[40px] font-semibold">The farmer needs money before the harvest exists</h2>
        <p className="max-w-2xl text-base text-muted-foreground">
          Seed, fertilizer and fuel are paid in spring. Grain arrives in autumn. Banks want collateral the farmer does not have yet.
          Field-to-Finance turns verified fields into an instrument investors can buy today.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section className="-mx-4 flex flex-col gap-8 bg-muted px-4 py-24">
        <h2 className="text-[40px] font-semibold">How a deal moves</h2>
        <DealPath />
      </section>

      <section className="flex flex-col gap-6 py-24">
        <h2 className="text-[40px] font-semibold">Investors are protected by rules, not promises</h2>
        <ul className="grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <li className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">70% buffer</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Only 30% of each farm&apos;s expected harvest backs the Instrument.</p>
          </li>
          <li className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">Shortfall rules</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Missing volume comes from other fields, missing quality is bought on the market, otherwise the farmer repays in USD with a fixed premium.</p>
          </li>
          <li className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">Compliance in the token</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Instrument accounts are frozen until KYC passes, so an unapproved wallet cannot hold it.</p>
          </li>
        </ul>
        <div>
          <Link href="/pools" className="inline-flex h-11 items-center rounded-md bg-primary px-5 font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover active:scale-[0.98]">
            View open pools
          </Link>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the math and components**

`apps/web/src/lib/pool-math.ts`:
```ts
import type { PoolView } from "./chain/views";
import { withYield } from "./format";

export const remaining = (p: PoolView) => {
  const r = BigInt(p.targetAmount) - BigInt(p.raisedAmount);
  return r > 0n ? r : 0n;
};

export function fundedPercent(p: PoolView): string {
  const target = BigInt(p.targetAmount);
  if (target === 0n) return "0.0";
  const tenths = (BigInt(p.raisedAmount) * 1000n) / target;
  return `${tenths / 10n}.${tenths % 10n}`;
}

export const payoutPer = (p: PoolView, principal: bigint) => withYield(principal, p.yieldBps);

export const termDays = (p: PoolView) => Math.floor((p.maturityTs - p.subscriptionCloseTs) / 86_400);
```

`apps/web/src/components/pool/funding-meter.tsx`:
```tsx
import { formatUsd } from "../../lib/format";

export function FundingMeter({ raised, target }: { raised: string; target: string }) {
  const t = BigInt(target);
  const tenths = t === 0n ? 0n : (BigInt(raised) * 1000n) / t;
  const pct = Number(tenths) / 10;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <div
        role="meter"
        aria-label="Raised"
        aria-valuemin={0}
        aria-valuemax={100}
        aria-valuenow={Math.round(pct)}
        aria-valuetext={`${formatUsd(raised)} of ${formatUsd(target)}`}
        className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-muted"
      >
        <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-primary transition-[width] duration-300 ease-[var(--ease-out)]" style={{ width: `${Math.min(pct, 100)}%` }} />
      </div>
      <div className="tabular flex justify-between text-sm">
        <span className="font-medium">{`${formatUsd(raised)} of ${formatUsd(target)}`}</span>
        <span className="text-muted-foreground">{pct.toFixed(1)}% funded</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { SETTLEMENT_LABELS } from "../../lib/chain/enums";
import type { ProjectWithFields } from "../../lib/chain/views";
import { formatHa, formatTonnes, formatUsd, shortKey } from "../../lib/format";

export function ProjectsTable({ projects }: { projects: ProjectWithFields[] }) {

    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
      <table className="tabular w-full text-sm">
        <thead className="bg-muted text-left text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <tr>
            <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">Producer</th>
            <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">Expected</th>
            <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">Tokenized (30%)</th>
            <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">Requested</th>
            <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">Fields</th>
            <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">Paid out</th>
            <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">Settlement</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {projects.map((p) => (
            <tr key={p.address} className="border-b border-border">
              <td className="px-3 py-3 font-mono text-[13px]">{shortKey(p.producer)}</td>
              <td className="px-3 py-3">{formatTonnes(p.expectedKg)}</td>
              <td className="px-3 py-3">{formatTonnes(p.tokenizedKg)}</td>
              <td className="px-3 py-3">{formatUsd(p.requestedAmount)}</td>
              <td className="px-3 py-3">
                <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  {p.fields.map((f) => (
                    <li key={f.address}>
                      <Link href={`/fields/${f.address}`} className="underline decoration-border underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-foreground">
                        Field {f.index + 1}
                      </Link>{" "}
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground">{formatHa(f.areaHaX100)}</span>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </td>
              <td className="px-3 py-3">{p.disbursed ? "Yes" : "Not yet"}</td>
              <td className="px-3 py-3">{p.settlement ? SETTLEMENT_LABELS[p.settlement] : "Pending"}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import type { PoolDetail } from "../../lib/chain/views";
import { formatDateUtc, formatUsd, formatYield, relativeTime } from "../../lib/format";
import { payoutPer, termDays } from "../../lib/pool-math";
import { ExplorerLink } from "../explorer-link";
import { ScasStubBadge } from "../scas-stub-badge";
import { PoolStatusBadge } from "../status-badge";
import { FundingMeter } from "./funding-meter";
import { ProjectsTable } from "./projects-table";

function Row({ title, children }: { title: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section className="grid gap-2 border-b border-border py-5 last:border-0 sm:grid-cols-[180px_1fr]">
      <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold">{title}</h2>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-sm">{children}</div>
    </section>
  );
}

export function InstrumentCard({ detail, cluster = "devnet" }: { detail: PoolDetail; cluster?: string }) {
  const { pool, projects } = detail;
  const fields = projects.flatMap((p) => p.fields);
  const verified = projects.reduce((n, p) => n + p.verifiedFieldCount, 0);
  const total = projects.reduce((n, p) => n + p.fieldCount, 0);
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-8">
      <Row title="Status">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <PoolStatusBadge status={pool.status} />
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">Pool</span>
          <ExplorerLink kind="address" value={pool.address} cluster={cluster} />
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">Mint</span>
          <ExplorerLink kind="address" value={pool.mint} cluster={cluster} />
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">Vault</span>
          <ExplorerLink kind="address" value={pool.vault} cluster={cluster} />
        </div>
      </Row>
      <Row title="Issuer">
        <p>
          Demo Issuer key <ExplorerLink kind="address" value={pool.issuer} cluster={cluster} />. The Issuer carries the obligation to pay principal plus the fixed yield at maturity.
        </p>
      </Row>
      <Row title="Farm Projects">
        <ProjectsTable projects={projects} />
      </Row>
      <Row title="SCAS checks">
        <p className="tabular">{`${verified} of ${total} fields verified`}</p>
        <ScasStubBadge />
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">{fields.length ? "Open a field to see its attestation timeline and evidence." : "No fields yet."}</p>
      </Row>
      <Row title="Amount">
        <FundingMeter raised={pool.raisedAmount} target={pool.targetAmount} />
      </Row>
      <Row title="Term">
        <dl className="tabular grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-4 gap-y-1">
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Subscription closes</dt>
          <dd>
            {formatDateUtc(pool.subscriptionCloseTs)} <span className="text-muted-foreground">({relativeTime(pool.subscriptionCloseTs)})</span>
          </dd>
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Maturity</dt>
          <dd>
            {formatDateUtc(pool.maturityTs)} <span className="text-muted-foreground">({relativeTime(pool.maturityTs)})</span>
          </dd>
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Term</dt>
          <dd>{termDays(pool) >= 1 ? `${termDays(pool)} days` : "Under a day (devnet demo)"}</dd>
        </dl>
      </Row>
      <Row title="Fixed yield">
        <p className="text-base font-semibold text-gold-text">Fixed yield {formatYield(pool.yieldBps)}</p>
        <p className="tabular">{`${formatUsd(payoutPer(pool, 1_000_000_000n))} back for every ${formatUsd(1_000_000_000n)}`}</p>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">The same for every investor, whichever Shortfall rule settles a project.</p>
      </Row>
      <Row title="Shortfall rules">
        <ol className="list-decimal space-y-1 pl-5">
          <li>Volume shortfall: the Producer covers the missing tonnes from other fields.</li>
          <li>Quality shortfall: the Producer buys grain of the contracted class on the market.</li>
          <li>{`No such grain on the market: the Producer repays in USD with a ${formatYield(pool.premiumBps)} premium.`}</li>
        </ol>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Only 30% of each project&apos;s expected harvest backs the Instrument.</p>
      </Row>
      <Row title="Risks">
        <ul className="list-disc space-y-1 pl-5 text-muted-foreground">
          <li>Issuer credit risk: payout depends on the Issuer funding redemption.</li>
          <li>Field checks come from a SCAS stub until the external provider is integrated.</li>
          <li>The Pool holds freeze authority over Instrument accounts and can freeze an account for compliance reasons.</li>
          <li>Devnet demo with test USDC. Nothing here is an offer of securities.</li>
        </ul>
      </Row>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { PoolSummaryCard } from "@/components/pool/pool-summary-card";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/components/state";
import { getReader } from "@/lib/server/context";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Pools" };

export default async function PoolsPage() {
  let pools;
  try {
    pools = await getReader().listPools();
  } catch {
    return <ErrorState title="Could not load pools" body="Solana devnet did not answer. This is usually brief." retryHref="/pools" />;
  }
  const open = pools.filter((p) => p.status === "Open");
  const rest = pools.filter((p) => p.status !== "Open");
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-10">
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Pools</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Each pool finances verified farm projects for one crop and season with a fixed USDC yield.</p>
      </header>
      {pools.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No pools yet" body="Pools appear here once the Issuer creates one from verified farm projects." />
      ) : (
        <>
          <section className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold">Open for subscription</h2>
            {open.length ? (
              <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">{open.map((p) => <PoolSummaryCard key={p.address} pool={p} />)}</div>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">No pool is open right now.</p>
            )}
          </section>
          {rest.length ? (
            <section className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <h2 className="text-base font-semibold">Other pools</h2>
              <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">{rest.map((p) => <PoolSummaryCard key={p.address} pool={p} />)}</div>
            </section>
          ) : null}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { getReader } from "@/lib/server/context";
import { serverEnv } from "@/lib/server/env";

export async function GET(_req: Request, ctx: { params: Promise<{ poolId: string }> }) {
  const { poolId } = await ctx.params;
  const detail = await getReader().getPool(poolId).catch(() => null);
  if (!detail) return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
  return Response.json({
    name: detail.pool.instrument?.name ?? `Pool ${poolId}`,
    symbol: detail.pool.instrument?.symbol ?? "",
    description: `Field-to-Finance pool ${poolId}: ${detail.pool.crop} ${detail.pool.season}, fixed yield. Devnet demo.`,
    external_url: `${serverEnv().PUBLIC_BASE_URL}/pools/${poolId}`,
  });
}
```

### Task 12: Field page (satellite map and attestation timeline)

**Skills:** `ui-design`, `emil-design-eng`.

The NDVI chart from the original spec is dropped: the SCAS stub produces no vegetation data. The page shows the field boundary over real satellite imagery and a four-stage attestation timeline with the stub badge.

`apps/web/src/lib/map.ts`:
```ts
import type { StyleSpecification } from "maplibre-gl";

/** Keyless satellite tiles; attribution is required by Esri's terms. */
export const ESRI_IMAGERY_TILES = "https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}";
export const ESRI_ATTRIBUTION = "Imagery © Esri, Maxar, Earthstar Geographics";

export function polygonBounds(polygons: { coordinates: number[][][] }[]): [[number, number], [number, number]] | null {
  let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  for (const p of polygons) {
    for (const ring of p.coordinates) {
      for (const [x, y] of ring) {
        if (x === undefined || y === undefined) continue;
        minX = Math.min(minX, x);
        minY = Math.min(minY, y);
        maxX = Math.max(maxX, x);
        maxY = Math.max(maxY, y);
      }
    }
  }
  return Number.isFinite(minX) ? [[minX, minY], [maxX, maxY]] : null;
}

export function satelliteStyle(): StyleSpecification {
  return {
    version: 8,
    sources: { imagery: { type: "raster", tiles: [ESRI_IMAGERY_TILES], tileSize: 256, maxzoom: 19, attribution: ESRI_ATTRIBUTION } },
    layers: [{ id: "imagery", type: "raster", source: "imagery" }],
  };
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/field/field-map.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import maplibregl from "maplibre-gl";
import "maplibre-gl/dist/maplibre-gl.css";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { polygonBounds, satelliteStyle } from "../../lib/map";

export function FieldMap({ polygons, height = 360 }: { polygons: { coordinates: number[][][] }[]; height?: number }) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const bounds = polygonBounds(polygons);
    if (!ref.current || !bounds) return;
    const map = new maplibregl.Map({
      container: ref.current,
      style: satelliteStyle(),
      bounds,
      fitBoundsOptions: { padding: 48, maxZoom: 16 },
      attributionControl: { compact: true },
      cooperativeGestures: true,
    });
    map.addControl(new maplibregl.NavigationControl({ showCompass: false }), "top-right");
    map.on("load", () => {
      map.addSource("fields", {
        type: "geojson",
        data: {
          type: "FeatureCollection",
          features: polygons.map((p, i) => ({ type: "Feature", properties: { i }, geometry: { type: "Polygon", coordinates: p.coordinates } })),
        },
      });
      // Gold reads on imagery and is a fill on a dark ground, which the style-lock allows.
      map.addLayer({ id: "fields-fill", type: "fill", source: "fields", paint: { "fill-color": "#c9a227", "fill-opacity": 0.18 } });
      map.addLayer({ id: "fields-line", type: "line", source: "fields", paint: { "line-color": "#c9a227", "line-width": 2 } });
    });
    return () => map.remove();
  }, [polygons]);

  return <div ref={ref} role="img" aria-label="Field boundary over satellite imagery" style={{ height }} className="w-full overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-ink" />;
}
```

```tsx
import { CircleCheck, CircleDashed } from "lucide-react";
import type { AttestationView } from "../../lib/chain/views";
import { formatDateUtc } from "../../lib/format";
import { ExplorerLink } from "../explorer-link";
import { ScasStubBadge } from "../scas-stub-badge";

export const STAGES = ["FieldVerified", "Sowing", "Vegetation", "Harvest"] as const;
const LABEL: Record<(typeof STAGES)[number], string> = {
  FieldVerified: "Field verified",
  Sowing: "Sowing",
  Vegetation: "Vegetation",
  Harvest: "Harvest",
};

export function AttestationTimeline({ attestations, cluster = "devnet" }: { attestations: AttestationView[]; cluster?: string }) {
  return (
    <section className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h2 className="text-base font-semibold">Attestations</h2>
        <ScasStubBadge />
      </div>
      <ol className="flex flex-col">
        {STAGES.map((stage) => {
          const all = attestations.filter((a) => a.kind === stage).sort((a, b) => b.sequence - a.sequence);
          const latest = all[0];
          return (
            <li key={stage} className="flex min-h-11 items-start gap-3 border-b border-border py-3 last:border-0">
              {latest ? (
                <CircleCheck aria-hidden strokeWidth={1.5} className="mt-0.5 size-4 text-success" />
              ) : (
                <CircleDashed aria-hidden strokeWidth={1.5} className="mt-0.5 size-4 text-muted-foreground" />
              )}
              <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="font-medium">{LABEL[stage]}</span>
                  {latest ? <span className="rounded-full border border-success px-2 text-xs text-success">{latest.verdict}</span> : null}
                </div>
                {latest ? (
                  <div className="tabular flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-muted-foreground">
                    <span>{formatDateUtc(latest.recordedAt)}</span>
                    {all.length > 1 ? <span>{all.length} attestations</span> : null}
                    <ExplorerLink kind="address" value={latest.address} cluster={cluster} />
                    <a href={latest.evidenceUri} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="underline decoration-border underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-foreground">
                      Evidence JSON
                    </a>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground">Not yet attested</span>
                )}
              </div>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </section>
  );
}
```
