```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { ExplorerLink } from "@/components/explorer-link";
import { AttestationTimeline } from "@/components/field/attestation-timeline";
import { FieldMapLoader } from "@/components/field/field-map-loader";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/components/state";
import { formatHa, formatTonnes } from "@/lib/format";
import { loadPolygon } from "@/lib/polygons";
import { getReader, getStore } from "@/lib/server/context";
import { serverEnv } from "@/lib/server/env";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Field" };

export default async function FieldPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ address: string }> }) {
  const { address } = await params;
  let detail;
  try {
    detail = await getReader().getField(address);
  } catch {
    return <ErrorState title="Could not load this field" body="Solana devnet did not answer." retryHref={`/fields/${address}`} />;
  }
  if (!detail) notFound();
  const polygon = await loadPolygon(getStore(), detail.field.polygonHash).catch(() => null);
  const cluster = serverEnv().SOLANA_CLUSTER;
  const { field, project, pool } = detail;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          {pool ? <Link href={`/pools/${pool.poolId}`} className="underline underline-offset-4">{pool.instrument?.symbol ?? `Pool ${pool.poolId}`}</Link> : "Not in a pool yet"}
        </p>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Field {field.index + 1}</h1>
      </header>
      <div className="grid items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1.4fr_1fr]">
        {polygon ? (
          <FieldMapLoader polygons={[polygon]} />
        ) : (
          <EmptyState title="Boundary file not found" body={`No stored polygon matches hash ${field.polygonHash.slice(0, 12)}…. The on-chain record is still valid.`} />
        )}
        <dl className="tabular grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-4 gap-y-2 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-6 text-sm">
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Area</dt>
          <dd>{formatHa(field.areaHaX100)}</dd>
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Crop</dt>
          <dd className="capitalize">{project.crop}, season {project.season}</dd>
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Project expected harvest</dt>
          <dd>{formatTonnes(project.expectedKg)}</dd>
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Verified</dt>
          <dd>{field.verified ? "Yes" : "Not yet"}</dd>
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Polygon hash</dt>
          <dd className="font-mono text-[13px] break-all">{field.polygonHash}</dd>
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Field account</dt>
          <dd><ExplorerLink kind="address" value={field.address} cluster={cluster} /></dd>
        </dl>
      </div>
      <AttestationTimeline attestations={detail.attestations} cluster={cluster} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 13: Investor view (invest panel, mock KYC, faucet, portfolio, refund, redeem)

KYC is platform-level (ADR 0011): an investor gets verified once (the server calls `approve_investor`), then subscribes to any pool; `subscribe` creates and thaws the pool's Instrument account in the same transaction.

**Skills:** `ui-design`, `emil-design-eng`. The KYC rejection is a demo beat: the message must be calm, specific, and offer one primary way out ("Complete KYC").

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/pool/invest-panel.tsx`, `pool/kyc-dialog.tsx`, `wallet/faucet-button.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/portfolio/portfolio-view.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/portfolio/page.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/invest.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/app/pools/[poolId]/page.tsx` (render `InvestPanel` inside `<aside id="invest">`)
- Test: `apps/web/test/invest.test.ts`, `test/invest-panel.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useAdapterSigner`, `ConnectButton` (Task 9); `useTransaction`, `TxStatus`, `useJson` (Task 9); `parseUsd`, `formatUsd`, `formatYield`, `formatDateUtc`, `withYield` (Task 2); `remaining` (Task 11); `PoolView`, `InvestorState`, `PositionView` (Task 2); `COUNTRIES` (Task 8); `track` (Task 9); `POST /api/kyc`, `POST /api/faucet`, `GET /api/investor/:wallet`, `POST /api/tx/subscribe|refund|redeem`.
- Produces:
  - `invest.ts`: `type PanelMode = "draft" | "subscribe" | "closed-funded" | "refund" | "redeem" | "closed"`, `panelMode(pool: PoolView, nowSec: number): PanelMode`, `validateAmount(input: string, max: bigint): { ok: true; amount: bigint } | { ok: false; message: string }`, `positionFor(state: InvestorState | null, poolId: string): PositionView | null`.
  - `InvestPanel({ pool }: { pool: PoolView })`, `KycDialog({ open, onOpenChange, wallet, onApproved })`, `FaucetButton({ wallet, usdc, onDone, label? })`, `PortfolioView()`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the logic**

`apps/web/src/lib/invest.ts`:
```ts
import type { InvestorState, PoolView, PositionView } from "./chain/views";
import { formatUsd, parseUsd } from "./format";

export type PanelMode = "draft" | "subscribe" | "closed-funded" | "refund" | "redeem" | "closed";

export function panelMode(pool: PoolView, nowSec: number): PanelMode {
  switch (pool.status) {
    case "Draft":
      return "draft";
    case "Open":
      return nowSec < pool.subscriptionCloseTs && BigInt(pool.raisedAmount) < BigInt(pool.targetAmount) ? "subscribe" : "closed";
    case "Funded":
      return "closed-funded";
    case "Refunding":
      return "refund";
    case "Redeeming":
      return "redeem";
  }
}

export function validateAmount(input: string, max: bigint): { ok: true; amount: bigint } | { ok: false; message: string } {
  let amount: bigint;
  try {
    amount = parseUsd(input);
  } catch (e) {
    return { ok: false, message: (e as Error).message };
  }
  if (amount <= 0n) return { ok: false, message: "Enter an amount above $0.00" };
  if (amount > max) return { ok: false, message: `The pool needs at most ${formatUsd(max)} more` };
  return { ok: true, amount };
}

export const positionFor = (state: InvestorState | null, poolId: string): PositionView | null =>
  state?.positions.find((p) => p.poolId === poolId) ?? null;
```

`apps/web/src/components/wallet/faucet-button.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { track } from "../../lib/analytics";
import { ExplorerLink } from "../explorer-link";

export function FaucetButton({ wallet, usdc, onDone, label }: { wallet: string; usdc: boolean; onDone?: () => void; label?: string }) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<{ busy: boolean; message: string | null; signature: string | null }>({ busy: false, message: null, signature: null });
  async function run() {
    setState({ busy: true, message: null, signature: null });
    const res = await fetch("/api/faucet", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ wallet, usdc }) })
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .catch(() => ({ ok: false, error: { title: "Could not reach the faucet" } }));
    if (res.ok) {
      track("faucet_used", { usdc });
      setState({ busy: false, message: res.signature ? "Test funds sent" : "You already have enough devnet SOL", signature: res.signature });
      onDone?.();
    } else {
      setState({ busy: false, message: res.error?.title ?? "The faucet failed", signature: null });
    }
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={run}
        disabled={state.busy}
        className="self-start rounded-md border border-border bg-card px-3 py-2 font-medium hover:bg-muted disabled:opacity-60"
      >
        {state.busy ? "Sending" : (label ?? (usdc ? "Get 1,000 test USDC" : "Get devnet SOL for fees"))}
      </button>
      {state.message ? (
        <p className="text-muted-foreground" role="status">
          {state.message} {state.signature ? <ExplorerLink kind="tx" value={state.signature} /> : null}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/pool/kyc-dialog.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { track } from "../../lib/analytics";
import { COUNTRIES } from "../../lib/kyc";
import { ExplorerLink } from "../explorer-link";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "../ui/dialog";

export function KycDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  wallet,
  onApproved,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (v: boolean) => void;
  wallet: string;
  onApproved: () => void;
}) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [signature, setSignature] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function submit(form: FormData) {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    const body = {
      wallet,
      fullName: String(form.get("fullName") ?? ""),
      country: String(form.get("country") ?? ""),
      email: String(form.get("email") ?? ""),
      eligible: form.get("eligible") === "on",
      demoConsent: form.get("demoConsent") === "on",
    };
    const res = await fetch("/api/kyc", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .catch(() => ({ ok: false, error: { title: "Could not reach the server", detail: "" } }));
    setBusy(false);
    if (res.ok) {
      track("kyc_approved");
      setSignature(res.signature);
      onApproved();
    } else {
      setError([res.error?.title, res.error?.detail].filter(Boolean).join(": "));
    }
  }

  const field = "flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm";
  const input = "h-10 rounded-md border border-input bg-card px-3 text-sm";
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent>
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Verify your identity</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            Demo KYC: the form is checked and discarded. One approval on devnet covers every pool.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        {signature ? (
          <div role="status" className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-sm">
            <p className="font-medium text-success">Verified. You can subscribe to any pool.</p>
            <ExplorerLink kind="tx" value={signature} />
            <button type="button" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)} className="self-start rounded-md bg-primary px-3 py-2 font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover">
              Back to subscription
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form action={submit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <label className={field}>
              Full name
              <input name="fullName" required autoComplete="name" className={input} />
            </label>
            <label className={field}>
              Country of residence
              <select name="country" required className={input} defaultValue="">
                <option value="" disabled>
                  Choose a country
                </option>
                {COUNTRIES.map((c) => (
                  <option key={c}>{c}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
            <label className={field}>
              Email
              <input name="email" type="email" required autoComplete="email" className={input} />
            </label>
            <label className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm">
              <input name="eligible" type="checkbox" required className="mt-1" />I confirm I am an eligible investor in my jurisdiction.
            </label>
            <label className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm">
              <input name="demoConsent" type="checkbox" required className="mt-1" />I understand this is a devnet demo with test USDC.
            </label>
            {error ? (
              <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">
                {error}
              </p>
            ) : null}
            <button type="submit" disabled={busy} className="h-10 rounded-md bg-primary px-4 font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover disabled:opacity-60">
              {busy ? "Approving on devnet" : "Submit and approve"}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/pool/invest-panel.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useWallet } from "@solana/wallet-adapter-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import type { ErrorAction } from "../../lib/chain/explain";
import type { InvestorState, PoolView } from "../../lib/chain/views";
import { formatDateUtc, formatUsd, formatYield, withYield } from "../../lib/format";
import { panelMode, positionFor, validateAmount } from "../../lib/invest";
import { remaining } from "../../lib/pool-math";
import { TxStatus } from "../tx/tx-status";
import { useTransaction } from "../tx/use-transaction";
import { useJson } from "../use-json";
import { ConnectButton } from "../wallet/connect-button";
import { FaucetButton } from "../wallet/faucet-button";
import { useAdapterSigner } from "../wallet/use-adapter-signer";
import { KycDialog } from "./kyc-dialog";

export function InvestPanel({ pool }: { pool: PoolView }) {
  const { publicKey } = useWallet();
  const signer = useAdapterSigner();
  const wallet = publicKey?.toBase58() ?? null;
  const investor = useJson<InvestorState>(wallet ? `/api/investor/${wallet}` : null);
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");
  const [amountError, setAmountError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [kycOpen, setKycOpen] = useState(false);
  const [showFaucet, setShowFaucet] = useState(false);
  const subscribe = useTransaction("subscribe", signer, { onSuccess: investor.reload });
  const refund = useTransaction("refund", signer, { onSuccess: investor.reload });
  const redeem = useTransaction("redeem", signer, { onSuccess: investor.reload });

  const mode = panelMode(pool, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
  const position = positionFor(investor.data, pool.poolId);
  const kycDone = Boolean(investor.data?.approved);
  const balance = BigInt(position?.balance ?? "0");
  const symbol = pool.instrument?.symbol ?? `POOL-${pool.poolId}`;

  const onAction = (a: ErrorAction) => {
    if (a === "kyc") setKycOpen(true);
    if (a === "faucet") setShowFaucet(true);
  };

  if (!wallet) {
    return (
      <Panel title="Invest">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Connect a Solana wallet on devnet to subscribe.</p>
        <ConnectButton />
      </Panel>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Panel title="Invest">
      <div className="tabular flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
        <div className="flex justify-between">
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">KYC</span>
          <span className={kycDone ? "font-medium text-success" : "font-medium text-warning"}>{kycDone ? "Verified" : "KYC not completed"}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex justify-between">
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">Test USDC</span>
          <span>{investor.data ? formatUsd(investor.data.usdcBalance) : "Loading"}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex justify-between">
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">You hold</span>
          <span>{`${formatUsd(balance)} of ${symbol}`}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      {investor.error ? <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{investor.error}</p> : null}

      {mode === "subscribe" ? (
        <form
          className="flex flex-col gap-2"
          onSubmit={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const v = validateAmount(amount, remaining(pool));
            if (!v.ok) {
              setAmountError(v.message);
              return;
            }
            setAmountError(null);
            void subscribe.run({ poolId: pool.poolId, amount: v.amount.toString() });
          }}
        >
          <label htmlFor="amount" className="text-sm font-medium">
            Amount (USD)
          </label>
          <input
            id="amount"
            inputMode="decimal"
            value={amount}
            onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={`Up to ${formatUsd(remaining(pool))}`}
            aria-invalid={amountError ? true : undefined}
            aria-describedby={amountError ? "amount-error" : "amount-hint"}
            className="tabular h-10 rounded-md border border-input bg-card px-3 text-sm aria-[invalid=true]:border-destructive"
          />
          {amountError ? (
            <p id="amount-error" className="text-sm text-destructive">
              {amountError}
            </p>
          ) : (
            <p id="amount-hint" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              {`Fixed yield ${formatYield(pool.yieldBps)}. Paid at maturity, ${formatDateUtc(pool.maturityTs)}.`}
            </p>
          )}
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={subscribe.busy}
            className="h-10 rounded-md bg-primary px-4 font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            Subscribe
          </button>
          <TxStatus state={subscribe.state} onAction={onAction} successLabel={`Subscribed. ${symbol} is in your wallet.`} />
        </form>
      ) : null}

      {mode === "draft" ? <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">This pool is not open yet.</p> : null}
      {mode === "closed" ? <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Subscription has ended. The Issuer closes the pool next.</p> : null}
      {mode === "closed-funded" ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{`Funded. Redemption opens after maturity on ${formatDateUtc(pool.maturityTs)} once the Issuer deposits principal plus yield.`}</p>
      ) : null}

      {mode === "refund" ? (
        <HolderAction
          disabled={balance === 0n || refund.busy}
          label={`Refund ${formatUsd(balance)}`}
          note="The pool closed under target. Refunds return your USDC and burn the Instrument."
          onClick={() => void refund.run({ poolId: pool.poolId, amount: balance.toString() })}
        >
          <TxStatus state={refund.state} onAction={onAction} successLabel="Refunded" />
        </HolderAction>
      ) : null}

      {mode === "redeem" ? (
        <HolderAction
          disabled={balance === 0n || redeem.busy}
          label={`Redeem for ${formatUsd(withYield(balance, pool.yieldBps))}`}
          note="Burns your Instrument and pays principal plus the fixed yield."
          onClick={() => void redeem.run({ poolId: pool.poolId, amount: balance.toString() })}
        >
          <TxStatus state={redeem.state} onAction={onAction} successLabel="Redeemed" />
        </HolderAction>
      ) : null}

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-border pt-4">
        {!kycDone ? (
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setKycOpen(true)} className="self-start text-sm font-medium text-gold-text underline underline-offset-4">
            Get verified
          </button>
        ) : null}
        {showFaucet || BigInt(investor.data?.usdcBalance ?? "0") === 0n ? <FaucetButton wallet={wallet} usdc onDone={investor.reload} /> : null}
      </div>

      <KycDialog open={kycOpen} onOpenChange={setKycOpen} wallet={wallet} onApproved={investor.reload} />
    </Panel>
  );
}

function Panel({ title, children }: { title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section className="flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-6">
      <h2 className="text-base font-semibold">{title}</h2>
      {children}
    </section>
  );
}

function HolderAction({ label, note, disabled, onClick, children }: { label: string; note: string; disabled: boolean; onClick: () => void; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{note}</p>
      <button type="button" disabled={disabled} onClick={onClick} className="h-10 rounded-md bg-primary px-4 font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover disabled:opacity-60">
        {label}
      </button>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}
```

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useWallet } from "@solana/wallet-adapter-react";
import type { InvestorState } from "../../lib/chain/views";
import { formatUsd, withYield } from "../../lib/format";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "../state";
import { PoolStatusBadge } from "../status-badge";
import { useJson } from "../use-json";
import { ConnectButton } from "../wallet/connect-button";
import { FaucetButton } from "../wallet/faucet-button";

export function PortfolioView() {
  const { publicKey } = useWallet();
  const wallet = publicKey?.toBase58() ?? null;
  const { data, error, loading, reload } = useJson<InvestorState>(wallet ? `/api/investor/${wallet}` : null);

  if (!wallet) return <EmptyState title="Connect a wallet" body="Your positions appear here once you connect a devnet wallet." action={<ConnectButton />} />;
  if (error) return <ErrorState title="Could not load your portfolio" body={error} onRetry={reload} />;
  if (loading || !data) return <div className="h-40 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted motion-reduce:animate-none" aria-label="Loading portfolio" />;

  const held = data.positions.filter((p) => BigInt(p.balance) > 0n);
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <dl className="tabular grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-4">
          <dt className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Test USDC</dt>
          <dd className="text-xl font-semibold">{formatUsd(data.usdcBalance)}</dd>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-4">
          <dt className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">KYC</dt>
          <dd className="text-xl font-semibold">{data.approved ? "Approved" : "Not completed"}</dd>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-4">
          <dt className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Positions</dt>
          <dd className="text-xl font-semibold">{held.length}</dd>
        </div>
      </dl>
      {held.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No positions yet" body="Subscribe to an open pool to hold its Instrument." action={<Link href="/pools" className="text-gold-text underline underline-offset-4">Browse pools</Link>} />
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-border bg-card">
          <table className="tabular w-full text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-muted text-left text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">Instrument</th>
                <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">Status</th>
                <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">Holding</th>
                <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">At maturity</th>
                <th className="px-3 py-2 font-medium" />
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {held.map((p) => (
                <tr key={p.pool} className="border-t border-border">
                  <td className="px-3 py-3 font-medium">{p.symbol}</td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-3"><PoolStatusBadge status={p.status} /></td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-3">{formatUsd(p.balance)}</td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-3">{formatUsd(withYield(BigInt(p.balance), p.yieldBps))}</td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-3 text-right">
                    <Link href={`/pools/${p.poolId}`} className="text-gold-text underline underline-offset-4">
                      {p.status === "Redeeming" ? "Redeem" : p.status === "Refunding" ? "Refund" : "View"}
                    </Link>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
      <FaucetButton wallet={wallet} usdc onDone={reload} />
    </div>
  );
}

`apps/web/src/lib/project-status.ts`:
```ts
import { SETTLEMENT_LABELS } from "./chain/enums";
import type { ProducerProject } from "./chain/views";
import { formatUsd } from "./format";

export interface ProjectStatus {
  label: string;
  tone: "pending" | "progress" | "success" | "warning";
}

export function projectStatus(p: ProducerProject): ProjectStatus {
  const symbol = p.symbol ?? "the pool";
  if (!p.pool) {
    return p.verifiedFieldCount < p.fieldCount
      ? { label: `Waiting for field checks (${p.verifiedFieldCount} of ${p.fieldCount} verified)`, tone: "pending" }
      : { label: "Verified. Waiting to join a pool", tone: "progress" };
  }
  if (p.settlement) return { label: `Settled: ${SETTLEMENT_LABELS[p.settlement]}`, tone: "success" };
  switch (p.poolStatus) {
    case "Draft":
      return { label: `In ${symbol}, opening soon`, tone: "progress" };
    case "Open":
      return { label: `In ${symbol}, raising from investors`, tone: "progress" };
    case "Refunding":
      return { label: `${symbol} did not reach its target; investors are refunded`, tone: "warning" };
    case "Funded":
    case "Redeeming":
      return p.disbursed
        ? { label: `Funded. ${formatUsd(p.requestedAmount)} paid to you`, tone: "success" }
        : { label: `Funded. Payout of ${formatUsd(p.requestedAmount)} is on its way`, tone: "progress" };
    default:
      return { label: "Status unavailable", tone: "pending" };
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/farmer/geojson-upload.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { track } from "../../lib/analytics";
import { GeoJsonInputError, MAX_FIELDS_PER_UPLOAD, splitFeatures } from "../../lib/geojson";

export interface UploadedField {
  name: string;
  polygonHash: string;
  areaHa: number;
  areaHaX100: number;
  polygon: { coordinates: number[][][] };
  declaredHa: string;
}

function nameOf(item: unknown, i: number): string {
  const n = (item as { properties?: { name?: unknown } })?.properties?.name;
  return typeof n === "string" && n.trim() ? n.trim() : `Field ${i + 1}`;
}

export function GeojsonUpload({ onUploaded }: { onUploaded: (fields: UploadedField[]) => void }) {
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);

  async function handle(text: string) {
    setError(null);
    setCount(0);
    let parsed: unknown;
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(text);
    } catch {
      setError("The file is not valid JSON.");
      return;
    }
    let items: unknown[];
    try {
      items = splitFeatures(parsed);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof GeoJsonInputError ? e.message : "The file could not be read.");
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const fields: UploadedField[] = [];
      for (const [i, item] of items.entries()) {
        const res = await fetch("/api/polygons", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(item) });
        const body = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${nameOf(item, i)}: ${body.error ?? "upload failed"}`);
        fields.push({ name: nameOf(item, i), polygonHash: body.polygonHash, areaHa: body.areaHa, areaHaX100: body.areaHaX100, polygon: body.polygon, declaredHa: body.areaHa.toFixed(2) });
      }
      setCount(fields.length);
      track("fields_uploaded", { count: fields.length });
      onUploaded(fields);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Upload failed.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <label htmlFor="geojson" className="text-sm font-medium">
        Field boundaries (GeoJSON)
      </label>
      <input
        id="geojson"
        type="file"
        accept=".geojson,.json,application/geo+json,application/json"
        disabled={busy}
        onChange={async (e) => {
          const f = e.target.files?.[0];
          if (f) await handle(await f.text());
        }}
        className="text-sm file:mr-3 file:rounded-md file:border file:border-border file:bg-card file:px-3 file:py-2 file:font-medium"
      />
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        {`One Polygon per field, up to ${MAX_FIELDS_PER_UPLOAD}. `}
        <button
          type="button"
          className="underline underline-offset-4"
          onClick={async () => handle(await (await fetch("/samples/demo-fields.geojson")).text())}
        >
          Use the sample file
        </button>
      </p>
      {busy ? <p role="status" className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Checking boundaries</p> : null}
      {count ? <p role="status" className="text-sm font-medium text-success">{`${count} field${count > 1 ? "s" : ""} ready`}</p> : null}
      {error ? (
        <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import type { ProducerProject } from "../../lib/chain/views";
import { formatHa, formatTonnes, formatUsd } from "../../lib/format";
import { projectStatus } from "../../lib/project-status";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "../state";
import { useJson } from "../use-json";

const TONE = { pending: "text-muted-foreground", progress: "text-info", success: "text-success", warning: "text-warning" } as const;

export function ProjectList({ wallet, refreshKey }: { wallet: string; refreshKey: number }) {
  const { data, error, loading, reload } = useJson<{ projects: ProducerProject[] }>(`/api/producer/${wallet}?k=${refreshKey}`);
  if (error) return <ErrorState title="Could not load your projects" body={error} onRetry={reload} />;
  if (loading && !data) return <div className="h-24 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted motion-reduce:animate-none" aria-label="Loading projects" />;
  if (!data?.projects.length) return <EmptyState title="No projects yet" body="Register your fields below to request financing for next season." />;
  return (
    <ul className="flex flex-col divide-y divide-border rounded-lg border border-border bg-card">
      {data.projects.map((p) => {
        const s = projectStatus(p);
        return (
          <li key={p.address} className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-4 text-sm">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span className="font-medium capitalize">
                {p.crop} {p.season}
              </span>
              <span className={`font-medium ${TONE[s.tone]}`}>{s.label}</span>
            </div>
            <p className="tabular text-muted-foreground">
              {`Expected ${formatTonnes(p.expectedKg)}, 30% tokenized (${formatTonnes(p.tokenizedKg)}). Requested ${formatUsd(p.requestedAmount)}.`}
            </p>
            <ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
              {p.fields.map((f) => (
                <li key={f.address}>
                  <Link href={`/fields/${f.address}`} className="underline decoration-border underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-foreground">
                    {`Field ${f.index + 1}, ${formatHa(f.areaHaX100)}${f.verified ? ", verified" : ""}`}
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/farmer/farmer-app.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import type { InvestorState } from "../../lib/chain/views";
import { formatTonnes, formatUsd, parseUsd } from "../../lib/format";
import { FieldMapLoader } from "../field/field-map-loader";
import { TxStatus } from "../tx/tx-status";
import { useTransaction } from "../tx/use-transaction";
import { useJson } from "../use-json";
import { FarmerSessionProvider, useFarmerSession } from "../wallet/farmer-session";
import { FaucetButton } from "../wallet/faucet-button";
import { GeojsonUpload, type UploadedField } from "./geojson-upload";
import { ProjectList } from "./project-list";

const SOL_FOR_FEES = 20_000_000;

function Register({ wallet, onRegistered }: { wallet: string; onRegistered: () => void }) {
  const session = useFarmerSession();
  const [fields, setFields] = useState<UploadedField[]>([]);
  const [tonnes, setTonnes] = useState("400");
  const [usd, setUsd] = useState("2000");
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const tx = useTransaction("register-project", session.signer, { onSuccess: onRegistered });
  const balance = useJson<InvestorState>(`/api/investor/${wallet}`);
  const lowSol = (balance.data?.solLamports ?? SOL_FOR_FEES) < SOL_FOR_FEES;
  let requestedLabel = "";
  try {
    requestedLabel = formatUsd(parseUsd(usd));
  } catch {
    requestedLabel = "";
  }

  function submit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!fields.length) return setFormError("Upload your field boundaries first.");
    const t = Number(tonnes);
    if (!Number.isFinite(t) || t <= 0) return setFormError("Enter the expected harvest in tonnes.");
    let requested: bigint;
    try {
      requested = parseUsd(usd);
    } catch (err) {
      return setFormError((err as Error).message);
    }
    const declared = fields.map((f) => Math.round(Number(f.declaredHa) * 100));
    if (declared.some((d) => !Number.isFinite(d) || d <= 0)) return setFormError("Enter a declared area above 0 ha for every field.");
    setFormError(null);
    void tx.run({
      crop: "wheat",
      season: 2027,
      expectedKg: String(Math.round(t * 1000)),
      requestedAmount: requested.toString(),
      fields: fields.map((f, i) => ({ polygonHash: f.polygonHash, areaHaX100: declared[i] as number })),
    });
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-col gap-6 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-6">
      <h2 className="text-base font-semibold">Request financing for the 2027 wheat season</h2>
      <GeojsonUpload onUploaded={setFields} />
      {fields.length ? (
        <>
          <FieldMapLoader polygons={fields.map((f) => f.polygon)} height={280} />
          <table className="tabular w-full text-sm">
            <thead className="text-left text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-2 font-medium">Field</th>
                <th className="py-2 font-medium">Measured</th>
                <th className="py-2 font-medium">Declared (ha)</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {fields.map((f, i) => (
                <tr key={f.polygonHash} className="border-t border-border">
                  <td className="py-2">{f.name}</td>
                  <td className="py-2">{`${f.areaHa.toFixed(2)} ha`}</td>
                  <td className="py-2">
                    <input
                      aria-label={`Declared area for ${f.name}`}
                      inputMode="decimal"
                      value={f.declaredHa}
                      onChange={(e) => setFields((prev) => prev.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, declaredHa: e.target.value } : x)))}
                      className="h-9 w-28 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-2"
                    />
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </>
      ) : null}
      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
          Expected harvest (tonnes)
          <input value={tonnes} onChange={(e) => setTonnes(e.target.value)} inputMode="decimal" className="tabular h-10 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3" />
          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{`30% backs the instrument: ${Number(tonnes) > 0 ? formatTonnes(BigInt(Math.round(Number(tonnes) * 300))) : "0 t"}`}</span>
        </label>
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
          Amount requested (USD)
          <input value={usd} onChange={(e) => setUsd(e.target.value)} inputMode="decimal" className="tabular h-10 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3" />
          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Paid in USDC when the pool is funded.</span>
        </label>
      </div>
      {formError ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{formError}</p> : null}
      {lowSol ? <FaucetButton wallet={wallet} usdc={false} onDone={balance.reload} label="Get free devnet SOL for fees" /> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={tx.busy} className="h-11 self-start rounded-md bg-primary px-5 font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-60">
        Register fields
      </button>
      <TxStatus state={tx.state} onAction={(a) => a === "faucet" && void balance.reload()} successLabel={`Registered. Requested ${requestedLabel}`} />
    </form>
  );
}

function Inner() {
  const session = useFarmerSession();
  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);
  if (!session.ready) return <div className="h-40 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted motion-reduce:animate-none" aria-label="Loading account" />;
  if (!session.authenticated || !session.signer) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-4 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="text-base font-semibold">Sign in to register your fields</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          {session.mode === "privy" ? "Sign in with your email. We create a secure account for you; there is no seed phrase to manage." : "Connect a Solana wallet on devnet."}
        </p>
        <button type="button" onClick={session.login} className="h-11 rounded-md bg-primary px-5 font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover">
          {session.mode === "privy" ? "Sign in with email" : "Connect wallet"}
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        <span>{session.email ? `Signed in as ${session.email}` : "Wallet connected"}</span>
        <button type="button" onClick={session.logout} className="underline underline-offset-4">
          Sign out
        </button>
      </div>
      <section className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <h2 className="text-base font-semibold">Your projects</h2>
        <ProjectList wallet={session.signer.publicKey} refreshKey={refreshKey} />
      </section>
      <Register wallet={session.signer.publicKey} onRegistered={() => setRefreshKey((k) => k + 1)} />
    </div>
  );
}

export function FarmerApp() {
  return (
    <FarmerSessionProvider>
      <Inner />
    </FarmerSessionProvider>
  );
}
```

import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ActionButton } from "../src/components/admin/action-button";

vi.mock("next/navigation", () => ({ useRouter: () => ({ refresh: vi.fn() }) }));

describe("ActionButton", () => {
  it("shows pending, then the message with an explorer link per signature", async () => {
    let resolve!: (v: unknown) => void;
    const action = vi.fn(() => new Promise((r) => (resolve = r)));
    render(<ActionButton label="Open pool" role="Issuer" action={action as never} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Open pool/ }));
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Open pool/ })).toBeDisabled();
    resolve({ ok: true, message: "Pool is open for subscription", signatures: ["5a", "5b"] });
    expect(await screen.findByText("Pool is open for subscription")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole("link")).toHaveLength(2);
  });

    render(<ActionButton label="Add project" role="Issuer" action={action as never} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Add project/ }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("Some fields are not verified yet");
    expect(screen.getByText("Program logs")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Disburse/ })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText("Needs a Funded pool")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("asks for confirmation inline when confirmText is set", async () => {
    const action = vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true, message: "done", signatures: [] }));
    render(<ActionButton label="Sweep" role="Issuer" action={action as never} confirmText="Return the vault remainder to the Issuer?" />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Sweep/ }));
    expect(action).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Confirm" }));
    expect(action).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});

    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Premium (%)")).toHaveValue("5.5");
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Subscription closes in (minutes)")).toHaveValue("10");
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Matures in (minutes)")).toHaveValue("20");
  });

  it("converts percentages to basis points", async () => {
    render(<CreatePoolForm defaultPoolId="3" />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Create pool/ }));
    expect(createPoolAction).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ poolId: "3", yieldBps: 800, premiumBps: 550, closeInMinutes: 10, maturityInMinutes: 20, season: 2027 }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Pool 3 created as Draft")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("blocks a premium outside 5 to 6 percent before calling the server", async () => {
    createPoolAction.mockClear();
    render(<CreatePoolForm defaultPoolId="3" />);
    const premium = screen.getByLabelText("Premium (%)");
    await userEvent.clear(premium);
    await userEvent.type(premium, "7");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Create pool/ }));
    expect(screen.getByText("Premium must be between 5% and 6%")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(createPoolAction).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

`apps/web/src/components/admin/action-button.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import type { ActionResult } from "../../app/admin/actions";
import { TxStatus } from "../tx/tx-status";
import { ExplorerLink } from "../explorer-link";

export function ActionButton({
  label,
  role,
  action,
  confirmText,
  disabled,
  disabledReason,
}: {
  label: string;
  role: "Issuer" | "Compliance" | "SCAS stub" | "Anyone";
  action: () => Promise<ActionResult>;
  confirmText?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  disabledReason?: string;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<ActionResult | null>(null);

  const go = () =>
    start(async () => {
      setConfirming(false);
      setResult(null);
      const r = await action();
      setResult(r);
      if (r.ok) router.refresh();
    });

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={disabled || pending}
          onClick={() => (confirmText ? setConfirming(true) : go())}
          className="h-9 rounded-md bg-primary px-3 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover active:scale-[0.98] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {pending ? `${label}…` : label}
        </button>
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Signed by {role}</span>
      </div>
      {disabled && disabledReason ? <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{disabledReason}</p> : null}
      {confirming ? (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
          <span>{confirmText}</span>
          <button type="button" onClick={go} className="rounded-md border border-destructive px-2 py-1 font-medium text-destructive hover:bg-muted">
            Confirm
          </button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setConfirming(false)} className="rounded-md px-2 py-1 hover:bg-muted">
            Cancel
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {result?.ok ? (
        <div role="status" className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
          <p className="font-medium text-success">{result.message}</p>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            {result.signatures.map((s) => (
              <ExplorerLink key={s} kind="tx" value={s} />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {result && !result.ok ? <TxStatus state={{ phase: "error", error: result.error, logs: result.logs }} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/admin/create-pool-form.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { createPoolAction } from "../../app/admin/actions";
import { ActionButton } from "./action-button";

const toBps = (pct: string) => Math.round(Number(pct) * 100);

export function CreatePoolForm({ defaultPoolId }: { defaultPoolId: string }) {
  const [v, setV] = useState({
    poolId: defaultPoolId,
    crop: "wheat",
    season: "2027",
    name: "Wheat 2027",
    symbol: "WHEAT-2027",
    yieldPct: "8.0",
    premiumPct: "5.5",
    closeIn: "10",
    maturityIn: "20",
  });
  const set = (k: keyof typeof v) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => setV((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: e.target.value }));

  const field = (k: keyof typeof v, label: string) => (
    <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
      {label}
      <input value={v[k]} onChange={set(k)} className="tabular h-9 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-2" />
    </label>
  );

  async function submit() {
    const premiumBps = toBps(v.premiumPct);
    if (!(premiumBps >= 500 && premiumBps <= 600)) {
      // Shown by ActionButton like any other rejection; the server re-validates.
      return { ok: false as const, error: { code: "BadRequest", title: "Premium must be between 5% and 6%", detail: "", action: "none" as const }, logs: [] };
    }
    return createPoolAction({
      poolId: v.poolId,
      crop: v.crop,
      season: Number(v.season),
      name: v.name,
      symbol: v.symbol,
      yieldBps: toBps(v.yieldPct),
      premiumBps,
      closeInMinutes: Number(v.closeIn),
      maturityInMinutes: Number(v.maturityIn),
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
        {field("poolId", "Pool id")}
        {field("crop", "Crop")}
        {field("season", "Season")}
        {field("name", "Name")}
        {field("symbol", "Symbol")}
        {field("yieldPct", "Fixed yield (%)")}
        {field("premiumPct", "Premium (%)")}
        {field("closeIn", "Subscription closes in (minutes)")}
        {field("maturityIn", "Matures in (minutes)")}
      </div>
      <ActionButton label="Create pool" role="Issuer" action={submit} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { recordSettlementAction } from "../../app/admin/actions";
import { SETTLEMENT_LABELS, SETTLEMENT_RULES, type SettlementRuleName } from "../../lib/chain/enums";
import { ActionButton } from "./action-button";

export function SettlementForm({ poolId, project, disabledReason }: { poolId: string; project: string; disabledReason?: string }) {
  const [rule, setRule] = useState<SettlementRuleName>("CoveredFromOtherFields");
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <select value={rule} onChange={(e) => setRule(e.target.value as SettlementRuleName)} aria-label="Shortfall rule" className="h-9 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-2 text-sm">
        {SETTLEMENT_RULES.map((r) => (
          <option key={r} value={r}>
            {SETTLEMENT_LABELS[r]}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      <ActionButton label="Record settlement" role="Issuer" action={() => recordSettlementAction(poolId, project, rule)} disabled={Boolean(disabledReason)} disabledReason={disabledReason} />
    </div>
  );
}

```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { approveInvestorAction, revokeInvestorAction } from "../../app/admin/actions";
import { ActionButton } from "./action-button";

export function WalletActionForm({ poolId, mode }: { poolId: string; mode: "approve" | "revoke" }) {
  const [wallet, setWallet] = useState("");
  const valid = /^[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,44}$/.test(wallet);
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <input
        value={wallet}
        onChange={(e) => setWallet(e.target.value.trim())}
        placeholder="Investor wallet address"
        aria-label={`Wallet to ${mode}`}
        className="h-9 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-2 font-mono text-[13px]"
      />
      <ActionButton
        label={mode === "approve" ? "Approve investor (all pools)" : "Revoke investor"}
        role="Compliance"
        action={() => (mode === "approve" ? approveInvestorAction(wallet) : revokeInvestorAction(poolId, wallet))}
        disabled={!valid}
        disabledReason={valid ? undefined : "Paste a wallet address"}
        confirmText={mode === "revoke" ? "Freeze this investor's Instrument account?" : undefined}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/admin/pool-console.tsx`:
```tsx
import {
  addProjectAction,
  attestStageAction,
  closeSubscriptionAction,
  disburseAllAction,
  fundRedemptionAction,
  openPoolAction,
  sweepAction,
} from "../../app/admin/actions";
import type { PoolDetail, ProjectWithFields } from "../../lib/chain/views";
import { formatDateUtc, formatUsd, relativeTime, shortKey } from "../../lib/format";
import { ExplorerLink } from "../explorer-link";
import { PoolStatusBadge } from "../status-badge";
import { ActionButton } from "./action-button";
import { SettlementForm } from "./settlement-form";
import { WalletActionForm } from "./wallet-action-form";

export function PoolConsole({ detail, unpooled, now }: { detail: PoolDetail; unpooled: ProjectWithFields[]; now: number }) {
  const { pool, projects } = detail;
  const id = pool.poolId;
  const s = pool.status;
  const full = BigInt(pool.raisedAmount) >= BigInt(pool.targetAmount) && BigInt(pool.targetAmount) > 0n;
  const closeReady = s === "Open" && (full || now >= pool.subscriptionCloseTs);
  const eligible = unpooled.filter((p) => p.crop === pool.crop && p.season === pool.season && p.fieldCount > 0 && p.verifiedFieldCount === p.fieldCount);
  const allDisbursed = projects.length > 0 && projects.every((p) => p.disbursed);
  const supply = BigInt(pool.instrument?.supply ?? "1");

  return (
    <section className="flex flex-col gap-6 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-6">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">{`${pool.instrument?.symbol ?? "Pool"} · #${id}`}</h2>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <PoolStatusBadge status={s} />
          <ExplorerLink kind="address" value={pool.address} />
        </div>
      </header>
      <p className="tabular text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        {`${formatUsd(pool.raisedAmount)} of ${formatUsd(pool.targetAmount)}. Closes ${formatDateUtc(pool.subscriptionCloseTs)} (${relativeTime(pool.subscriptionCloseTs, now * 1000)}). Matures ${formatDateUtc(pool.maturityTs)} (${relativeTime(pool.maturityTs, now * 1000)}).`}
      </p>

      <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">1. Build the pool</h3>
          {eligible.length === 0 ? <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No verified project with this crop and season is waiting.</p> : null}
          {eligible.map((p) => (
            <ActionButton
              key={p.address}
              label={`Add project ${shortKey(p.address)} (${formatUsd(p.requestedAmount)})`}
              role="Issuer"
              action={addProjectAction.bind(null, id, p.address)}
              disabled={s !== "Draft"}
              disabledReason={s !== "Draft" ? "Only a Draft pool accepts projects" : undefined}
            />
          ))}
          <ActionButton label="Open pool" role="Issuer" action={openPoolAction.bind(null, id)} disabled={s !== "Draft" || pool.projectCount === 0} disabledReason={s !== "Draft" ? "Already opened" : pool.projectCount === 0 ? "Add a project first" : undefined} />
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">2. Investors</h3>
          <WalletActionForm poolId={id} mode="approve" />
          <ActionButton label="Close subscription" role="Anyone" action={closeSubscriptionAction.bind(null, id)} disabled={!closeReady} disabledReason={closeReady ? undefined : "Available when the pool is full or after the close time"} />
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">3. Pay farmers and attest the season</h3>
          <ActionButton label="Disburse to every project" role="Issuer" action={disburseAllAction.bind(null, id)} disabled={s !== "Funded" || allDisbursed} disabledReason={s !== "Funded" ? "Needs a Funded pool" : allDisbursed ? "Every project is paid" : undefined} />
          {(["Sowing", "Vegetation", "Harvest"] as const).map((k) => (
            <ActionButton key={k} label={`Attest ${k.toLowerCase()} for all fields`} role="SCAS stub" action={attestStageAction.bind(null, id, k)} disabled={projects.length === 0} disabledReason={projects.length === 0 ? "No projects in this pool" : undefined} />
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">4. Settle and redeem</h3>
          {projects.map((p) => (
            <div key={p.address} className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{`Project ${shortKey(p.address)}${p.settlement ? `: ${p.settlement}` : ""}`}</span>
              <SettlementForm
                poolId={id}
                project={p.address}
                disabledReason={p.settlement ? "Already settled" : !p.disbursed ? "Disburse first" : !p.harvestAttested && now < pool.maturityTs ? "Attest harvest first" : undefined}
              />
            </div>
          ))}
          <ActionButton label="Fund redemption" role="Issuer" action={fundRedemptionAction.bind(null, id)} disabled={s !== "Funded"} disabledReason={s !== "Funded" ? "Needs a Funded pool" : undefined} />
          <ActionButton label="Sweep vault remainder" role="Issuer" action={sweepAction.bind(null, id)} confirmText="Return the vault remainder to the Issuer?" disabled={s !== "Redeeming" || supply !== 0n} disabledReason={s !== "Redeeming" ? "Needs a Redeeming pool" : supply !== 0n ? "Investors still hold the Instrument" : undefined} />
          <WalletActionForm poolId={id} mode="revoke" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```
Server actions passed to client components via `.bind(null, ...)` are the supported pattern; plain closures over server data are not.

export function LoginForm() {

  return (
    <form action={action} className="flex max-w-sm flex-col gap-3">
      <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
        Password
        <input name="password" type="password" required autoComplete="current-password" className="h-10 rounded-md border border-input bg-card px-3" />
      </label>
      {state.error ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">{state.error}</p> : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={pending} className="h-10 rounded-md bg-primary px-4 font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover disabled:opacity-60">
        Sign in

  );
}

`apps/web/src/app/admin/page.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { ActionButton } from "@/components/admin/action-button";
import { CreatePoolForm } from "@/components/admin/create-pool-form";
import { PoolConsole } from "@/components/admin/pool-console";
import { ScasStubBadge } from "@/components/scas-stub-badge";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/components/state";
import { ADMIN_COOKIE, isAdminToken } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { nextPoolId, roleMismatches } from "@/lib/chain/admin-ops";
import { formatHa } from "@/lib/format";
import { getReader, roleKeypair } from "@/lib/server/context";
import { serverEnv } from "@/lib/server/env";
import { logoutAction, verifyAllFieldsAction } from "./actions";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Demo console", robots: { index: false } };

function safeKey(role: "issuer" | "compliance" | "scas"): string | undefined {
  try {
    return roleKeypair(role).publicKey.toBase58();
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
}

export default async function AdminPage() {
  if (!serverEnv().ADMIN_PASSWORD) return <ErrorState title="Demo console is disabled" body="Set ADMIN_PASSWORD on the server to enable it." />;
  if (!isAdminToken(serverEnv().ADMIN_PASSWORD, (await cookies()).get(ADMIN_COOKIE)?.value)) redirect("/admin/login");

  const reader = getReader();
  let data;
  try {
    const [config, pools, unpooled] = await Promise.all([reader.config(), reader.listPools(), reader.unpooledProjects()]);
    const details = (await Promise.all(pools.map((p) => reader.getPool(p.poolId)))).filter((d) => d !== null);
    data = { config, pools, unpooled, details };
  } catch {
    return <ErrorState title="Could not load devnet state" body="Solana devnet did not answer." retryHref="/admin" />;
  }
  const keys = { issuer: safeKey("issuer"), compliance: safeKey("compliance"), scas: safeKey("scas") };
  const mismatches = roleMismatches(data.config, keys);
  const missing = Object.entries(keys).filter(([, v]) => !v).map(([k]) => k);
  const unverified = data.unpooled.flatMap((p) => p.fields.filter((f) => !f.verified));
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Demo console</h1>
        <form action={logoutAction}>
          <button type="submit" className="text-sm underline underline-offset-4">Sign out</button>
        </form>
      </header>

      {mismatches.length || missing.length ? (
        <div role="alert" className="rounded-lg border border-destructive bg-card p-4 text-sm">
          {missing.length ? <p>{`Missing keys on the server: ${missing.join(", ")}.`}</p> : null}
          {mismatches.map((m) => <p key={m}>{m}</p>)}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <section className="flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-6">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Field verification</h2>
          <ScasStubBadge />
        </div>
        {unverified.length ? (
          <ul className="tabular text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            {unverified.map((f) => <li key={f.address}>{`Field ${f.index + 1} of project ${f.project.slice(0, 4)}…, ${formatHa(f.areaHaX100)}`}</li>)}
          </ul>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Every field of every project waiting for a pool is verified.</p>
        )}
        <ActionButton label="Verify all fields" role="SCAS stub" action={verifyAllFieldsAction} disabled={unverified.length === 0} disabledReason={unverified.length === 0 ? "Nothing to verify" : undefined} />
      </section>

      <section className="flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Create a pool</h2>
        <CreatePoolForm defaultPoolId={nextPoolId(data.pools.map((p) => p.poolId))} />
      </section>

      {data.details.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No pools yet" body="Create one above, add a verified project, then open it." />
      ) : (
        data.details.map((d) => <PoolConsole key={d.pool.address} detail={d} unpooled={data.unpooled} now={now} />)
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 16: `scripts/demo-e2e.ts`: the main flow on devnet with fresh keypairs

The script runs spec section 5 end to end with fresh Producer and investor keypairs, prints numbered scenes with explorer links, and doubles as the demo-video script. It reuses the web app's library code, so what the video shows is what the app does.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/demo/fields.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/demo/main-flow.ts`
- Create: `scripts/demo-e2e.ts`
- Modify: `package.json` (root: add `"demo": "tsx scripts/demo-e2e.ts"` to `scripts`)
- Test: `apps/web/test/demo-flow.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `parseServerEnv` (Task 1), `parseKeypair` (Task 1), `createProgram`, `createChainReader` (Task 2), `S3Store`, `uploadPolygon` (Task 4), all Task 5 builders plus `sendServerTx`, `sendSkippingPreflight`, `explainChainError`, `attestStub`, `attestStage` (Task 6), `createPool`, `createPoolInput`, `addProject`, `openPool`, `approveInvestor`, `closeSubscription`, `disburseAll`, `recordSettlement`, `fundRedemption`, `sweep`, `nextPoolId`, `type OpsContext` (Task 8), `explorerTx`, `formatUsd`, `withYield` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - `fields.ts`: `DEMO_FIELDS: { name: string; geojson: object }[]` (the two sample fields from Task 4).
  - `main-flow.ts`: `DEMO_TARGET = 2_000_000_000n`, `demoSchedule(): { closeInMinutes: number; maturityInMinutes: number }` (4 and 6), `sceneLine(n: number, title: string, url?: string): string`, `runFromEnv(env?: Record<string, string | undefined>, log?: (line: string) => void): Promise<{ poolId: string; scenes: number }>`.
  - Root script `pnpm demo` (run under `infisical run --env=dev --`).

`apps/web/src/lib/demo/main-flow.ts`:
```ts
import { poolPda, projectPda } from "@f2f/sdk";
import { TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID, createTransferCheckedInstruction } from "@solana/spl-token";
import { Connection, Keypair, PublicKey, SystemProgram, type TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import {
  addProject,
  approveInvestor,
  closeSubscription,
  createPool,
  createPoolInput,
  disburseAll,
  fundRedemption,
  nextPoolId,
  openPool,
  recordSettlement,
  sweep,
  type OpsContext,
} from "../chain/admin-ops";
import { explainChainError } from "../chain/explain";
import { projectsInPool } from "../chain/filters";
import { createInstrumentAtaIx, createUsdcAtaIx, instrumentAta, mintUsdcIx, redeemIx, registerFieldIx, registerProjectIx, subscribeIx, usdcAta } from "../chain/ix";
import { createProgram } from "../chain/program";
import { createChainReader } from "../chain/reader";
import { sendServerTx, sendSkippingPreflight } from "../chain/send";
import { parseServerEnv } from "../env";
import { explorerAddress, explorerTx, formatUsd, withYield } from "../format";
import { parseKeypair } from "../keys";
import { uploadPolygon } from "../polygons";
import { attestStage, attestStub, type StubDeps } from "../scas-stub";
import { S3Store } from "../store";
import { DEMO_FIELDS } from "./fields";

export const DEMO_TARGET = 2_000_000_000n; // $2,000.00
export const demoSchedule = () => ({ closeInMinutes: 4, maturityInMinutes: 6 });

export const sceneLine = (n: number, title: string, url?: string) => (url ? `Scene ${n}: ${title}\n         ${url}` : `Scene ${n}: ${title}`);

const sleep = (ms: number) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

export async function runFromEnv(source: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env, log: (line: string) => void = console.log) {
  const env = parseServerEnv(source);
  const missingBucket = (["BUCKET_NAME", "BUCKET_ENDPOINT", "BUCKET_ACCESS_KEY_ID", "BUCKET_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY"] as const).filter((k) => !env[k]);
  if (missingBucket.length) throw new Error(`Set ${missingBucket.join(", ")} (Railway bucket credentials) before running the demo`);
  const cluster = env.SOLANA_CLUSTER;
  const tx = (sig: string) => explorerTx(sig, cluster);
  const connection = new Connection(env.SOLANA_RPC_URL, "confirmed");
  const programId = new PublicKey(env.F2F_PROGRAM_ID);
  const usdcMint = new PublicKey(env.USDC_MINT);
  const program = createProgram(connection, programId);
  const reader = createChainReader({ program, connection, usdcMint, ttlMs: 0 });
  const store = new S3Store({
    bucket: env.BUCKET_NAME as string,
    endpoint: env.BUCKET_ENDPOINT as string,
    region: env.BUCKET_REGION,
    accessKeyId: env.BUCKET_ACCESS_KEY_ID as string,
    secretAccessKey: env.BUCKET_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY as string,
    forcePathStyle: env.BUCKET_FORCE_PATH_STYLE,
  });
  const keys = {
    deployer: parseKeypair("F2F_DEPLOYER_KEYPAIR", env.F2F_DEPLOYER_KEYPAIR),
    issuer: parseKeypair("F2F_ISSUER_KEYPAIR", env.F2F_ISSUER_KEYPAIR),
    compliance: parseKeypair("F2F_COMPLIANCE_KEYPAIR", env.F2F_COMPLIANCE_KEYPAIR),
    scas: parseKeypair("SCAS_SECRET_KEY", env.SCAS_SECRET_KEY),
  };
  const send = (signers: Keypair[], ixs: TransactionInstruction[]) => sendServerTx(connection, signers, ixs);
  const ops: OpsContext = {
    program,
    usdcMint,
    roles: { issuer: keys.issuer.publicKey, compliance: keys.compliance.publicKey, deployer: keys.deployer.publicKey },
    send: (role, ixs) => send([keys[role]], ixs),
    loadPool: async (id) => program.account.pool.fetch(poolPda(programId, id)),
    loadProjects: async (pool) => (await program.account.farmProject.all([projectsInPool(pool)])).map((r) => ({ address: r.publicKey, account: r.account })),
    tokenBalance: async (a) => {
      try {
        return BigInt((await connection.getTokenAccountBalance(a, "confirmed")).value.amount);
      } catch {
        return 0n;
      }
    },
    accountExists: async (a) => (await connection.getAccountInfo(a, "confirmed")) !== null,
    now: () => Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
  };
  const stub: StubDeps = {
    program,
    readField: (a) => program.account.field.fetchNullable(a),
    send: (ixs) => send([keys.scas], ixs),
    store,
    scas: keys.scas.publicKey,
    publicBaseUrl: env.PUBLIC_BASE_URL,
    cluster,
  };

  let n = 0;
  const scene = (title: string, url?: string) => log(sceneLine(++n, title, url));

  // Fresh actors for every run, so the video never depends on old state.
  const producer = Keypair.generate();
  const alice = Keypair.generate(); // will pass KYC
  const bob = Keypair.generate(); // never passes KYC
  // Role keys pay rent too (compliance: InvestorRecord, SCAS: Attestations, Issuer: pool accounts).
  const roleTopUps = [];
  for (const k of [keys.issuer, keys.compliance, keys.scas]) {
    if ((await connection.getBalance(k.publicKey, "confirmed")) < 200_000_000) {
      roleTopUps.push(SystemProgram.transfer({ fromPubkey: keys.deployer.publicKey, toPubkey: k.publicKey, lamports: 300_000_000 }));
    }
  }
  const fund = await send([keys.deployer], [
    ...roleTopUps,
    ...[producer, alice, bob].map((k) => SystemProgram.transfer({ fromPubkey: keys.deployer.publicKey, toPubkey: k.publicKey, lamports: 200_000_000 })),
    ...[alice, bob].flatMap((k) => [createUsdcAtaIx(keys.deployer.publicKey, k.publicKey, usdcMint), mintUsdcIx(usdcMint, usdcAta(usdcMint, k.publicKey), keys.deployer.publicKey, 5_000_000_000n)]),
  ]);
  scene(`Fresh Producer ${producer.publicKey.toBase58()} and investors Alice and Bob funded with devnet SOL and test USDC`, tx(fund));

  // 1. Producer registers a Farm Project with two fields.
  const uploads = [];
  for (const f of DEMO_FIELDS) uploads.push(await uploadPolygon(store, f.geojson));
  const projectId = BigInt(Date.now());
  const project = projectPda(programId, producer.publicKey, projectId);
  const reg = await send([producer], [
    await registerProjectIx(program, { producer: producer.publicKey, projectId, crop: "wheat", season: 2027, expectedKg: 400_000n, requestedAmount: DEMO_TARGET }),
    ...(await Promise.all(uploads.map((u, index) => registerFieldIx(program, { producer: producer.publicKey, project, index, polygonHash: u.polygonHash, areaHaX100: u.areaHaX100 })))),
  ]);
  scene("Producer registers a wheat project with two fields; boundaries hashed with the sdk polygon hash", tx(reg));

  // 2. SCAS (stub) verifies both fields.
  for (let i = 0; i < uploads.length; i++) {
    const r = await attestStub(stub, { project, fieldIndex: i, kind: "FieldVerified" });
    scene(`SCAS stub attests field ${i + 1}: FieldVerified, Pass. Evidence ${r.evidenceUri}`, tx(r.signature));
  }

  // 3. Issuer creates WHEAT-2027, adds the project and opens the pool.
  const poolId = nextPoolId((await program.account.pool.all()).map((p) => p.account.poolId.toString()));
  const created = await createPool(ops, createPoolInput.parse({ poolId, crop: "wheat", season: 2027, name: "Wheat 2027", symbol: "WHEAT-2027", yieldBps: 800, premiumBps: 550, ...demoSchedule() }), env.PUBLIC_BASE_URL);
  scene(`Issuer creates pool ${poolId} (WHEAT-2027, fixed yield 8.0%, premium 5.5%), maturity in ${demoSchedule().maturityInMinutes} minutes`, tx(created.signature));
  scene("Issuer adds the verified project", tx(await addProject(ops, BigInt(poolId), project)));
  scene("Issuer opens the pool for subscription", tx(await openPool(ops, BigInt(poolId))));
  const pool = await program.account.pool.fetch(poolPda(programId, BigInt(poolId)));
  const poolKey = poolPda(programId, BigInt(poolId));
  const holder = (k: Keypair, amount: bigint) => ({ investor: k.publicKey, pool: poolKey, mint: pool.mint, vault: pool.vault, usdcMint, amount });

  // 4. Bob has no KYC: the program rejects his subscription on-chain (no InvestorRecord).
  const rejected = await sendSkippingPreflight(connection, [bob], [await subscribeIx(program, holder(bob, 100_000_000n))]);
  scene(`Bob (no KYC) tries to subscribe $100.00 and fails: ${explainChainError({ logs: rejected.logs, error: rejected.err }).title}`, tx(rejected.signature));

  // Compliance approves Alice once for every pool; her account is created and thawed when she subscribes.
  scene("Compliance approves Alice (platform-level KYC, valid for every pool)", tx(await approveInvestor(ops, alice.publicKey)));
  scene(`Alice subscribes ${formatUsd(DEMO_TARGET)}: her WHEAT-2027 account is created and thawed, USDC in, WHEAT-2027 out, one transaction`, tx(await send([alice], [await subscribeIx(program, holder(alice, DEMO_TARGET))])));

  // The token itself enforces compliance: Bob can open a WHEAT-2027 account, but it starts frozen,
  // so Alice cannot send him the Instrument.
  await send([bob], [createInstrumentAtaIx(bob.publicKey, bob.publicKey, pool.mint)]);
  const frozen = await sendSkippingPreflight(connection, [alice], [
    createTransferCheckedInstruction(instrumentAta(pool.mint, alice.publicKey), pool.mint, instrumentAta(pool.mint, bob.publicKey), alice.publicKey, 1_000_000n, 6, [], TOKEN_2022_PROGRAM_ID),
  ]);
  scene(`Alice tries to send $1.00 of WHEAT-2027 to Bob: ${explainChainError({ logs: frozen.logs, error: frozen.err }).title}`, tx(frozen.signature));

  // 5. Pool is funded; Issuer disburses to the Producer.
  scene("Pool reaches its target; subscription closes as Funded", tx(await closeSubscription(ops, BigInt(poolId))));
  for (const s of await disburseAll(ops, BigInt(poolId))) scene(`Issuer disburses ${formatUsd(DEMO_TARGET)} to the Producer`, tx(s));

  // 6. SCAS (stub) attests the season.
  const detail = await reader.getPool(poolId);
  for (const kind of ["Sowing", "Vegetation", "Harvest"] as const) {
    const r = await attestStage(stub, detail?.projects ?? [], kind);
    if (r.failures.length) throw new Error(`${kind}: ${r.failures[0]?.error}`);
    scene(`SCAS stub attests ${kind} for ${r.results.length} fields`, tx(r.results[0]!.signature));
  }

  // 7. Settlement, redemption funding, investor redemption.
  scene("Issuer records settlement: volume shortfall covered from other fields", tx(await recordSettlement(ops, BigInt(poolId), project, "CoveredFromOtherFields")));
  let funded = await fundRedemption(ops, BigInt(poolId));
  scene(`Issuer deposits principal plus yield: ${formatUsd(funded.deposited)}`, tx(funded.signatures.at(-1)!));
  while (Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) < pool.maturityTs.toNumber()) {
    log(`         waiting for maturity (${pool.maturityTs.toNumber() - Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)} s)`);
    await sleep(15_000);
  }
  if (!funded.maturityPassed) {
    funded = await fundRedemption(ops, BigInt(poolId));
    scene("Maturity passed; the redemption check opens the pool for redemption", tx(funded.signatures.at(-1)!));
  }
  const before = await ops.tokenBalance(usdcAta(usdcMint, alice.publicKey));
  const redeemed = await send([alice], [createUsdcAtaIx(alice.publicKey, alice.publicKey, usdcMint), await redeemIx(program, holder(alice, DEMO_TARGET))]);
  const after = await ops.tokenBalance(usdcAta(usdcMint, alice.publicKey));
  scene(`Alice redeems ${formatUsd(DEMO_TARGET)} of WHEAT-2027 for ${formatUsd(after - before)} (expected ${formatUsd(withYield(DEMO_TARGET, 800))})`, tx(redeemed));
  scene("Issuer sweeps the empty vault", tx(await sweep(ops, BigInt(poolId))));
  scene(`Done. Pool ${poolId} is Redeeming with zero supply`, explorerAddress(poolKey.toBase58(), cluster));
  return { poolId, scenes: n };
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/instrumentation.ts`, `apps/web/instrumentation-client.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/global-error.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/error.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/observability.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/test/observability.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `publicEnv` (Task 1); `track` callers from Tasks 9, 13, 14 (they start sending once PostHog is initialized here).
- Produces: `scrubEvent<T extends SentryLikeEvent>(event: T): T` (drops cookies, auth headers, query strings, user, and request bodies), `posthogOptions(appEnv: "dev" | "prod", host: string): PostHogConfigSubset`, `SENTRY_BASE: { tracesSampleRate: 0; sendDefaultPii: false }` (omit `sendDefaultPii` if `@sentry/nextjs` 11 types reject it, as the worker found with `@sentry/node` 11).

No `withSentryConfig` wrapper in `next.config.ts`: GlitchTip needs no source-map upload, and the wrapper is the part of `@sentry/nextjs` most likely to break across Next majors. Instrumentation files are enough for error capture.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`apps/web/src/lib/observability.ts`:
```ts
export interface SentryLikeEvent {
  request?: { url?: string; cookies?: unknown; headers?: Record<string, string>; data?: unknown; query_string?: unknown };
  user?: unknown;
  [k: string]: unknown;
}

const DROP_HEADERS = new Set(["cookie", "authorization", "x-forwarded-for", "x-real-ip"]);

/** GlitchTip must never receive the admin cookie, KYC form values or wallet-identifying query strings. */
export function scrubEvent<T extends SentryLikeEvent>(event: T): T {
  if (event.request) {
    const { url, headers } = event.request;
    event.request = {
      ...(url ? { url: url.split("?")[0] } : {}),
      ...(headers ? { headers: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(headers).filter(([k]) => !DROP_HEADERS.has(k.toLowerCase()))) } : {}),
    };
  }
  delete event.user;
  return event;
}

export const SENTRY_BASE = { tracesSampleRate: 0 } as const;

interface PostHogLike {
  register(p: Record<string, unknown>): void;
  setPersonProperties(p: Record<string, unknown>): void;
}

export function posthogOptions(appEnv: "dev" | "prod", host: string) {
  return {
    api_host: host,
    defaults: "2025-05-24" as const,
    person_profiles: "always" as const,
    disable_session_recording: true,
    loaded: (ph: PostHogLike) => {
      // Global rule: environment targeting uses the person property app_env (dev / prod).
      ph.register({ app_env: appEnv });
      ph.setPersonProperties({ app_env: appEnv });
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/web/instrumentation.ts`:
```ts
import * as Sentry from "@sentry/nextjs";
import { SENTRY_BASE, scrubEvent } from "./src/lib/observability";

export async function register() {
  const dsn = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN;
  if (!dsn) return;
  Sentry.init({
    dsn,
    environment: process.env.SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT ?? "development",
    ...SENTRY_BASE,
    beforeSend: (event) => scrubEvent(event as never),
  });
}

export const onRequestError = Sentry.captureRequestError;
```

`apps/web/instrumentation-client.ts`:
```ts
import * as Sentry from "@sentry/nextjs";
import posthog from "posthog-js";
import { SENTRY_BASE, posthogOptions, scrubEvent } from "./src/lib/observability";
import { publicEnv } from "./src/lib/public-env";

if (publicEnv.NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN) {
  Sentry.init({
    dsn: publicEnv.NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN,
    environment: publicEnv.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV === "prod" ? "devnet" : "development",
    ...SENTRY_BASE,
    beforeSend: (event) => scrubEvent(event as never),
  });
}

if (publicEnv.NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_KEY) {
  posthog.init(
    publicEnv.NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_KEY,
    posthogOptions(publicEnv.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV, publicEnv.NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_HOST) as never,
  );
}

export const onRouterTransitionStart = Sentry.captureRouterTransitionStart;
```
The deployed app sets `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV=prod` and server `SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT=devnet`, so both sides report environment `devnet` in GlitchTip; local runs report `development`.

`apps/web/src/app/error.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import * as Sentry from "@sentry/nextjs";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/state";

export default function RouteError({ error, reset }: { error: Error & { digest?: string }; reset: () => void }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    Sentry.captureException(error);
  }, [error]);
  return <ErrorState title="Something went wrong" body="The error was reported. Try again, or go back to the pools." onRetry={reset} />;
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/global-error.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import * as Sentry from "@sentry/nextjs";
import { useEffect } from "react";

export default function GlobalError({ error, reset }: { error: Error & { digest?: string }; reset: () => void }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    Sentry.captureException(error);
  }, [error]);
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <body style={{ fontFamily: "system-ui", padding: 32, background: "#f5f2ea", color: "#0d2019" }}>
        <h1>Something went wrong</h1>
        <p>The error was reported.</p>
        <button type="button" onClick={reset}>
          Try again
        </button>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}
```
Delete `apps/web/src/app/pools/error.tsx` from Task 11: the root `error.tsx` now covers every route and also reports to GlitchTip.
