### Task 18: Docker image and Railway service

**Skills:** `railway:use-railway`.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore` (repo root)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pnpm --filter @f2f/web build` (`output: "standalone"`), `GET /api/health`.
- Produces: Railway service `web` in project `field-to-finance` (id `6be3d83c-91d0-466e-9521-40b7c80863fc`, environment `production`) with a public domain, healthcheck `/api/health`, bucket variables referenced from `f2f-evidence`, secrets from Infisical.

Environment variables of the `web` service:

| Variable | Source | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `RAILWAY_DOCKERFILE_PATH` | literal | `apps/web/Dockerfile` |
| `SOLANA_RPC_URL`, `SOLANA_CLUSTER` | Infisical / literal | devnet RPC; `devnet` |
| `F2F_PROGRAM_ID`, `USDC_MINT` | Infisical | program id and test USDC mint |
| `F2F_DEPLOYER_KEYPAIR`, `F2F_ISSUER_KEYPAIR`, `F2F_COMPLIANCE_KEYPAIR`, `SCAS_SECRET_KEY` | Infisical | JSON byte arrays, server only |
| `BUCKET_NAME`, `BUCKET_ENDPOINT`, `BUCKET_REGION`, `BUCKET_ACCESS_KEY_ID`, `BUCKET_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` | Railway references | `${{f2f-evidence.BUCKET}}`, `${{f2f-evidence.ENDPOINT}}`, `${{f2f-evidence.REGION}}`, `${{f2f-evidence.ACCESS_KEY_ID}}`, `${{f2f-evidence.SECRET_ACCESS_KEY}}` |
| `BUCKET_FORCE_PATH_STYLE` | literal | `false` (virtual-host style) |
| `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` | Railway reference | `https://${{RAILWAY_PUBLIC_DOMAIN}}`; at most 50 characters |
| `ADMIN_PASSWORD` | Infisical | at least 8 characters |
| `FAUCET_USDC` | literal | `1000` |
| `SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT` | literal | `devnet` |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_CLUSTER` | literal | public devnet RPC (never a keyed RPC); `devnet` |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIVY_APP_ID`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_KEY`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN` | Infisical | build-time; passed as Docker build args |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_HOST`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV` | literal | `https://eu.i.posthog.com`; `prod` |

`NEXT_PUBLIC_*` values are inlined at build time. Railway passes service variables to the Docker build only for names declared with `ARG` in the Dockerfile, so the Dockerfile declares each of them.

```bash

```

Role keys pay rent and fees from their own balance (compliance: InvestorRecord; SCAS: Attestations; Issuer: pools and vaults). Top them up from the deployer once:
```bash
for k in F2F_ISSUER_KEYPAIR F2F_COMPLIANCE_KEYPAIR SCAS_SECRET_KEY; do
  infisical secrets get "$k" --env=dev --plain --silent > /tmp/f2f-role.json
  PUB=$(solana-keygen pubkey /tmp/f2f-role.json); rm /tmp/f2f-role.json
  infisical secrets get F2F_DEPLOYER_KEYPAIR --env=dev --plain --silent > /tmp/f2f-dep.json
  solana transfer --url devnet --keypair /tmp/f2f-dep.json --allow-unfunded-recipient "$PUB" 1 >/dev/null && echo "funded $PUB"
  rm /tmp/f2f-dep.json
done
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the Dockerfile and ignore file**

`apps/web/Dockerfile`:
```dockerfile
# Build context: repository root. Railway: RAILWAY_DOCKERFILE_PATH=apps/web/Dockerfile.
FROM node:22-slim AS build
ENV PNPM_HOME=/pnpm PATH=/pnpm:$PATH CI=true NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 COREPACK_ENABLE_DOWNLOAD_PROMPT=0
RUN corepack enable
WORKDIR /repo
COPY . .
RUN pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@f2f/web..."
ARG NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL
ARG NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_CLUSTER
ARG NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIVY_APP_ID
ARG NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_KEY
ARG NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_HOST
ARG NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV
ARG NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN
ENV NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL=$NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL \
    NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_CLUSTER=$NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_CLUSTER \
    NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIVY_APP_ID=$NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIVY_APP_ID \
    NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_KEY=$NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_KEY \
    NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_HOST=$NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_HOST \
    NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV=$NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV \
    NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN=$NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN
RUN pnpm --filter @f2f/web run build

FROM node:22-slim
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=8080 HOSTNAME=0.0.0.0 NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=build /repo/apps/web/.next/standalone ./
COPY --from=build /repo/apps/web/.next/static ./apps/web/.next/static
COPY --from=build /repo/apps/web/public ./apps/web/public
USER node
EXPOSE 8080
CMD ["node", "apps/web/server.js"]
```

```
**/.next
anchor/target

id.json
.superpowers
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create the Railway service and set variables**

```bash
railway link --project 6be3d83c-91d0-466e-9521-40b7c80863fc --environment production
railway add --service web
railway domain --service web          # prints the public domain; it must keep PUBLIC_BASE_URL ≤ 50 chars
railway variables --service web --skip-deploys \
  --set "RAILWAY_DOCKERFILE_PATH=apps/web/Dockerfile" \
  --set "SOLANA_CLUSTER=devnet" --set "NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_CLUSTER=devnet" \
  --set "NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL=https://api.devnet.solana.com" \
  --set 'BUCKET_NAME=${{f2f-evidence.BUCKET}}' --set 'BUCKET_ENDPOINT=${{f2f-evidence.ENDPOINT}}' \
  --set 'BUCKET_REGION=${{f2f-evidence.REGION}}' --set 'BUCKET_ACCESS_KEY_ID=${{f2f-evidence.ACCESS_KEY_ID}}' \
  --set 'BUCKET_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY=${{f2f-evidence.SECRET_ACCESS_KEY}}' --set "BUCKET_FORCE_PATH_STYLE=false" \
  --set 'PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://${{RAILWAY_PUBLIC_DOMAIN}}' \
  --set "FAUCET_USDC=1000" --set "SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT=devnet" \
  --set "NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_HOST=https://eu.i.posthog.com" --set "NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV=prod"
# Secrets: copied from Infisical without printing them.
for k in SOLANA_RPC_URL F2F_PROGRAM_ID USDC_MINT F2F_DEPLOYER_KEYPAIR F2F_ISSUER_KEYPAIR F2F_COMPLIANCE_KEYPAIR SCAS_SECRET_KEY \
         ADMIN_PASSWORD NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIVY_APP_ID NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_KEY NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN; do
  v="$(infisical secrets get "$k" --env=dev --plain --silent 2>/dev/null)"
  [ -n "$v" ] && railway variables --service web --skip-deploys --set "$k=$v" >/dev/null && echo "set $k"
done
unset v
```

If `--skip-deploys` is not supported by the installed CLI (5.57.2 supports it), drop the flag. Do not create `railway.json`/`railway.toml`.

`docs/architecture/decisions/0012-scas-external-provider-stub.md`:
```markdown
---
status: Accepted
date: "2026-10-03"
---

# SCAS is an external provider; the MVP uses a stub

## Context

ADR 0008 had our own worker pull Sentinel-2 NDVI and sign Attestations. SCAS is meant to be an independent verifier, and the team will integrate an external SCAS provider rather than operate one. Building and running a satellite pipeline before the 2026-10-12 deadline would cost days that the web app and the demo need.

## Decision

- SCAS is an external provider, integrated after the hackathon.
- For the MVP the web server runs a SCAS stub (`apps/web/src/lib/scas-stub.ts`). It writes an evidence document that states it is a stub ("External SCAS provider integration pending"), stores it in the Railway bucket under its SHA-256, and signs `record_attestation` with the SCAS key (`Config.scas`): verdict `Pass`, NDVI 0, scene date = now.
- Every Attestation shown in the UI carries the badge "Verified by SCAS stub: external provider pending".
- The Program, the Attestation account layout and the evidence hash and URI scheme do not change, so a real provider can replace the stub by signing with its own key after `Config.scas` is rotated.

## Consequences

- The demo shows the full Attestation mechanics on-chain (signer check, hash-linked public evidence, `FieldVerified` gating `add_project`, `Harvest` gating settlement) without satellite data.
- No NDVI chart and no Sentinel-2 numbers in the MVP; spec success criterion 2 changes accordingly.
- No separate SCAS worker service: ADR 0010's second Railway service is not deployed for the MVP. `apps/scas-worker` stays in the repo, stopped (see its plan).
- The SCAS key now lives in the web service's environment. It is still separate from the Issuer and admin keys.

## Alternatives considered

- Finish the Sentinel-2 worker (ADR 0008): real data, about three more days of work before the deadline.
- Mock Attestations in the UI only: faster, but nothing on-chain; judges could not verify anything.

## Related code

- `apps/web/src/lib/scas-stub.ts`
- `apps/web/src/app/api/evidence/[hash]/route.ts`

## Related docs

- [ADR 0008](0008-scas-sentinel2-signed-attestations.md) (superseded)
- [Field verification flow](../../flows/field-verification.md)
```

```markdown
### 4.4 SCAS (stub until the external provider is integrated)

SCAS is an external provider ([ADR 0012](../architecture/decisions/0012-scas-external-provider-stub.md)). For the MVP the web server runs a stub:

- Evidence: a canonical JSON document `{ schema, provider: "scas-stub", note: "External SCAS provider integration pending", kind, verdict: "Pass", polygonHash, fieldAreaHaX100, recordedAt, subject, signer }`, stored in the bucket under its SHA-256 and served at `GET /api/evidence/<hash>` after a hash check.
- Attestation: `record_attestation` signed with the SCAS key, verdict `Pass`, `ndvi_mean_x10k = 0`, `scene_date = now`. Attestations on one Field are sent one at a time because their addresses depend on `attestation_count`.
- Triggers: the admin console's "Verify all fields" (`FieldVerified` for every unverified field not yet in a pool) and "Attest season stage" (`Sowing`, `Vegetation` or `Harvest` for every field of a pool), and the end-to-end script.
- Field polygons uploaded by farmers are stored the same way under `polygons/<polygonHash>.json`, where `polygonHash` is the `@f2f/sdk` hash recorded by `register_field`.
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the system doc**

`docs/systems/web.md`:
```markdown
# Web app

## Purpose

`apps/web` is the only deployed service. It shows Pools, Fields and Attestations from devnet, lets Producers register fields and investors subscribe with their own wallets, and runs the demo console where server-held keys act as Issuer, compliance and the SCAS stub.

## Questions this doc answers

- Where do privileged keys live and who can use them?
- How does a user transaction get built, signed and confirmed?
- How are reads cached and when are they refreshed?
- What does the SCAS stub write and sign?

## Scope

Pages, route handlers, server actions, the read layer over Program accounts, server-built transactions, the SCAS stub, polygon and evidence storage, the test-USDC faucet, mock KYC, GlitchTip and PostHog.

## Non-scope

- Program rules: see [program.md](program.md).
- Real KYC, the fiat on-ramp and a real SCAS provider.

## Key concepts

Pool, Instrument, Farm Project, Field, Attestation, KYC Thaw, Shortfall. See the [glossary](../glossary.md).

## How the system works

- **Reads.** Server components call a `ChainReader` that uses `program.account.*.all()` with memcmp filters (projects by producer or pool, fields by project, attestations by field) and decodes Token-2022 metadata for the Instrument name and symbol. Results are cached in process for 5 seconds; every server-sent or server-confirmed transaction clears the cache.
- **User transactions.** The browser posts intent to `/api/tx/<action>` (`register-project`, `subscribe`, `refund`, `redeem`). The server builds a v0 transaction with `accountsStrict`, simulates it without signatures, and returns either the unsigned transaction or a plain-language error. The wallet signs and sends; the browser posts the signature to `/api/tx/confirm`, which confirms on the server's RPC and refreshes reads. A program rejection, such as a subscribe from a wallet without KYC, is shown before the wallet opens.
- **Wallets.** Investors use the Solana wallet adapter (Phantom and other Wallet Standard wallets). Producers sign in with email through Privy and get an embedded Solana wallet; without `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRIVY_APP_ID` the farmer view falls back to the wallet adapter.
- **Server-signed actions.** Admin server actions and the KYC and faucet routes sign with keys from the environment: Issuer (pool lifecycle), compliance (`approve_investor`, `revoke_investor`), SCAS (stub attestations), deployer (test-USDC mint authority, SOL top-ups).
- **SCAS stub.** See [ADR 0012](../architecture/decisions/0012-scas-external-provider-stub.md) and the [field verification flow](../flows/field-verification.md).

## Important flows

- [Primary subscription](../flows/primary-subscription.md)
- [Field verification](../flows/field-verification.md)
- [Pool lifecycle](../flows/pool-lifecycle.md)

## Data and state

- No database. State is Program accounts on devnet.
- Bucket `f2f-evidence`: `polygons/<sha256>.json` (canonical field polygons) and `evidence/<sha256>.json` (stub evidence). Keys are content hashes; objects never change.
- In-process: the read cache, the SCAS send queue, the faucet cooldown. All reset on restart, which is safe.

## Interfaces and entry points

| Route | Who | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `/`, `/pools`, `/pools/[poolId]`, `/fields/[address]` | public | read views |
| `/farmer`, `/portfolio` | Producer, investor | write flows signed in the browser |
| `/admin`, `/admin/login` | demo operator | server actions with server-held keys |
| `GET /api/health` | Railway | liveness, no devnet call |
| `POST /api/polygons`, `GET /api/polygons/[hash]` | farmer view, field page | polygon upload and hashed read |
| `GET /api/evidence/[hash]` | anyone | stub evidence, hash-checked |
| `GET /api/instrument/[poolId]` | wallets | Token-2022 metadata URI target |
| `POST /api/tx/[action]`, `POST /api/tx/confirm` | browser | server-built user transactions |
| `POST /api/kyc`, `POST /api/faucet` | investor and farmer views | mock KYC approval, test funds |
| `GET /api/producer/[wallet]`, `GET /api/investor/[wallet]` | browser | wallet-scoped reads |

## Dependencies

Solana devnet RPC, the `f2f` Program, `@f2f/sdk`, Railway bucket, Privy, GlitchTip (org `personal`, project `f2f-web`), PostHog EU (project `field-to-finance`), Esri World Imagery tiles.

## Downstream effects

Renaming a Program instruction, account field or seed breaks `src/lib/chain/ix.ts`, `views.ts` and `filters.ts`. Changing the polygon normalization in `@f2f/sdk` orphans stored polygons.

## Invariants and assumptions

- Privileged keys are only read in server code (`src/lib/server/*`, route handlers, server actions).
- `register_field` stores the `@f2f/sdk` polygon hash of the uploaded polygon, and the polygon is stored under that hash before the transaction is built.
- Evidence is stored before the Attestation that points at it is sent.
- Stub attestations are serialized per process.
- `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` is at most 50 characters so evidence and metadata URIs fit 128 bytes.

## Error handling

Program and wallet errors go through `explainChainError` into a title, a detail and one way out (`kyc`, `faucet`, `switch-network`, `retry`). Unexpected server errors go to GlitchTip with cookies, auth headers, query strings, request bodies and user data removed.

## Security and privacy notes

- The demo console is protected by `ADMIN_PASSWORD` (HMAC session cookie, httpOnly, SameSite strict). It is a demo control, not production auth.
- Mock KYC values are validated and discarded; nothing personal is stored, logged or sent to analytics.
- Evidence and polygons are public. Do not upload real pilot contours without the farmers' consent.
- The faucet is rate-limited per wallet and mints only test USDC.

## Observability and debugging

- GlitchTip `f2f-web`: `GET /api/0/projects/personal/f2f-web/issues/?query=is:unresolved`.
- PostHog events: `tx_succeeded`, `tx_failed` (with the explained error code), `kyc_approved`, `faucet_used`, `fields_uploaded`, `farmer_login`; person property `app_env`.
- The admin console warns when a role key in the environment differs from Config.

## Testing notes

`pnpm nx run web:test` runs Vitest (node and jsdom). Fixtures encode accounts with the real IDL. `pnpm demo` runs the whole flow on devnet.

## Common pitfalls

- Phantom on mainnet: transactions fail with "Blockhash not found"; the UI asks to switch to Devnet.
- Privy must sign on `solana:devnet`; otherwise it submits to mainnet.
- `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` variables are build-time; changing them on Railway needs a rebuild.
- Demo Pools need a maturity a few minutes out.

## Source map

- [src/lib/chain/reader.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/chain/reader.ts) — reads and cache
- [src/lib/chain/ix.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/chain/ix.ts) — instruction builders
- [src/lib/chain/user-tx.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/chain/user-tx.ts) — server-built user transactions
- [src/lib/chain/admin-ops.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/chain/admin-ops.ts) — Issuer and compliance operations
- [src/lib/chain/explain.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/chain/explain.ts) — plain-language errors
- [src/lib/scas-stub.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/scas-stub.ts) — SCAS stub
- [src/lib/server/context.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/server/context.ts) — keys and singletons
- [src/app/admin/actions.ts](../../apps/web/src/app/admin/actions.ts) — demo console actions
- [src/lib/demo/main-flow.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/demo/main-flow.ts) — end-to-end script

## Related docs

- [Program](program.md), [MVP design](../specs/2026-10-03-mvp-design.md), [ADR 0005](../architecture/decisions/0005-wallet-model.md), [ADR 0011](../architecture/decisions/0011-platform-level-kyc.md), [ADR 0012](../architecture/decisions/0012-scas-external-provider-stub.md)
```

`docs/flows/primary-subscription.md`:
```markdown
# Primary subscription

## Purpose

An investor gets verified once and buys a Pool's Instrument with USDC in one transaction. This is the demo's compliance beat: a wallet without KYC cannot get the Instrument.

## Trigger

The investor presses Subscribe on an Open pool's page.

## Participants

Investor wallet (Phantom), web app (`/api/tx/subscribe`, `/api/tx/confirm`, `/api/kyc`, `/api/faucet`), compliance key on the server, the Program, Token-2022, devnet.

## Step-by-step flow

1. The investor connects a devnet wallet. The invest panel reads `/api/investor/<wallet>`: KYC status, test USDC, positions.
2. Optional: "Get 1,000 test USDC" posts to `/api/faucet`; the deployer mints test USDC and tops up SOL.
3. The investor enters an amount up to the pool's remaining capacity and presses Subscribe.
4. The server builds `subscribe(amount)` and simulates it.
   - No InvestorRecord: simulation fails with `AccountNotInitialized` on `investor_record`. The panel shows "This wallet hasn't passed KYC" with "Complete KYC". The wallet never opens.
5. KYC: the mock form posts to `/api/kyc`. The server validates and discards the values and signs `approve_investor` with the compliance key. One approval covers every pool ([ADR 0011](../architecture/decisions/0011-platform-level-kyc.md)).
6. Subscribe again. Simulation passes; the wallet signs and sends. In one transaction the Program creates the investor's Token-2022 Instrument account if needed, thaws it because the record is approved, moves USDC to the vault and mints the same amount of Instrument.
7. The browser posts the signature to `/api/tx/confirm`; the server confirms, clears its read cache, and the panel shows the explorer link.

## Data and state changes

InvestorRecord created or set approved (step 5); Instrument account created and thawed, USDC moved, Instrument minted, `raised_amount` increased (step 6).

## Success behavior

The investor holds Instrument equal to the USDC paid; the pool page shows the new raised amount.

## Failure behavior

- `InvestorNotApproved` (revoked): same KYC message.
- `ExceedsTarget`, `SubscriptionClosed`, `PoolNotOpen`: plain messages; the amount field is pre-checked against remaining capacity.
- Missing USDC or SOL: "Get test funds".
- Wallet on mainnet: "Your wallet is on a different network".
- Cancelled signature: nothing sent.

## External dependencies

Devnet RPC, Phantom or another Wallet Standard wallet, Railway (web app).

## Invariants and assumptions

- `raised_amount <= target_amount`.
- A wallet without an approved record never holds a thawed Instrument account.

## Security and privacy notes

Compliance signs on the server only. No personal data is stored on-chain or in the app.

## Observability and debugging

PostHog `tx_failed` with `code=KycRequired` counts the demo beat; GlitchTip catches server errors. Program logs are behind "Program logs" in the error panel.

## Testing notes

`test/explain.test.ts`, `test/user-tx.test.ts`, `test/invest-panel.test.tsx`; `pnpm demo` scenes for Bob (no KYC) and Alice.

## Source map

- [invest-panel.tsx](../../apps/web/src/components/pool/invest-panel.tsx)
- [user-tx.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/chain/user-tx.ts)
- [api/kyc/route.ts](../../apps/web/src/app/api/kyc/route.ts)
- [explain.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/chain/explain.ts)

## Related docs

- [Web app](../systems/web.md), [Program](../systems/program.md), [Pool lifecycle](pool-lifecycle.md)
```

`docs/flows/field-verification.md`:
```markdown
# Field verification

## Purpose

Takes a Producer's field from an uploaded boundary to a `FieldVerified` Attestation on Solana, which `add_project` requires.

## Trigger

A Producer uploads GeoJSON in the farmer view.

## Participants

Producer (Privy embedded wallet), web app (`/api/polygons`, `/api/tx/register-project`, admin console), bucket `f2f-evidence`, SCAS stub with the SCAS key, the Program.

## Step-by-step flow

1. The browser splits the file into one Polygon per field (MultiPolygon is rejected) and posts each to `/api/polygons`.
2. The server normalizes the polygon with `@f2f/sdk` (unwrap Feature, drop altitude, round to 7 decimals, close rings), rejects self-crossing or out-of-range boundaries, computes the geodesic area, and stores `polygons/<polygonHash>.json`.
3. The Producer confirms declared areas and the financing request; the server builds `register_project` plus one `register_field(polygonHash, area_ha_x100)` per field and simulates it; the Producer signs.
4. In the admin console, "Verify all fields" runs the SCAS stub for every unverified field of every project not in a pool.
5. For each field the stub reads `attestation_count`, writes the stub evidence to `evidence/<sha256>.json`, then signs `record_attestation(FieldVerified, Pass, hash, uri, 0, now)`. The Program sets the field verified.
6. The field page shows the boundary over satellite imagery and the timeline with the evidence link and the stub badge.

## Data and state changes

FarmProject and Field accounts (step 3); Attestation account, Field `verified`, project `verified_field_count` (step 5); bucket objects (steps 2 and 5).

## Success behavior

Every field shows "Field verified · Pass" and the project shows "Verified. Waiting to join a pool".

## Failure behavior

- Invalid boundary: the upload is refused with the reason; nothing is stored.
- Boundary not uploaded: `register-project` returns 422.
- Two attestations at once on one field: serialized; both land with consecutive sequences.
- RPC failure during a batch: the batch continues and reports the failed fields.

## External dependencies

Devnet RPC, Railway bucket, Privy.

## Invariants and assumptions

- The on-chain polygon hash equals the stored polygon's SHA-256.
- Evidence exists before its Attestation.
- The stub always says it is a stub ([ADR 0012](../architecture/decisions/0012-scas-external-provider-stub.md)).

## Security and privacy notes

Field boundaries become public through `/api/polygons/<hash>`. Use demo boundaries unless the farmers agree.

## Observability and debugging

The admin console lists unverified fields; batch failures name the field. GlitchTip reports server errors.

## Testing notes

`test/polygons.test.ts`, `test/geojson.test.ts`, `test/scas-stub.test.ts`, `test/user-tx.test.ts`.

## Source map

- [geojson-upload.tsx](../../apps/web/src/components/farmer/geojson-upload.tsx)
- [polygons.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/polygons.ts)
- [scas-stub.ts](../../apps/web/src/lib/scas-stub.ts)
- [admin/actions.ts](../../apps/web/src/app/admin/actions.ts)

## Related docs

- [Web app](../systems/web.md), [Program](../systems/program.md), [ADR 0012](../architecture/decisions/0012-scas-external-provider-stub.md)
```

### Task 20: Review on rendered pages, fixes, optional smoke test

**Skills:** `screen-critique` (on screenshots of rendered pages), `better-interface` (model-run UI review of the code). Do not invoke `interface-review`: it is user-invocable only; mention to the user that they can run it themselves before merging.

**Files:**
- Modify: whatever the reviews flag (components under `apps/web/src/components`, pages under `apps/web/src/app`)
- Optional (stretch): `apps/web/playwright.config.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Capture screenshots**

With the deployed app (or `infisical run --env=dev -- pnpm -C apps/web start` after a build) and at least one pool in each of Open, Funded and Redeeming (run `pnpm demo` twice, interrupting one run after the subscribe scene), capture at 1440px and 390px: `/`, `/pools`, `/pools/<open>`, `/pools/<redeeming>`, `/fields/<address>`, `/farmer` (signed in, with the upload table filled), `/portfolio` (with a position), `/admin`, and the invest panel in its KYC-rejected state. Use the browser tools available in the session (Claude in Chrome: load its skill first) and save the images under the session scratchpad, not the repo.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run screen-critique**

Load `screen-critique` and critique each screenshot against `.tastemaker/style-lock.md`. Record the P1/P2/P3 list in the task notes.
Expected: a prioritized list. Fix every P1 and the P2s that touch the demo path (pool page, invest panel, field page, admin console). Typical checks: gold never used as text on paper, one primary button per view, tabular numbers aligned, no em dashes in UI copy, 44px rows in tables and the timeline, focus ring visible on every control, no horizontal scroll at 390px.

- [ ] **Step 3: Run better-interface on the code**

Load `better-interface` and review `apps/web/src/components` and `apps/web/src/app`. Fix findings on accessibility (labels, roles, focus order, live regions for transaction status), copy (plain words for farmers, "Fixed yield 8.0%" with the term), and interaction states (hover only under `@media (hover:hover)`, pressed, disabled with a reason, loading).

- [ ] **Step 4: Re-run tests and build**

Run: `pnpm -C apps/web exec vitest run && pnpm -C apps/web exec tsc --noEmit && pnpm nx run web:build`
Expected: all green. Re-capture the screenshots that changed and confirm `screen-critique` reports no P1.

```ts
});
```

`apps/web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`:
```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";

for (const path of ["/", "/pools", "/farmer", "/portfolio", "/admin/login"]) {
  test(`${path} renders without horizontal scroll`, async ({ page }) => {
    const res = await page.goto(path);
    expect(res?.status()).toBeLessThan(400);
    const overflow = await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth);
    expect(overflow).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
  });
}

test("the pool list links to a pool page with the Instrument card", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/pools");
  const first = page.locator("article a").first();
  test.skip((await first.count()) === 0, "no pools on devnet");
  await first.click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Fixed yield" })).toBeVisible();
});
```
Run: `E2E_BASE_URL=https://<domain> pnpm -C apps/web exec playwright test`
Expected: all pass (the pool test skips when devnet has no pools).

```bash
git commit -m "fix(web): review fixes from screen-critique and better-interface"
```

## Self-review notes

**Spec coverage** (spec section → task):
- 4.5 Landing and pools list → Tasks 10, 11. Pool page with the Instrument card (Issuer, Farm Projects, SCAS checks, amount, term, fixed yield, Shortfall rules, risks, status, explorer links) → Task 11. Field page (satellite map, attestation list with explorer and evidence links) → Task 12; the NDVI chart is dropped by ADR 0012 (Task 19). Farmer view (email login with embedded wallet, GeoJSON, financing status) → Tasks 9, 14. Investor view (Phantom, mock KYC, subscribe, portfolio, refund, redeem) → Tasks 9, 13. Admin view behind server keys → Tasks 8, 15. USD display → Task 2 formatters, used everywhere.
- 5 Main flow, all seven steps → Task 16 script and Task 15 Step 6 in the browser.
- 6 Error handling: under-subscription refund (Tasks 13, 15), unverified field and missing harvest (program errors explained in Task 5, surfaced in Task 15), signer checks (role mismatch warning in Task 15), GlitchTip (Task 17). Satellite API errors no longer apply (stub).
- 7 Testing: one end-to-end devnet script that prints explorer links and doubles as the video script → Task 16. Program tests are owned by the program plan.
- 8 Schedule → Global Constraints and the effort table.
- Every user-facing state (loading, empty, error with a way out, pending, success with explorer link) → `state.tsx` (Task 3), route `loading.tsx` files (Tasks 11, 12), `TxStatus` (Task 9), `ActionButton` (Task 15). The non-KYC failure is a dedicated message with "Complete KYC" (Tasks 5, 13) and two on-chain scenes in the script (Task 16: missing record, frozen transfer).
- Scope changes from the coordinator are folded in: SCAS stub instead of the worker (Task 6, ADR 0012 in Task 19), no worker deployment (Task 18), Privy as the primary farmer login with adapter fallback (Tasks 9, 14), style-lock consumed not regenerated (Task 3), platform-level KYC (Tasks 5, 7, 8, 13, 15, 16), ADR number 0012, `better-interface` instead of `interface-review` (Task 20).

**Placeholder scan:** no TBD/TODO. Runtime values in shell commands (`<domain>`, `<id>`, the devlog date) are values the executor reads from the previous command's output.

**Type consistency:** checked `TxPhase`, `TxSigner`, `ExplainedError`/`ErrorAction`, `OpsContext` (with `accountExists`), `StubDeps`, `PoolView`/`ProjectWithFields`/`ProducerProject`/`InvestorState`, `ActionResult`, and every builder's argument object across the tasks that use them. `approveInvestor(ctx, wallet)` and `approveInvestorAction(wallet)` take no pool everywhere; `revokeInvestor(ctx, poolId, wallet)` keeps the pool.

**Review Focus:** each of the five lines has its test in the owning task (Tasks 1, 4, 5, 6, 13, 14).

**Known risks for the executor:**
- `@privy-io/react-auth` 3.47.0 option names (`chain`, `embeddedWallets.solana`) may differ from this plan; Task 9 says how to adapt without losing the devnet pin.
- `@sentry/nextjs` 11 is a new major; `sendDefaultPii` and `withSentryConfig` are deliberately not used.
- The Anchor coder's instruction names may come back snake_case or camelCase; tests normalize them.
- The public devnet RPC rate-limits `getProgramAccounts`; the 5-second cache and per-project field queries keep the console under the limit for a demo-sized dataset. If it bites, set `SOLANA_RPC_URL` to a keyed RPC (server only; `NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL` stays public).
- Esri World Imagery tiles are used keyless with attribution; acceptable for a hackathon demo, revisit before any commercial use.
